\section{Consequence-Equilibrium Attention: the shape, and the programme
that builds
it}\label{consequence-equilibrium-attention-the-shape-and-the-programme-that-builds-it}

\textbf{Invented by \href{https://teerthsharma.vercel.app/}{Teerth
Sharma}} · \texttt{https://github.com/teerthsharma/resolvent} ·
teerths57@gmail.com

\emph{Front matter, coordinator, 2026-09-03 (revised after the author's
clarification that the body of this paper is the development plan).
Evidence classes: RUN (executed on this box this session), READ
(\texttt{path:line} at HEAD \texttt{207e7b9}), CITED {[}V{]} (primary
page fetched, title matched; bib key), DERIVED (steps shown). The
assembler keeps them.}

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\subsection{Abstract}\label{abstract}

This paper is a development programme, and it states first what it is a
programme for. Self-attention reads a context as one convex mixture per
query, each query's read independent of every other's --- the right
operator for the next token and, on the one task shape every bed in this
repository asked (a scalar at one position), the operator a single
attention layer is provably Bayes-optimal for (Marion et al.~2025;
Duranthon et al.~2026). Fifteen rounds of this campaign raced softmax on
that ground and could not win, for that reason (\texttt{MISTAKES.md}
D-1). What the author wants is different ground: the \emph{consequence}
of an intervention on a context, the \emph{safest move} under several
constraints at once, and the \emph{next transient state} toward
equilibrium. The shape that carries those three is
Consequence-Equilibrium Attention (CEQ): the causal resolvent read
\(O=(1-\gamma)P(I-\gamma P)^{-1}V\) of Fagnou et al.~(ChaCAL, EMNLP
2024), taken over the three-corner operator \(P\) this repository proved
in Lean, with two things no source in eight prior-art sweeps holds
together --- absorbing boundary rows for \(K\ge 2\) constraint sets and
a goal set, so that \(K+1\) value channels through one triangular solve
read the reach-avoid committors of every position; and an interventional
re-solve, so that the displacement of the jointly determined state under
\(\mathrm{do}(a)\) is the consequence of a candidate move and the safest
move is the one that reaches the goal before any constraint. At
\(\gamma=0\) the read is softmax bitwise (RUN); the record's
path-product corner is the resolvent of the sub-diagonal chain (RUN,
exact); the committor bed's label is the resolvent read with absorbing
rows (RUN, exact); the Neumann tail of a row-stochastic \(P\) equals
\(\gamma^{K+1}/(1-\gamma)\), a certificate with no sum over the context
length (RUN; planted negative fires). No CEQ arm has been trained. The
body of this paper is therefore the plan: a directed graph of small,
priced, pre-registered milestones on the certified RTX 4060, each with
the number that passes it, the number that kills it, the control that
would kill it (ChaCAL at the same \(\gamma\) without boundaries; three
softmax skylines at matched parameters), the failure mechanism it is
designed against, and its deliverable --- ordered
cheapest-decisive-first so the author can develop it one evening at a
time.

\textbf{Keywords:} attention mechanisms · resolvent · absorbing Markov
chains · committor · reach-avoid safety · interventions · research
programme · pre-registration · Lean 4 · persistent homology · negative
results

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\subsection{1. Introduction}\label{introduction}

\subsubsection{1.1 What this document is}\label{what-this-document-is}

It is a plan with its justification attached. Section 2 defines the
shape exactly and lists what is proved, run, cited and open about it.
Section 3 says why per-row softmax cannot occupy the ground the shape is
built for, citing each theorem only as far as its hypotheses reach.
Section 4 compresses fifteen rounds of record into the facts the plan
rests on and the mistakes it is designed against. \textbf{Section 5 is
the programme} --- the reason the document exists --- written as
milestone cards a single developer can pick up in any order the
dependency graph allows. Section 6 is the apparatus the cards refer to:
beds, labels, floors, metrics, controls, binds, certificates, prices.
Section 7 is the occupancy table. Section 8 collects the limits once.

\subsubsection{1.2 The north star, in the author's
words}\label{the-north-star-in-the-authors-words}

The repository's immutable north star
(\texttt{CEQ\_V20\_R15\_CONTRACT.md:42-45}, READ) asks for
\emph{attention equal to self-attention on its own ground, built from
softmax and AdamW, and capable on ground they cannot occupy ---
predicting the next state toward equilibrium, not the next token.} On
2026-09-03 the author sharpened it:

\begin{quote}
the main attention mechanism i want is causality for ai to learn
consequences for it to predict safest move under multiple constraint
something self attention is bad at and any other benefit coming from
next transient state phase equilibrium predictor based attention
\end{quote}

and, the same day, fixed what this document is for:

\begin{quote}
the main goal of this research paper was to make the whole future plan
so i can slowly develop it
\end{quote}

Three capabilities are named, and every milestone in Section 5 serves
one of them:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Consequences.} Given a context and an intervention on it --- a
  token rewritten, a move taken --- what happens to everything else: the
  displacement of the whole configuration, not the next symbol.
\item
  \textbf{The safest move under several constraints at once.} Given
  candidate moves, which one keeps every constraint, and by how much.
\item
  \textbf{The next transient state, the phase, the equilibrium.} Where
  the context is going: which basin, whether it is at a decision point,
  which transition.
\end{enumerate}

\subsubsection{1.3 The thesis in one
paragraph}\label{the-thesis-in-one-paragraph}

Take the record's three-corner operator \(W_{\beta,qk,g}\)
(\texttt{ceq/arm\_smprime.py}, \texttt{lean/CEQ/V16Domain.lean}, READ):
softmax at \(\beta=1\), linear attention at \(\beta=0\), the exact path
product at \(\beta=0\) with the content term off, machine-checked and
measured distinct. Solve one lower-triangular system,
\(z=(I-\gamma W)^{-1}V\), and read \(O=(1-\gamma)Wz\). At \(\gamma=0\)
this is softmax bitwise. At \(\gamma\uparrow 1\) over a row-stochastic
\(W\) with some rows made absorbing --- a goal set and \(K\) constraint
sets, the BOS position declared inside one of them because it is
absorbing by construction --- it is the fundamental matrix of an
absorbing chain: with the value channel \(V=\mathbf 1_{\mathcal A_k}\)
the read at a transient position is \(\mathbb E[\gamma^{\tau_k-1}]\),
the hitting-time transform of set \(k\), whose limit is the committor;
the \(K+1\) reads sum to one. Over the record's strictly causal chain at
\(\gamma=1\) it is the path product the record already banked: corner 3
was a resolvent all along. A candidate move is an intervention on the
context whose consequence is the difference of two solves --- one extra
solve, rank-one when a single row moves. The parameter \(\gamma\) is a
horizon dial from the next token to the equilibrium. That is the shape.
Its operator is ChaCAL's; its base is the record's; its boundary rows,
its interventional channel, its decision rule, its certificates and its
proofs are what the sweeps report as held by no one together.

\subsubsection{1.4 What self-attention is bad at, as theorems rather
than a
slogan}\label{what-self-attention-is-bad-at-as-theorems-rather-than-a-slogan}

Each statement names exactly what its source licenses; two of the four
fail at this repository's own geometry and are cited only for the beds
that satisfy them.

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Per-row independence} (definition). One softmax step computes
  \(O_i=\sum_{j\le i}P_{ij}V_j\); the read at \(i\) never constrains the
  read at \(j\). A jointly determined configuration is not what a
  per-row mixture computes in one read.
\item
  \textbf{Hops need depth.} \(k\) content-dependent hops need depth
  \(\lfloor\log_2 k\rfloor+2\) (Sanford--Hsu--Telgarsky 2024, Thm 4.2,
  \citep{sanford-2024-logdepth}); the lower bound is conditional on the
  1-vs-2-cycle conjecture (Cor. 4.3). Connectivity needs logarithmic
  depth (\citep{sanford-2024-graph-algorithms}). ChaCAL reaches the same
  \(\lceil\log_2(n+1)\rceil\) bound independently. Depth is not
  necessary at linear width (\citep{yehudai-2025-depthwidth}), so every
  comparison matches width too.
\item
  \textbf{Composition in one layer.} A single attention layer cannot
  compose two functions on a large domain
  (\citep{peng-2024-transformer-limitations}, Thm 1, unconditional) ---
  but its inequality is not satisfied at \(s=64\), \(d=16\)
  (\(384<544\)), so it licenses nothing at the record's geometry.
\item
  \textbf{Optimality on the wrong ground.} One attention layer attains
  Bayes risk on single-location regression
  (\citep{marion-2025-single-location}, asymptotic, \texttt{erf} gate;
  \citep{duranthon-2026-softmax-advantage} for softmax proper). Every
  bed the campaign ran was that task
  (\texttt{scale/negation\_scope.py:286-304}, READ).
\end{itemize}

Two facts bound what the plan may ever claim. The exact resolvent is a
determinant-class computation (Cook 1985;
\(\mathrm{NL}\subseteq\mathrm{DET}\subseteq
\mathrm{NC}^2\)), at or above the class where log-precision
constant-depth transformers sit (\citep{merrill-2023-parallelism}): CEQ
does not evade the depth law, it relocates the log-depth into one linear
solve, and ``depth one'' here means one parameter set. And a looped or
deeper softmax stack computes the same resolvent by iteration
(\citep{wang-2024-incontext-td}) with truncation error exactly
\(\gamma^{K+1}/(1-\gamma)\) after \(K\) loops (Prop. 2.x), so every
claim is made at matched depth, width and parameters, and ``beats
softmax'' is not a sentence this paper writes.

\subsubsection{1.5 How the programme is
organised}\label{how-the-programme-is-organised}

Section 5's milestone cards each carry: prerequisites and the cards they
can run beside (the record's dependency law D-1); what to build, as a
specification; what to prove; what to measure, with the bed, the seed
count and the statistic; the PASS number; the KILL number and what dies
with it; the price in GPU-minutes on the certified RTX 4060, labelled
fitted, assumed, or run; the \texttt{MISTAKES.md} mechanism it is
designed against; the deliverable; and an estimate in evenings. Phases
end in a gate the author decides alone with the numbers in hand. The
five cheapest decisive cards are marked, and a first-evening list opens
the section. Nothing in the plan launches on Kaggle without the author's
explicit yes.

\subsubsection{1.6 What this document is
not}\label{what-this-document-is-not}

It is not a result: no CEQ arm has been trained, and the record's last
deciding measurement crossed a one-hop threshold on five seeds of eight,
straddled it on the interval, and that threshold was later shown not to
be an information floor. It is not a novelty claim for the resolvent,
the committor, the successor representation, the triangular solve, the
attention sink or the discount factor --- each has an owner, cited
before it is named. Every number in it names what it was compared
against and which file or command produced it; struck constants of the
record do not appear; sources not reached at the primary text are marked
\texttt{{[}U{]}} and used as pointers, never as theorems.

\subsubsection{1.7 Contributions}\label{contributions}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  The shape, exactly defined, with its containment of softmax and of the
  record's path-product corner machine-checked and run (§2).
\item
  Boundary rows as the capability mechanism, with the sum-to-one
  identity that forces the goal set and the BOS convention to exist, and
  the honest statement that absorption redirects mass and never
  subtracts (§2).
\item
  The interventional channel with the order-of-operations proposition in
  both halves (§2).
\item
  An exact certificate with no sum over the context length, resolving
  the record's open impossibility (§2).
\item
  The programme: a dependency graph of small, priced, pre-registered
  milestones with predictions and counters, three fellow approximators
  in every table, the cheapest killer beside every claim, and the
  evenings count derived from the critical path (§5).
\item
  The apparatus the programme measures with, designed against sixty-six
  named failure mechanisms (§6), and eight corrections to the record
  found while writing (§4, §7).
\end{enumerate}

\subsection{2.0 Occupancy --- cited before the shape is
named}\label{occupancy-cited-before-the-shape-is-named}

The read operator on a causal row-stochastic softmax matrix, solved as a
triangular system and reverting to standard attention at \(\gamma = 0\),
is \textbf{ChaCAL} (\citep{fagnou-2024-chacal}, EMNLP 2024, Eq. 5, Eq.
7, Thm 1; CITED {[}V{]}; \texttt{0} files in the tree cite it --- a
V-7-on-search finding for §3). One detail decides two controls below:
ChaCAL removes the diagonal inside the inverse
(\texttt{refute\_falsify\_math.md} §3.15, \texttt{{[}V-fetched{]}}), so
its resolvent is a finite regime-N sum and its read is
\textbf{sub-stochastic} --- \texttt{RUN{[}J{]}} row sums
\texttt{0.400\ …\ 0.765} against \texttt{1.000} diagonal-kept. Its
blockwise evaluation is \citep{zhao-2026-structuredsparse} {[}V{]}. The
discounted Neumann closure of a content-adaptive attention with
learnable per-head \(\gamma\), read as the fundamental matrix of an
absorbing chain with one leak state, is \citep{roffo-2026-infsa} {[}V{]}
(ReLU Frobenius base, non-causal). Multi-hop PageRank on attention:
\citep{wang-2021-magna}, \citep{feng-2022-diffuser},
\citep{yuan-2025-paraformer} (lineage \citep{chien-2020-gprgnn}); the
implicit resolvent step of an attention flow
\citep{chamberlain-2021-grand}; the exact resolvent layer on a fixed
graph \citep{gasteiger-2019-appnp}; \((I-\gamma P)^{-1}\) as policy
evaluation, successor representation, Katz/PageRank index
\citep{bellman-1957-markovian}, \citep{dayan-1993-successor},
\citep{katz-1953-status}, \citep{brin-1998-anatomy}. The three corners:
\citep{vaswani-2017-attention} (\(\beta=1\)),
\citep{katharopoulos-2020-linearattention} (\(\beta=0\), \(g\equiv0\)),
\citep{dao-2024-ssd} Def. 3.1 (the 1-semiseparable mask); the chunked
triangular solve \citep{yang-2024-deltanet} Eq. 10; no finite-state dual
for row-softmax \citep{hu-2025-ssdtheory} {[}V{]}.

Boundary rows: \(N=(I-Q)^{-1}\), \(B=NR\) ---
\citep{kemeny-1960-finitemarkov} {[}V-cat{]} (the bib carries the same
book as \citep{kemeny-1976-finite} and \citep{kemeny-1976-finitemarkov};
the assembler collapses the three) and
\citep{grinstead-1997-probability} Thm 11.6 {[}U{]}; clamped labelled
nodes as absorbing boundary with a harmonic read \emph{as a learning
layer} --- \citep{zhu-2003-harmonic} {[}V{]},
\citep{zhou-2003-consistency}, \citep{wu-2012-partially-absorbing},
\citep{begga-2023-diffusion-jump}, \citep{azad-2022-harmonic-extension};
the discrete Dirichlet committor ---
\citep{metzner-2009-tpt-markov-jump}, \citep{e-2006-transition-paths},
\citep{e-2010-tptreview}, \citep{doyle-1984-electric}; one absorbing row
inside attention (\texttt{do()} with the token fixed and forbidden from
attending) --- \citep{karbalayghareh-2026-doformer} {[}V{]}; the
attention \emph{sink}, a column device distinct from a row condition ---
\citep{xiao-2023-attentionsinks}, \citep{gu-2024-sinkemerges},
\citep{ranmilo-2026-attentionsinks}. Decision: reach-avoid probability
--- \citep{summers-2010-reach-avoid}, \citep{abate-2008-reachability},
\citep{kariotoglou-2014-lp-reach-avoid}; several target sets in one
linear system with a verdict --- \citep{baier-2008-modelchecking};
Chebyshev \citep{vanmoffaert-2013-chebyshev}, lexicographic
\citep{yang-2026-lexisafe}, max--min \citep{park-2026-maxmin}, CMDP
\citep{altman-1999-cmdp}, argmin over evaluated values
\citep{bellman-1957-markovian}, a move from a linear first-exit solve
\citep{todorov-2006-lmdp} / \citep{todorov-2009-efficient}, a
transformer choosing the lowest predicted violation probability
\citep{jeddi-2021-lyapunovsafe} (NEAR-MISS), safety filter
\citep{hsu-2023-safetyfilter} / \citep{borquez-2023-lrf}, the discount
that buys a contraction \citep{fisac-2019-bridging} /
\citep{hsu-2021-reachavoidrl}. Intervention: row surgery on a linear SCM
\citep{pearl-2009-causality}, \citep{shimizu-2006-lingam}; consequence
as equilibrium displacement \citep{mooij-2013-ode2scm},
\citep{bongers-2021-cyclic}, first-order
\citep{bottou-2013-counterfactual} §7.3; rank-one re-solve
\citep{sherman-1950-inverse-adjustment}, \citep{hager-1989-updating};
fundamental-matrix perturbation \citep{schweitzer-1968-perturbation};
barrier re-solve on a fixed chain \citep{piray-2021-linearrl} Eq. 5; the
SCM as the fixed point of a causally masked transformer with
\texttt{do()} by clamp-and-re-solve \citep{scetbon-2024-fip} {[}V{]};
order of intervention and equilibration \citep{dash-2005-emc};
oracle-scored effect with a no-change floor
\citep{vakalis-2026-interventiongap}, \citep{lin-2026-scratchworld}.
Neumann tail: textbook (\citep{meyer-2000-matrix},
\citep{horn-2013-matrix}). Segmentation mechanism:
\citep{lin-2025-forgetting-transformer}, \citep{yang-2023-gla},
\citep{hwang-2025-hnet}; converse \citep{yang-2026-boundary-repair} Thm
1. Topology: thresholded attention barcodes
\citep{kushnareva-2021-tda-attention}, \citep{kushnareva-2022-betti};
intervention effects on persistence \citep{kim-2026-topological-causal};
directed stability \citep{turner-2019-quasimetric-rips},
\citep{chowdhury-2017-path-homology}; Mapper \citep{singh-2007-mapper}
(records {[}V{]}, landing {[}U{]}),
\citep{carriere-2018-mapper-statistics}; cluster covers
\citep{cho-2022-sbm-attention}, \citep{roy-2021-routing-transformer},
\citep{kitaev-2020-reformer}, \citep{yuan-2025-nsa}; the
persistence-derived CSR schedule in an attention kernel --- the author's
own \citep{sharma-2026-kernels-22} {[}V{]}.

\textbf{The delta, stated narrowly.} No fetched source combines the
following in one attention operator, and each part is owned above (eight
sweeps, queries recorded; absence is bounded by those queries, V-7): (e)
\(K\ge2\) absorbing constraint sets, a goal set and a declared sink as
identity rows of a \emph{content-dependent, causal, row-stochastic}
read; (g) the interventional re-solve with the displacement as a jointly
scored vector label at two stated prices; (h) the committor vector and
the safest-move rules over candidate moves placed in the context; (i) a
printed certificate with a planted negative and the \(1/(1-\gamma)\)
mask amplification; (j) a learnable discount in a causal LM read pinned
by a boundary-corrected LR test; (k) machine-checked containment and the
zero-gate iff; (l) restricted to the F0 endpoint; (m) the
cover-to-schedule path. The name is the repository's,
\textbf{Consequence--Equilibrium Attention (CEQ)}; its operator is
ChaCAL's read with the diagonal kept in the inverse, on the record's
three-corner base.

\subsection{2.1 Definitions}\label{definitions}

\textbf{Definition 1 (base family).} For \(s\) positions with logits
\(qk_{ij}=q_i\cdot k_j\), magnitudes \(m_k\in[0,1]\), phases
\(\theta_k\), and a switch \(\beta\in\{0,1\}\),
\[W_{\beta,qk,m,\theta}[i,j]=\mathbb 1[j\le i]\;\mathrm{pathProd}(m,\theta)_{ij}\;e^{qk_{ij}}/Z_i^{\beta},\qquad
\mathrm{pathProd}(m,\theta)_{ij}=\prod_{k=j+1}^{i} m_k e^{i\theta_k},\]
\texttt{READ\ lean/CEQ/V16Domain.lean:92-97},
\texttt{ceq/arm\_smprime.py:144}. The exponential prefix-scan form of
\texttt{V16Domain.lean:366-378} (\texttt{Hop}) is its restriction to
\(m>0\) (\texttt{prefix\_logit\_mask\_restated} clause 5, \texttt{:221})
and carries no zero
(\texttt{no\_prefix\_scan\_represents\_a\_zero\_gate}, \texttt{:165}).
Corners: \(\beta=1,m\equiv1\) softmax; \(\beta=0,m\equiv1\) linear
attention; \(\beta=0\), QK off, the path product
(\texttt{three\_corners\_containment} \texttt{:433},
\texttt{corners\_are\_distinct} \texttt{:445}). Ruling: the base carries
the multiplicative gate (\texttt{refute\_theory\_math} item 1,
\texttt{refute\_theory\_occvac} §1.2 row 9) so that Proposition 6 has a
subject. Mechanism: V-25, P-7.

\textbf{Definition 2 (two regimes, one solve).} Regime S: \(\beta=1\),
\(W=P\) row-stochastic with \(P_{ii}>0\) for \(i\ge1\) and \(P_{00}=1\);
\(\gamma\in[0,1)\); \((I-\gamma P)^{-1}=\sum_{t\ge0}(\gamma P)^t\) by
\(\|\gamma P\|_\infty=\gamma<1\). Regime N: \(W=A\) strictly causal
(\(A_{ij}=0\) for \(j\ge i\)), any \(\gamma\), \(A^s=0\)
(\texttt{Nilpotent.pow\_card\_eq\_zero} \texttt{:77},
\texttt{occupancy\_is\_exact\_inverse} \texttt{:96}). The boundary is a
theorem (\texttt{one\_not\_nilpotent} \texttt{:105}), and an absorbing
row is not \texttt{StrictlyLower}: regime N admits no absorbing row, and
at \(\gamma=1\) one makes \(I-A'\) singular (\texttt{RUN{[}M{]}}
\(\det(I-A')=0.0\)). The north star lives in regime S, corner 3 in
regime N; they do not meet on a boundary row. Mechanism: V-25, C8.

\textbf{Definition 3 (boundary rows).} Disjoint position sets: the sink
\(\mathcal A_{\rm sink}\ni0\), the goal \(\mathcal A_0\), the
constraints \(\mathcal A_1,\dots,\mathcal A_K\);
\(\mathcal A=\bigsqcup\mathcal A_\bullet\),
\(T=[s]\setminus\mathcal A\), the query position in \(T\).
\(P_{a,\cdot}=e_a\) for \(a\in\mathcal A\); canonical form
\(P=\begin{pmatrix}Q&R\\0&I\end{pmatrix}\), \(Q=P_{TT}\),
\(R_k=P_{T\mathcal A_k}\). Two construction facts
(\texttt{THESIS\_CORRECTIONS\_2.md} §1, re-run \texttt{RUN{[}J{]}}):
\textbf{F1} row 0 of any causal softmax at \(\beta=1\) is \(e_0\), so
position 0 is absorbing whether or not declared; undeclared,
\(\det(I-Q)=0.0\). \textbf{F2} walks descend, so a boundary set after
the query has committor exactly \(0.0\); every set precedes the query.
Ruling on BOS (\texttt{refute\_theory\_math} C8(b),
\texttt{refute\_falsify\_math} §3.11 against \texttt{design\_theory}
§1.3): BOS is its \textbf{own sink set with value \(0\) on every
indicator channel}, not a goal member --- otherwise ``reach the goal''
collapses to ``descend to 0 without a constraint'' and the two rules of
Definition 7 become one by construction (V-3, C4); the sink share
(``fell off the prompt'') is printed beside every label. The value-zero
column sink of \texttt{V15Fork.Asink} (\texttt{V15Fork.lean:67-70}) and
the sink row are two distinct conditions that coexist on any causal
softmax (\texttt{sweep\_resolvent.md} §2.14; P-7). Mechanism: V-25, V-8,
V-12, D-3.

\textbf{Definition 4 (state, mixing matrix, read).}
\[z(\gamma)=(I-\gamma W)^{-1}V,\qquad \Pi_\gamma=(1-\gamma)W(I-\gamma W)^{-1},\qquad O(\gamma)=\Pi_\gamma V.\]
The read carries the \((1-\gamma)\) factor. For any row-stochastic \(W\)
--- boundary rows or not --- the bare \(W(I-\gamma W)^{-1}\) has every
row sum \(1/(1-\gamma)\) (\texttt{RUN{[}J{]}}, \texttt{RUN{[}M{]}}:
\(2.5\) at \(\gamma=0.6\) with and without the sets); the factor is a
property of the class, not of the boundary rows. Mechanism: V-17, V-23.

\textbf{Definition 5 (horizon dial).} \(\gamma\in[0,1)\) is one
learnable scalar per head: the discount of
\citep{bellman-1957-markovian}, learnable as in
\citep{roffo-2026-infsa}, the \(\gamma\uparrow1\) limit of
\citep{kemeny-1960-finitemarkov}. At \(\gamma=1\) with any absorbing row
\(I-P\) is singular (\texttt{RUN{[}M{]}}); the endpoint is a limit
(Proposition 9).

\textbf{Definition 6 (interventional channel, two objects).} A candidate
move \(a\) is an intervention. (i) On the \emph{oracle's} latent chain:
a row clamp \(P'_{v_a,\cdot}\leftarrow e_{u_a}\), rank one,
\(u^\top\mathbb 1=0\). (ii) On the \emph{arm's} operator: a token
rewrite at position \(i\), which changes \(q_i\) and \(k_i\), hence
every softmax row \(j\ge i\) through \(qk_{ji}\) and \(Z_j\) ---
\texttt{RUN{[}J{]}} at \(i=12\), \(s=32\):
\(\mathrm{rank}(\Delta P)=20=s-i\). Two objects, two prices (Proposition
7); \texttt{design\_theory} §1.6 (``supported on the intervened rows'')
is KILLED for (ii), kept for (i). Mechanism: M-8, P-8.

\textbf{Definition 7 (labels and rules).} Per move, the reach-avoid
vector \((q^{({\rm sink})},q^{(0)},q^{(1)},\dots,q^{(K)})(a)\) with
\(\sum=1\) on \(T\) (Proposition 8). Two rules as columns of one bed
(V-1): \(a^\star=\arg\max_a q^{(0)}(\mathrm{do}\,a)\) (reach the goal
before any constraint) and the Chebyshev
\(a^\dagger=\arg\min_a\max_{k\ge1}q^{(k)}(\mathrm{do}\,a)\);
lexicographic as a third column; the disagreement fraction printed. Two
heads: (H-q) the committor vector by the \textbf{exact triangular solve
at \(\gamma=1\) on the transient block},
\(\hat q=(I-\hat Q)^{-1}\hat R_k\mathbb 1\); (H-z) the
state/displacement channel at the trained \(\hat\gamma\). Ruling
(\texttt{refute\_instrument\_occvac} FATAL-2,
\texttt{refute\_falsify\_math} item 16): an undiscounted label and a
discounted read at \(\hat\gamma<1\) pull \(\gamma\) in opposite
directions --- \(q\) to \(1\%\) at \(\tau=8\) needs
\(\hat\gamma\ge0.99857\), \(1/(1-\hat\gamma)\approx697\)
(\texttt{RUN{[}F{]}}) --- so the committor head does not carry
\(\gamma\); the discounted read \(E_i[\gamma^{\tau-1}\mathbb 1_k]\) is a
separate registered label at a bed constant \(\gamma_{\rm env}\)
(\citep{fisac-2019-bridging}) for the horizon-dial bet only. Mechanism:
V-17, D-2, D-3, M-20.

\subsection{2.2 Propositions}\label{propositions}

\textbf{Proposition 1 (parity at \(\gamma=0\), with a rejection
region).} For every causal \(W\) and \(V\), \(O(0)=WV\); in IEEE-754
forward substitution at \(\gamma=0\), \(z=V\) bitwise and the read is
the same matmul as attention. \emph{Proof.} \((I-0\cdot W)=I\);
\(0\cdot x=0\), \(v-0=v\), \(v/1=v\) exact for finite entries.
\(\square\) Carrier \texttt{V16Domain.corner\_softmax} \texttt{:394};
target \texttt{gamma\_zero\_is\_softmax} {[}M{]}. \emph{Evidence.}
\texttt{RUN{[}coord{]}} \texttt{torch.equal(O(0),PV)=True} at
\(s=64,d=16\); rejection region
\(\max|O(0.5)-O(0)|=2.3002850040264393\); the record's own softmax
corner is \(1.110223\times10^{-16}\) off \texttt{ceq/lm.py} on \(19/64\)
entries (\texttt{READ\ V16\_ARM\_SMPRIME.md:266-293}), so ``bitwise'' is
against the lane's own routine. \emph{Battery}
(\texttt{refute\_theory\_occvac} §2.1): the plant ``non-causal \(W\)''
is deleted (it passes at \(\gamma=0\), \texttt{RUN{[}M{]}}
\texttt{array\_equal\ True}); kept: \(\gamma\ne0\) and \(\beta=0\) at
the softmax corner (\(\max|{\rm gap}|>0.5\),
\texttt{READ\ V16\_ARM\_SMPRIME.md:336-339}); declared-empty row:
``ChaCAL at the same \(\gamma\)'' --- a reproduction of ChaCAL's Eq. 5
at \(\gamma=0\), not a contribution. Mechanism: V-24, V-3. Status:
\textbf{RUN + LEAN (corner)}.

\textbf{Proposition 2 (corner 3 is a resolvent, regime N).} For \(A\)
strictly lower bidiagonal with \(A_{i,i-1}=a_i\):
\([(I-A)^{-1}]_{ij}=\prod_{k=j+1}^{i}a_k\) for \(j\le i\), \(0\) above.
\emph{Proof.} One walk per \((i,j)\) of length \(i-j\); \(A^s=0\);
\(\sum_{m<s}A^m=(I-A)^{-1}\) (\texttt{occupancy\_is\_exact\_inverse}),
entry \((i,j)\) receiving the \(m=i-j\) term. \(\square\) Consequence:
BED-M's label \(y_{s-1}=((I-A)^{-1}b)_{s-1}\)
(\texttt{READ\ scale/negation\_scope.py:286-304}) is \(z(1)_{s-1}\), so
any arm containing corner 3 reproduces BED-M's label as its own forward
--- BED-M is contained, not won. \emph{Evidence.}
\texttt{RUN{[}coord{]}} \(\max|G-(I-A)^{-1}|=0.0\), last row vs
\texttt{equilibrium\_oracle} \(6.217248937900877\times10^{-15}\),
\(A^{64}=0\) exactly. Mechanism: D-2. Status: \textbf{LEAN} (two halves)
+ target \texttt{pathprod\_is\_chain\_resolvent} {[}S{]}.

\textbf{Proposition 3 (the committor is the resolvent read with
absorbing rows --- classical).} \(P\) row-stochastic including its
absorbing rows, \(\rho(Q)<1\), \(V=\mathbb 1_{\mathcal A_k}\); for
\(i\in T\),
\[(1-\gamma)z(\gamma)_i=E_i[\gamma^{\tau_k}\mathbb 1_k],\qquad O(\gamma)_i=E_i[\gamma^{\tau_k-1}\mathbb 1_k],\qquad
\lim_{\gamma\uparrow1}O(\gamma)_i=q^{(k)}_i=[(I-Q)^{-1}R_k\mathbb 1]_i,\]
and, when the declared sets exhaust the absorbing states,
\(\sum_\bullet q^{(\bullet)}=\mathbb 1\) on \(T\); at
\(i\in\mathcal A_k\) the read is \(1\), not \(\gamma^{-1}\).
\emph{Proof.} Exchange sums in \(z_i=\sum_t\gamma^tP_i(\tau_k\le t,k)\);
first-step analysis; monotone convergence;
\(\sum_kR_k\mathbb 1=\mathbb 1-Q\mathbb 1\). \(\square\) Every clause is
Kemeny--Snell; the \emph{read} form on an attention \(P\) is the
composition. Discounted reads sum below one: \texttt{RUN{[}I{]}}
state-form \([0.136,0.340]\), read-form \([0.227,0.567]\) at
\(\gamma=0.6\), ratio exactly \(\gamma\); the delay share printed beside
every safest-move reading is \(1-\sum_kE[\gamma^{\tau-1}\mathbb 1_k]\).
\emph{Evidence.} \texttt{RUN{[}J{]}}/\texttt{RUN{[}M{]}}: read vs
\(E[\gamma^{\tau-1}]\) to \(8.33\times10^{-17}\); BED-1's real sets
\(A=[0],B=[1],|T|=9\): resolvent read vs \texttt{bed{[}"q"{]}} \(0.0\),
residual \(1.04\times10^{-17}\), \(\rho(Q)=0.9408612510154677\);
Kirchhoff \(<10^{-10}\) (\texttt{READ\ MATHEMATICS.md:542-600}).
\emph{Ruling on the \(3.8\times10^{-7}\) line.}
\texttt{refute\_theory\_math} item 8 derived a universal gap
\(\ge1-\gamma\); the derivation dropped the indicator ---
\(q_i-E_i[\gamma^{\tau}\mathbb 1_k]=E_i[(1-\gamma^\tau)\mathbb 1_k]\ge(1-\gamma)q_i\),
small where \(q_i\) is. \texttt{RUN{[}J{]}} at \(\gamma=1-10^{-6}\) on
channel \(\{15\}\): \(\max_T=1.42\times10^{-7}\). The line stands with
\((1-\gamma)q_i\) printed beside it. \emph{Read-side plant} (ADD):
declare \(\mathcal A_k\) on the wrong set \(\Rightarrow q\) moves by
\(O(1)\). Mechanism: D-2, V-12, V-25. Status: \textbf{CLASSICAL, RUN on
the arm's \(P\)}; targets \texttt{committor\_is\_resolvent\_read} (a)
{[}M{]}, (b-causal) {[}M{]} via Proposition 10, (b-chain) {[}S{]},
\texttt{hitting\_time\_transform} {[}D{]}.

\textbf{Proposition 4 (Neumann tail: attained on the class, and what a
mask does).} (i) \(P\ge0\) row-stochastic (identity rows included),
\(\gamma\in[0,1)\), \(K\ge0\):
\[\Big\|(I-\gamma P)^{-1}-\sum_{k\le K}(\gamma P)^k\Big\|_\infty=\frac{\gamma^{K+1}}{1-\gamma}\quad(\text{equality});\]
for \(\|P\|_\infty\le1\) the same quantity is \(\le\) the right side;
for \(\|P\|_\infty>1\) the bound \emph{can} fail (a nilpotent \(A\) with
\(\|A\|_\infty=2\) satisfies it at \(K=s-1\), \texttt{RUN{[}M{]}}
\(1.98\times10^{-10}\) vs \(3.68\times10^{-5}\)). \emph{Proof.} The tail
is entrywise non-negative with row sums \(\sum_{k>K}\gamma^k\); the
\(\infty\)-norm of a non-negative matrix is its largest row sum
(\citep{meyer-2000-matrix}, \citep{horn-2013-matrix}). \(\square\) On
the vector: \(\le\gamma^{K+1}\|V\|_\infty/(1-\gamma)\), never the bare
\(\delta\) (V-17). \texttt{err\ =\ bound} is a declared V-3 identity;
the bind is the planted non-stochastic \(P\) --- quote the convergent
plant (rows \(1.5\), \(\gamma=0.6\), \(K=2\): \(7.29\) vs \(0.54\),
\texttt{RUN{[}M{]}}); the coordinator's \(\gamma=0.7\) plant (\(119.37\)
vs \(1.143\)) is a divergent series (\(\gamma\cdot1.5=1.05\)) and is
labelled so. (ii) \textbf{Mask amplification} (ADD,
\texttt{refute\_instrument\_math} row 8): for a sparsified \(P_m\) with
dropped row mass \(\varepsilon=\|P-P_m\|_\infty\),
\(\|O_{\rm full}-O_{\rm mask}\|_\infty\le\varepsilon\|V\|_\infty/(1-\gamma)\),
attained up to a constant: \texttt{RUN{[}J{]}} \(s=3\), \(\gamma=0.9\),
\(\varepsilon=0.1\): \(0.5263157894736843\) against the naive \(0.1\).
Every F1 union bound carries \(1/(1-\gamma)\); the exact solve on an F1
mask is refused (L-CERT). \emph{Census.} \(\mathrm{rowsum}(\hat P)\) on
every trained cell; at \(\hat\beta\ne1\) rows sum \(1.31\dots10.29\)
(\texttt{READ\ V16\_ARM\_SMPRIME.md:28-32}) and no certificate is
printed (V-25). The \texttt{≈7.6e-05} vector bound of
\texttt{sec\_cost.md} §4.x.3 carries
\texttt{{[}ASSUMED\ ‖V‖\_∞\ ≈\ 5{]}}. Mechanism: V-3, V-10, V-17, V-24,
L-CERT. Status: \textbf{DERIVED (textbook) + RUN}; targets
\texttt{neumann\_truncation\_bound} {[}S{]},
\texttt{neumann\_tail\_attained} {[}S{]}, \texttt{mask\_amplification}
{[}S{]}.

\textbf{Proposition 5 (one triangular solve; \(\Pi_\gamma\) is a
mixture; the support does not move).} (a) For causal \(W\),
\(I-\gamma W\) is lower-triangular with diagonal
\(1-\gamma W_{ii}\in[1-\gamma,1)\) in regime S (row 0 and every
absorbing row attain \(1-\gamma\); \texttt{RUN{[}M{]}} min \(0.4\) at
\(\gamma=0.6\)) and \(=1\) in regime N; \(z\) is a forward substitution
costing \(+s^2d/2\) MACs and depth \(s\) \textbf{over} the \(s^2d\)
causal MACs of the softmax head it contains at \(\gamma=0\) (\(+50\%\);
\(+25\%\) if \(QK^\top\) is counted dense) --- an increment, never a
stand-alone figure (\texttt{sec\_cost.md} §4.x.1, M-8). (b) In regime S,
\(\Pi_\gamma=(1-\gamma)\sum_t\gamma^tP^{t+1}\) is row-stochastic and
non-negative, with or without absorbing rows (\texttt{RUN{[}F{]}} row
sums \(1.000000000000000\)), so \(O_i\in\mathrm{hull}\{V_j:j\le i\}\)
for every \(\gamma\): the record's Q2/W3 hull bound applies verbatim,
and labels outside the hull need the value rescale of
\texttt{V15Fork.Asink\_computes\_chain} or regime N; committors live in
\([0,1]\). (c) On a dense causal softmax,
\(\mathrm{supp}\,\Pi_\gamma=\mathrm{supp}\,P\) with or without boundary
rows (\texttt{RUN{[}J{]}} \texttt{True}; lower-triangle minimum on
transient rows \(2.1\times10^{-3}>0\)): the resolvent adds \textbf{no
support} in regime S; absorbing rows change \emph{weights} downstream
(mass redirected to \(a\)); the ``transitive closure'' sentence has
content only under F0 zeros. The claim ``identity rows change the
support'' (\texttt{design\_falsify} §3.1) is KILLED
(\texttt{refute\_falsify\_math} item 13,
\texttt{refute\_instrument\_math} row 5). \emph{Evidence.}
\texttt{RUN{[}coord{]}} \texttt{solve\_triangular} vs dense inverse
\(1.7763568394002505\times10^{-15}\); \texttt{RUN} (NEPTUNE, certified
RTX 4060, float32, \(n=2048,s=64,d=16\); producer owed to
\texttt{scripts/k\_cert.py}): solve\(+Pz\) fwd+bwd \(2.514\) ms vs
\(PV\) \(1.473\) ms vs one Neumann hop \(3.001\) ms --- truncation never
wins in MACs. Kernel boundary: no finite-state dual for softmax \(P\)
(\citep{hu-2025-ssdtheory}); the exact path is the full \(s\times s\)
solve or Zhao et al.'s \(\tilde O(n^{4/3}d)\). Mechanism: M-8, M-3,
V-17, P-7. Status: \textbf{DERIVED + RUN}; targets
\texttt{lower\_triangular\_isUnit}, \texttt{diag\_one\_sub\_smul\_pos}
{[}M{]}, \texttt{resolvent\_is\_triangular\_solve},
\texttt{mixing\_matrix\_rowStochastic} {[}S{]}.

\textbf{Proposition 6 (segmentation is exact, and a zero gate is a
boundary condition).} (i) In the Definition-1 base, \(m_c=0\) gives
\(W_{ij}=0\) for all \(j<c\le i\) (\texttt{pathProd\_eq\_zero\_iff}
\texttt{:129}, no hypothesis); then \(I-\gamma W\) is block
lower-triangular with a zero sub-block and inverts blockwise; an
identity row respects every cut, so absorbing rows are consistent with
the block structure (\texttt{refute\_instrument\_math} row 15). In
regime S an exact zero is a \(-\infty\) logit --- F0 by mask, not by
gate --- and the iff has no producing theorem on the softmax corner
(\texttt{no\_prefix\_scan\_represents\_a\_zero\_gate});
\texttt{segmentation\_blockdiag} {[}M{]} is stated for
\texttt{StrictlyLower} and \texttt{resolvent\_fromBlocks} {[}M{]} is the
half that transfers. (ii) \textbf{ADD} (\texttt{refute\_theory\_math}
item 11, \texttt{RUN{[}J{]}}): after a cut at \(c\) the row \(c\) has
window \(\{c\}\) and renormalises to \(P_{cc}=1.0\) --- a new undeclared
absorbing state; with the sets of Definition 3 and
\(c\notin\mathcal A\), \(\rho(Q)=1.0\) and the committor solve is
singular; declaring \(c\) absorbing severs every boundary set before the
cut from every \(i\ge c\) (\(q=0.0\), \texttt{RUN{[}M{]}}); segmentation
and the reach-avoid read coexist only if every segment carries its own
sink, goal and constraint sets, with the cut position a registered dial.
(iii) The dividend is stated in pair-count units,
\(D=s(s+1)/\sum_mL_m(L_m+1)\): BED-M reads \(31.04\times\) from the
live-pair fraction \(0.0322\)
(\texttt{READ\ V16\_ARM\_SMPRIME.md:518-522}; the source line's pair
counts disagree by \(2\times\), flagged); \((C5)\)'s \(s^2/\sum L_m^2\)
would read \(58.5\) (V-17); a corpus property, \(1\times\) on the
softmax corner. Mechanism: L-CERT, V-25, D-3, V-22, V-24 (the
\(10^{-300}\) plant reads F0 by underflow after \(\ge80\) gates at
\(0.5\); the safe plant is a \(-30\) logit,
\(e^{-30}=9.36\times10^{-14}\), \texttt{refute\_instrument\_math} row
14). Status: \textbf{LEAN (annihilation) + DERIVED + RUN}; targets
\texttt{cut\_makes\_segment\_head\_absorbing} {[}M{]},
\texttt{masked\_softmax\_blockdiag} {[}S{]}.

\textbf{Proposition 7 (consequence as a displacement field).} (i) For
\((P,V)\to(P',V')\),
\(\Delta z=(I-\gamma P')^{-1}(\Delta V+\gamma\,\Delta P\,z)\) exactly
--- one extra solve (four lines; \citep{bottou-2013-counterfactual} §7.3
is its linearisation). (ii) For lower-triangular \(P'\) with
\(P'_{<i,\cdot}=P_{<i,\cdot}\), \(V'_{<i}=V_{<i}\): \(z'_{<i}=z_{<i}\)
and the suffix re-solve equals the full re-solve --- by
\emph{triangularity}, not nilpotency (\texttt{RUN{[}F{]}}
\(5.6\times10^{-17}\) on the non-nilpotent softmax corner); consequences
propagate forward only, \(\Delta z_0=0\) on every draw. (iii) For a
\textbf{row clamp} (Definition 6(i)) \(P'=P+e_iu^\top\),
\(u^\top\mathbb 1=0\), \(M=(I-\gamma P)^{-1}\):
\(\Delta z=\gamma(Me_i)(u^\top z)/(1-\gamma u^\top Me_i)\) with
denominator \((1-\gamma p'_{ii})/(1-\gamma P_{ii})>0\) for every
\(\gamma<1\), absorbing rows included (\texttt{RUN{[}I{]}}
\(1.45\times10^{-15}\); \texttt{RUN{[}F{]}} \(0.511918\)); the column
\(Me_i\) costs one substitution (\(s^2/2\) MACs), the update \(O(sd)\);
\(m\) candidates cost \(m\cdot s^2/2\) against the solve's \(s^2d/2\),
ratio \(m/d=0.5\) at \((8,16)\). For a \textbf{token rewrite}
(Definition 6(ii)) \(\Delta P\) has rank \(s-i\) and the price is the
suffix re-solve \((s-i)^2d/2\) --- pricing the arm at the oracle's
rank-one rate is M-8 literally. (iv)
\(V\equiv\mathbb 1\Rightarrow\Delta z\equiv0\) exactly; in floats
\(\le10^{-15}\) (\texttt{RUN{[}F{]}} \(1.1\times10^{-16}\); a bitwise
\(0.0\) is a one-code-path accident); the region is a Gaussian \(V\)
(\(0.1096\) at \(s=32\), \(1.127\) at \(s=64\)). (v) A value-only move
leaves every \(q^{(k)}\) constant across moves --- the \(m(K+1)\)-RHS
route is legal for \(\Delta z\) only. \emph{Metric} (VENUS):
position-matched per-coordinate NRMSE over coordinates \(\ge i_{\min}\),
field cosine, magnitude ratio, harmonic residual
\(r(\hat z)=\|(I-\gamma P_{\rm env})\hat z-V\|_\infty/\|V\|_\infty\)
with \(\sigma_{\min}\) and \(\|I-\gamma P_{\rm env}\|_\infty\) printed
(its units), the sign column kept (softmax reads \(0.807843\),
\texttt{READ\ MATHEMATICS.md:396-406}); McNemar on the sign column only,
paired \(t\)/Wilcoxon on the cosine. Mechanism: V-24, D-5, V-26, M-8,
P-8, V-17. Status: \textbf{DERIVED + RUN}; targets
\texttt{displacement\_identity} {[}M{]}, \texttt{causal\_forward\_only}
{[}S{]}, \texttt{sherman\_morrison\_row} {[}S{]} (docstring: row clamp
only).

\textbf{Remark (EMC, retired to a remark).} For \(\gamma<1\) the map
\(x\mapsto V+\gamma P'x\) is a contraction with one fixed point, so
``settle then intervene'' and ``intervene then solve'' coincide for
every row-stochastic \(P'\), cyclic or not (\texttt{RUN{[}J{]}}
\(8.9\times10^{-16}\) on a dense feedback \(P\); \texttt{RUN{[}I{]}}
\(1.33\times10^{-15}\); \texttt{RUN{[}F{]}} \(4.4\times10^{-16}\)).
\citep{dash-2005-emc} Thm 1 concerns a reduced model whose equilibrated
form hides feedback; an explicit linear fixed point has no such gap. The
``planted feedback instance'' exhibits loss of forward-only propagation
(\(\Delta z_{<i}\ne0\) on a non-causal \(P\)) and is filed as a
\textbf{triangularity} bind under Proposition 7(ii).
\citep{momennejad-2017-sr} / \citep{russek-2017-predictive} (mechanism
{[}U{]}) remain the external argument that a cached resolvent needs the
re-solve under a transition change. Ruling: two refuters
KEEP-with-label, two KILL; the KILL stands because the rejection region
was an artefact of a wrong algorithm (V-24, V-3).

\textbf{Proposition 8 (the safest move; degeneracy; the reach-avoid
identity with a sink).} With Definition 3,
\(q^{({\rm sink})}+q^{(0)}+\sum_{k\ge1}q^{(k)}=\mathbb 1\) on \(T\)
(Proposition 3; \texttt{RUN{[}I{]}} \([0.9999999999999993,1.0]\)).
\emph{Degeneracy lemma.} If the constraint sets exhaust the absorbing
states, \(\sum_{k\ge1}q^{(k)}=\mathbb 1\) and \(\max_kq^{(k)}\ge1/K\) by
pigeonhole --- ``avoid every constraint'' is unattainable (V-12
generalised). Its planted negative is ``BOS declared inside a constraint
set''; the plant ``drop \(\mathcal A_0\)'' cannot run (dropping BOS
makes \(I-Q\) singular, \texttt{refute\_instrument\_math} row 3), and
the coordinator's \(0.605\) was computed \emph{with} the goal counted.
\emph{Floors.} The zero-information floor on the argmin is chance,
\(1-\max_a\pi(a^\star)=1-1/m\) uniform: \(0.75/0.875/0.9375\) at
\(m=4/8/16\); the weak Fano \(1-\ln2/\ln m\) (\(0.5/0.6667/0.75\)) is
struck as a floor (an arm at error \(0.60\), \(m=4\), would print above
it while worse than chance); tight Fano \((\ln m-\ln2)/\ln(m-1)\)
(\(0.6309/0.7124/0.7679\), \texttt{RUN{[}J{]}}) only where
\(I(X_{\le k};a^\star)>0\) is computed; \(m=2\) has floor \(0.5\). The
zero-hop view carries the move tokens and membership flags, so
\(I(X_{\le0};a^\star)>0\) and the plug-in is computed (V-25 on a floor).
\emph{Census.} Label sd over the admitted query region (the prefix
before the first constraint is constant); every class frequency of
\(a^\star\) in \((0.05,0.95)\) (the ``argmin-unique'' gate was
inverted); rule-disagreement fraction \(>0\); discard count;
\(q^{({\rm sink})}\) apart from \(q^{(0)}\). \emph{Bed.} The environment
chain must lie inside the arm's operator class or the identity licenses
nothing (\texttt{refute\_theory\_occvac} §2.4,
\texttt{refute\_instrument\_occvac} FATAL-1): an undirected
Rips/\texttt{bed\_1} chain has \texttt{SymmSupport} and no causal
\(\hat P\) equals it (one line; target
\texttt{lowerTriangular\_ne\_symmSupport}); a causal chain recoverable
from edge tokens makes the label the arm's own resolvent (D-2). Ruling:
BED-S's environment is a random DAG in token order with self-loops,
adjacency rows as multi-hot node features so \(\hat P=P_{\rm env}\) is
representable at \(d_{\rm model}\ge n_{\rm nodes}\); the VOID list is
pre-registered --- \texttt{shape\ −\ softmax} and
\texttt{shape\ −\ skyline} are reproduction-vs-non-reproduction
contrasts; creditable are \texttt{shape\ −\ ChaCAL-diag-with-sink}
(boundary mechanism), \texttt{shape\ −\ Neumann-K} (exactness) and
\(\|\hat P-P_{\rm env}\|_\infty\) per seed (identification); leak clause
C2(a) is dropped (the graph is the bed's input), C2(c) kept;
\texttt{bed\_1}/E4′ remain oracle cross-checks (Kirchhoff at \(K=2\)).
Hazard carried to Limits: \citep{misra-2023-safety-constrained-mdp}.
Mechanism: V-8, V-12, V-25, D-2, D-3, M-2, V-10, L-FLOOR. Status:
\textbf{OPEN} (no cell; discharged by PLAN items S-1 to S-4 of the
ledger).

\textbf{Proposition 9 (the horizon dial).} \(\gamma=0\): next step,
softmax bitwise (P1); \(0<\gamma<1\): the hitting-time transform
\(E[\gamma^{\tau-1}]\) (P3); \(\gamma\uparrow1\): absorption
probabilities, basin membership, the \(q=\tfrac12\) isocommittor for
\(K=2\) (\texttt{ceq/beds/bed\_1.py:5-8,:382},
\citep{e-2010-tptreview}); corner 3 is \(\gamma=1\) on a nilpotent chain
(P2). \emph{Pinning.} Ruling 10′'s
\(\Lambda=2[LL(\hat\gamma)-LL(\gamma=0)]\) on held-out, null and
held-out set declared for \(\gamma\) (Ruling 10′ was stated for
\(\beta\)); with \(\gamma\in[0,1)\) the null sits on a boundary and the
\(95\%\) point is \(\tfrac12\chi^2_0+\tfrac12\chi^2_1=2.7055\), not
\(3.8415\) (\texttt{RUN{[}J{]}}; the citation is owed, not in
\texttt{references.bib}); the \(|\hat\gamma|<0.05\) rule is deleted (two
verdicts for one cell); the mirror kill \(\hat\gamma>0.99\) prints
\(1/(1-\hat\gamma)\) beside every \(\delta\)
(\texttt{READ\ V15\_R1.md:56}). Mechanism: V-9, M-2, V-17. Status:
\textbf{DERIVED (corollary)}; instrument OPEN.

\textbf{Proposition 10 (invertibility on the causal class is a diagonal
read).} For the arm's causal \(P\) with finite logits, \(Q=P_{TT}\) is
lower-triangular, \(\rho(Q)=\max_{i\in T}P_{ii}\) (\texttt{RUN{[}J{]}}
\(0.6926596893360386\) both), and \(I-Q\) is invertible \textbf{iff}
\(0\in\mathcal A\) (\(\det(I-Q)=0.0\) undeclared): ``absorption a.s.''
is the single census line ``BOS declared'', and
\texttt{committor\_is\_resolvent\_read}(b) is {[}M{]} via
\texttt{lower\_triangular\_isUnit}; the Perron certificate is for the
oracle's non-causal chain only (\texttt{SymmSupport},
\(\rho(Q)=0.9409\)). The \(\|Q\|_\infty\) Neumann bound on the committor
is vacuous (\(\|Q\|_\infty=0.958\), bound \(19.2\) at \(K=4\) vs error
\(2.5\times10^{-3}\), \texttt{RUN{[}I{]}}): the committor head is the
exact solve and claims no truncation certificate. Mechanism: V-10, V-25,
L-CERT. Status: \textbf{DERIVED + RUN};
\texttt{isUnit\_one\_sub\_of\_perron} split into (b-causal) {[}M{]} and
(b-chain) {[}S{]}.

\textbf{Proposition 11 (regime boundary).} The softmax corner keeps
\(P_{ii}>0\), so \(\gamma P\) is not nilpotent
(\texttt{one\_not\_nilpotent}) and ``all \(s\) hops by nilpotency''
holds in regime N only. The discriminating evidence is
\(\max_{i\ge1}(\gamma P)^{32}_{ii}=6.27\times10^{-13}\) beside
\(\gamma^{32}=7.96\times10^{-8}\) attained at \((0,0)\)
(\texttt{RUN{[}M{]}}; the design's number was V-4). The masked-diagonal
route is not a matrix identity
(\(\|(I-\gamma P_m)^{-1}-\sum_{t<s}(\gamma P_m)^t\|=1.99\times10^{-7}\));
it is exact on a value channel iff \(V_0=0\), which the Definition-3
sink satisfies on every indicator channel (target
\texttt{masked\_sink\_finite\_sum} {[}S{]}). Default: keep the diagonal
and carry Proposition 4. Mechanism: V-25, P-3, V-4. Status: \textbf{LEAN
+ RUN}.

\subsection{2.3 What the shape computes}\label{what-the-shape-computes}

Consequence: \(\Delta z\) (Proposition 7), one extra solve, scored as a
field. Committor vector: \(\hat q\) by the exact solve on \(\hat Q\)
(Propositions 3, 10). Safest move: the two rules of Definition 7 with
their disagreement fraction; the threshold form
\(\max_k\hat q^{(k)}+\delta\|V\|_\infty\le\delta_{\rm thr}\) bounds the
solve, never the model error \(\hat P\ne P_{\rm env}\) --- no move is
``admitted'' by it (V-17). Next transient state / phase / equilibrium:
the dial (Proposition 9); ``phase'' is metastable membership
(\citep{prinz-2011-msm}, \citep{ramsauer-2021-hopfield},
\citep{erel-2025-attentionchains}).

\subsection{2.5 The cost law and the kernel
path}\label{the-cost-law-and-the-kernel-path}

Per head, MACs, increments over the softmax head contained at
\(\gamma=0\) (\texttt{sec\_cost.md} §4.x.9): serial substitution
\(+s^2d/2\), depth \(s\), \(O(s^2)\) memory (\(P\) explicit; no bf16
path for \texttt{solve\_triangular} {[}U{]}); chunked
\(+s^2d/2+sCd/2+sC^2/3\), depth \(s/C\),
\texttt{NOT\ MEASURED\ —\ needs\ a\ chunked\ kernel} (pattern
\citep{yang-2024-deltanet}); Neumann \(+Ks^2d/2\), depth \(K\), never
wins in MACs; F0-segmented \(+\sum_mL_m^2d/2\); CSR two-stage
\(+\,\)units\(\cdot B^2d/2\), forward-only. Measured increment
\(2.514-1.473=1.041\) ms/step at \(n=2048\) (\(2.312\) at \(4096\),
\(4.542\) at \(8192\)), a per-op floor under a dispatch gap of
\(2.0\times\)--\(6.6\times\)
(\texttt{READ\ scale/m3\_flops.py:101-121}). One bed-cell pair on the
softmax corner \(\approx34\) s; seven arms
\(7\times8\times1.680+4.0\approx98\) s \(\approx1.6\) GPU-min
(\texttt{refute\_instrument\_math} row 28 corrects the design's \(4\));
the corner-3 base (\(\approx133\) s) has no certificate domain
(\(\hat\beta=0\) rows sum \(1.31\)--\(10.29\)). Determinism:
\texttt{solve\_triangular} bitwise forward and backward over 8 repeats
under strict mode, no torch-documented guarantee; \texttt{cumsum}
raises; the shape on the softmax corner is the first \emph{gated wing}
whose training step runs under
\texttt{use\_deterministic\_algorithms(True)}. Circuit class: DET
(\citep{cook-1985-taxonomy}, §3.3). Must-fire checks: parity at
\(\gamma=0\) with the \(\gamma=0.5\) region; solve vs dense inverse with
a non-triangular plant; certificate in vector units with the convergent
plant; segmentation \texttt{torch.equal} zeros with the \(-30\) plant;
CSR fill-in guard; three-outcome determinism; cell price within the
law-vs-measured band. Mechanism: M-8, M-3, L-CERT, V-17, P-1, P-8, V-16,
V-23.

\subsection{2.7 Limits carried to §8}\label{limits-carried-to-8}

Every \texttt{RUN{[}J{]}} number is one numpy float64 draw at \(s=32\),
\(\gamma=0.6\), seed 0, CPU, an identity or counterexample check, not a
statistic; other planets' runs are quoted at their own geometries. The
NEPTUNE timings are one box, float32, a per-op floor, producer owed to
\texttt{scripts/k\_cert.py}. The ChaCAL diagonal convention rests on one
HTML fetch (\texttt{refute\_falsify\_math} §3.15) and is re-read against
the PDF before typesetting. Theorem numbers inside cited sources inherit
the sweeps' \texttt{{[}U{]}} marks; \citep{grinstead-1997-probability}
is \texttt{{[}U{]}}; the boundary-null citation is owed. Proposition 8
has no cell, no realised sd, no measured \(t^\star\); its DAG bed is
written, not run. The barcode and Mapper rows have no instrument. Lean
grades are statements about statements; nothing was compiled. No code
file, no git write, no external fetch by this judge.

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\section{3. Why softmax cannot occupy this ground --- exactly as the
theorems license
it}\label{why-softmax-cannot-occupy-this-ground-exactly-as-the-theorems-license-it}

JUPITER (MYCROFT), 2026-09-03, HEAD \texttt{207e7b9}. Evidence classes
as in \texttt{judge/sec\_shape.md}. Every obstruction sentence below
carries a theorem number, the model class it is stated for, its
hypotheses as a predicate on the geometry the record ran (\(s=64\),
\(d=16\), \(h=1\), float32 so \(p=32\)), and the fraction of that
geometry it admits. A ``cannot'' sentence outside the skyline table of
§3.7 is refused. Mechanism throughout: P-10 (a source's intro cited as
its theorem), V-25 (a theorem whose hypothesis no draw satisfies), P-3 /
P-11 (a conditional bound stated as settled), P-8 / V-17 (``depth'' in
two units).

\subsection{3.1 Obstruction 1 --- per-row independence
(definitional)}\label{obstruction-1-per-row-independence-definitional}

One softmax step is \(O_i=f(P_{i\cdot},V)\): the read at \(i\) never
depends on the read at \(j\). The shape's \(z\) is the unique fixed
point of the \(\gamma\)-contraction \(z=V+\gamma Pz\) in
\(\|\cdot\|_\infty\) (\texttt{Contraction.rowStochastic\_perron}
\texttt{:118}, \texttt{weighted\_contraction} \texttt{:72}), so the
reads are mutually constrained. No citation licenses more than this; the
nearest published fixed points are per-query
(\citep{ramsauer-2021-hopfield}), and the sharpness failure of the row
mixture is \citep{velickovic-2025-softmaxnotenough}. What makes it
measurable is the harmonic residual \(r(\hat z)\) of Proposition 7 with
its two unit factors printed --- a \(10\times\) ratio at equal marginal
error is possible only if one arm's error lies in the slow mode, which
is a statement about what the control mislearns, not ``joint
consistency'' (\texttt{refute\_falsify\_occvac} §3.12). The claim
``joint determination in one read'' is UNTESTED
(\texttt{READ\ MATHEMATICS.md:106-127}) and stays a prediction with the
counter ``\(r_{\rm softmax}/r_{\rm shape}\le2\)'' (SPLIT in \((2,10)\)).
Mechanism: V-26, V-17, D-7.

\subsection{3.2 Obstruction 2 --- hops need depth (skyline
unconditional, lower bounds
conditional)}\label{obstruction-2-hops-need-depth-skyline-unconditional-lower-bounds-conditional}

\emph{Skyline (unconditional).} \citep{sanford-2024-logdepth} Thm 4.2: a
causally masked transformer computes \(\mathrm{hop}_k\) at depth
\(\lfloor\log_2k\rfloor+2\), \(m=O(1)\), \(H=1\) --- \(3/5/7\) at
\(t^\star=2/8/32\). \citep{merrill-2025-littledepth} Thm 2: connectivity
at \(\lceil\log_2n\rceil\) unrolls of a uniform block.
\citep{fagnou-2024-chacal} Thm 1:
\(\lceil\log_2(\mathrm{depth}(G)+1)\rceil\) layers for entity tracking
--- the same law with different constants, cited together. \emph{Lower
bounds.} \citep{sanford-2024-logdepth} Cor. 4.3, \(\Omega(\log k)\),
\textbf{conditional} on the one-vs-two-cycle conjecture,
\(k=\Theta(N^\xi)\), \(mH=O(k^{1-\varepsilon})\);
\citep{sanford-2024-graph-algorithms} Thm 3/19, same conjecture;
\citep{chen-2024-multilayer} Thm 1.1, \textbf{unconditional} for
decoder-only softmax at depth \(L\) against \(L\)-sequential
composition, hypothesis \(H\,d\,p\le n^{2^{-4L}}\): at \(L=1\),
\(n=64\), \(64^{1/16}=1.2968\) against \(H\,d\,p=512\) --- vacuous at
every geometry the record ran; \citep{sanford-2024-inductionheads} Thm
1, unconditional at \(t^\star=2\), hypothesis \(h\,m\,p=\Omega(n)\):
\(512\ge64\), not violated, silent here. \emph{Width.}
\citep{yehudai-2025-depthwidth}: depth is not necessary at linear width;
the matched control fixes width as well as depth, and at the record's
geometry \(d_{\rm model}=16<s=64\) the wide skyline is not instantiated
at matched parameters --- said, not left blank. \emph{Circuit class.}
\citep{merrill-2023-parallelism} Thm 2: log-precision constant depth
\(\subseteq\) uniform \(TC^0\); connectivity L-complete; consequences
conditional on class separations. The clause ``linear systems
P-complete'' is dropped: rational linear systems are in
\(DET\subseteq NC^2\) (\citep{cook-1985-taxonomy}); P-completeness is
linear \emph{inequalities} (\texttt{refute\_theory\_math} item 13).
\emph{What the four license together:} an asymptotic, lineage argument
that one softmax parameter set cannot compose \(t^\star\ge2\)
content-dependent hops --- and nothing at the record's geometry.
\emph{The missing reduction.} \(\mathrm{hop}_k\) is pointer chasing in a
token-defined graph; the shape's reachability is in the graph \(P\)
\emph{the layer computes}; ``every \(\mathrm{hop}_k\) instance is a
committor instance of some \(P\)'' is plausible and NOT FOUND
(\texttt{sweep\_expressivity.md} §3.5). Until written, obstruction 2 is
a lineage argument, and the skyline depth
\(\lfloor\log_2t^\star\rfloor+2\) for BED-S is a choice \textbf{by
analogy} with Thm 4.2, which does not cover BED-S's task. Mechanism:
P-10, V-25, P-3, P-4.

\subsection{3.3 The DET-class relocation --- what ``one operator'' may
claim}\label{the-det-class-relocation-what-one-operator-may-claim}

The exact committor solves \((I-Q)q=R\mathbb 1\); the exact \(z\) is
\((I-\gamma P)^{-1}V\). Inversion and iterated product are DET-complete,
\(NL\subseteq DET\subseteq NC^2\) (\citep{cook-1985-taxonomy}), and a
triangular inverse is DET-hard (iterated product embeds in
\((I-A)^{-1}\) for block sub-diagonal \(A\)). So the resolvent as a
circuit sits at or above the class the conditional bounds place
reachability in: \textbf{the shape relocates the log-depth from the
parameter stack into the linear solve} --- \(O(s)\) sequential rounds by
substitution, or recursive block inversion at \(O(\log^2 s)\) circuit
depth / \(O(\log s)\) matmul rounds with \(O(s^3)\)-class work, exact in
regime N and a certified Neumann truncation at \(K=2^k-1\) in regime S
(\texttt{refute\_theory\_math} item 14,
\texttt{refute\_instrument\_math} row 24;
\texttt{NOT\ MEASURED\ —\ needs\ a\ parallel-prefix\ kernel\ timing}).
``Depth-1'' in this paper means \textbf{one attention parameter set}
(\(P\), \(\gamma\)); the circuit-depth reading is disclaimed in this
paragraph. Mechanism: P-8, V-17.

\subsection{3.4 Obstruction 3 ---
composition}\label{obstruction-3-composition}

\citep{peng-2024-transformer-limitations} Thm 1 (identifier confirmed;
unconditional, communication complexity): a single \(H\)-head softmax
layer at \(p\) bits errs on \(f(g(x))\) with probability
\(\ge R/(3n\log n)\), \(R=n\log n-H(d+1)p>0\). At \(n=64\):
\(64\cdot6=384\) against \(1\cdot17\cdot32=544\) --- not satisfied,
vacuous at \(s=64\), \(d=16\), float32; it bites at \(n\gtrsim100\) or
\(p=16\). \citep{kozachinskiy-2025-strassen} Thm 3.4 removes precision
(infinite precision, asymptotic \(n^{\Omega(1)}\)). ``A consequence
\emph{is} a composition'' (\(z'=R(P')V'\) after \(P'=S(P,a)\)) is a
reduction sketch, not a theorem about the committor. Strassen attention
is a NEAR-MISS: two hops per layer by arity, not all hops by inversion.
Mechanism: V-25, P-10.

\subsection{3.5 Obstruction 4 --- non-negativity, WITHDRAWN as
written}\label{obstruction-4-non-negativity-withdrawn-as-written}

For row-stochastic \(P\) the mixing matrix \(\Pi_\gamma\) is
non-negative and stays non-negative under absorbing rows (Proposition
5b), so \(\partial O_i/\partial V_j\ge0\) survives the resolvent and
\texttt{ceq/attention.py}'s min-entry test reads exactly \(0\) on the
shape --- by the identity rows and the mask, a theorem instance, not a
reading (\texttt{RUN{[}F{]}}: \(151\) exact zeros from the identity
rows, lower-triangle min \(2.6\times10^{-4}\) without them). Absorption
\textbf{redirects} mass: a walk that hits \(a\in\mathcal A_k\) stays at
\(a\), so the weight on every earlier token the walk would have reached
through \(a\) goes to \(a\) instead; on a dense causal softmax the
\emph{support} of \(\Pi_\gamma\) does not change (Proposition 5c), only
the weights on rows downstream of \(a\). The paper writes ``a boundary
row captures the mass that would have reached earlier positions'', never
``veto'', ``subtract'' or ``negative influence''; the signed programme
is not revived (\texttt{RESEARCH.md}, \texttt{PROGNOSIS.md}); DoFormer's
single fixed row is the NEAR-MISS named beside ``boundary rows''.
Mechanism: P-7, V-23, V-3.

\subsection{3.6 D-1, stated with the right predictor and the right
census}\label{d-1-stated-with-the-right-predictor-and-the-right-census}

\citep{marion-2025-single-location} Cor. 2 (erf predictor,
\(d\to\infty\), \(L=o(d)\)) and \citep{duranthon-2026-softmax-advantage}
Prop. 4.2 (softmax proper): one layer is Bayes-optimal on
single-location regression \(Y=X_{J_0}^\top v^\star+\xi\). The record
ran \(L/d=4.00\) (\texttt{READ\ MATHEMATICS.md:1044-1057}), the wrong
direction; and the theorems' label model admits \textbf{0 of 3} record
beds --- BED-M's label is a product
\(a_{s-1}\cdots a_{s-t^\star}\,b_{s-1-t^\star}\) of \(t^\star+1\)
features at \(t^\star+1\) positions, not single-location regression for
any \(t^\star\ge1\) (\texttt{refute\_theory\_occvac} §1.2). D-1 is
therefore a \emph{mechanism}: no bed in the record leaves the
scalar-readout class (\(\mathrm{out}[:,s-1]\),
\texttt{READ\ MISTAKES.md:677-708}), and the SLR theorems are the
nearest owned regime, cited as such. No bed in this paper scores a
scalar at one position: the labels are \(z\), \(\Delta z\), the
reach-avoid tensor, the argmin. Single-effect estimation is also
softmax's ground (\citep{zhang-2023-cina}), which is why the sign column
of Proposition 7 is kept and not claimed. Mechanism: D-1, P-10, V-25.

\subsection{3.7 The skyline table --- the only place a ``cannot''
sentence may
stand}\label{the-skyline-table-the-only-place-a-cannot-sentence-may-stand}

Every bed carries every row; a claim is at matched depth and parameters
(\(4{,}769\), recounted per arm because a \([m,K+1]\) head or a vector
readout changes it --- Ruling 3) against the fellow approximators, and
the skylines at unmatched depth/width/decode length are read beside it
(R-SKY, \texttt{READ\ CEQ\_V16\_CONTRACT.md:209}).

\citep{wang-2024-incontext-td} and \citep{xie-2026-softmax-rl} {[}V{]}
(abstract level; theorem numbers not read, {[}U{]}) show
linear-then-softmax transformers implement TD policy evaluation layer by
layer, so the deeper stack computes the \emph{same} resolvent by
iteration: the looped block \(z^{(t+1)}=V+\gamma Pz^{(t)}\) run \(K\)
times is the truncated Neumann sum with matrix-residual \(\infty\)-norm
exactly \(\gamma^{K+1}/(1-\gamma)\) and vector error at most
\(\gamma^{K+1}\|V\|_\infty/(1-\gamma)\) (Proposition 4;
\citep{yang-2024-looped}, \citep{gasteiger-2019-appnp} power iteration).
A nonlinear loop is a DEQ (\citep{bai-2019-deq}) and out of scope.
``Beats softmax'' and ``beats native'' (\citep{yang-2024-deltanet},
\citep{dao-2024-ssd} on the linear corner) are banned sentences; the
shape's separate claims are exactness with a printed
\(\delta\|V\|_\infty\), one-read consistency as a \textbf{cost}
statement (one solve, depth \(s\)), and the boundary-row mechanism, each
stated as a property with its counter-prediction. Mechanism: D-1, R-SKY,
D-7, V-17, P-4.

\subsection{3.8 The honest sentence}\label{the-honest-sentence}

One \emph{parameterised} operator (one \(P\), one \(\gamma\)), not one
parallel round; its hops are exact and certified (Propositions 2, 4)
rather than learned layer by layer; on a bed whose environment chain is
inside the arm's class the contrast against softmax is a
reproduction-vs-non-reproduction contrast and is pre-registered VOID as
a capability number (Proposition 8); what is creditable is the boundary
mechanism against ChaCAL-diag-with-sink, exactness against the Neumann
read, and the identification \(\|\hat P-P_{\rm env}\|_\infty\). Under
the record's calibration (7 of 8 optimistic,
\texttt{READ\ V16\_CALIBRATION.md:96-100}) the counter is the point
estimate for every row above.

\subsection{3.9 Limits carried to §8}\label{limits-carried-to-8-1}

Every obstruction clause is asymptotic, and every unconditional one is
vacuous at \(s=64\), \(d=16\), float32; the inequalities are printed so
a reader sees it. The \(\mathrm{hop}_k\to\) committor reduction is
unwritten. The TD-policy-evaluation skyline claim rests on two
abstracts. The skyline depths are by analogy, the skyline prices are
assumed or unmeasured, and the two non-depth skylines have no cell. No
sentence here is a result.

\texttt{Nonneg\ True}, \texttt{SymmSupport\ True},
\(\rho(Q)=0.9408612510154677\),
\(\max\lvert Q^{11}\rvert=0.47739216729378814\ne 0\) (\textbf{RUN}) &
BED-1; \textbf{D-2} (\texttt{MISTAKES.md:710}) as a theorem \\
\texttt{OracleSeparation.truncation\_never\_exact} &
\texttt{OracleSeparation.lean:180} & the same & Every truncation rung
leaves a residual on such a \(Q\). & the committor head therefore ships
as an \textbf{exact solve} with \(\delta=0\) to rounding, never as a
Neumann \(\delta\) (ledger row P10) & BED-1, E4′ \\
\end{longtable}
}

\textbf{Where the census is empty.} \texttt{V15.prefix\_logit\_mask}
admits 1 of 3 drawn values, and every magnitude theorem in
\texttt{V15Phase} carries \(\forall k,\ 0<m_k\), so all are decoration
on BED-M's \(a=0\) draw (\texttt{READ\ V16\_LEAN\_DOMAIN.md:151-160});
their closed-support replacements are the \texttt{pathProd\_*} clauses,
at 3 of 3. That substitution is the record's answer to \textbf{V-25}
(\texttt{MISTAKES.md:1954}), kept as the standing law \textbf{L-DOM} and
discharged per target by \textbf{J-L18} and per bed by \textbf{S-12}.

\textbf{The regime boundary.} The shape's default operator is the
\(\beta=1\) corner: a causal softmax row over \(j\le i\), so
\(P_{ii}>0\) and \(\gamma P\) is lower-triangular \textbf{with} a
diagonal. \texttt{pow\_card\_eq\_zero} needs \texttt{StrictlyLower}, and
\texttt{one\_not\_nilpotent} (\texttt{Nilpotent.lean:105}) is the file's
own warning that the strictness is load-bearing. \((I-\gamma P)^{-1}\)
is therefore an \emph{infinite} Neumann series, not the \(n\)-term sum,
so ``the resolvent computes all \(s\) hops in one operator by
nilpotency'' holds only for the diagonal-free operator. The paper names
two regimes and says which theorem carries which: \textbf{regime N}, the
strictly causal corner, where \texttt{occupancy\_is\_exact\_inverse}
applies verbatim; \textbf{regime S}, the softmax corner, where §4.2's
certificate pays instead. On the non-nilpotent corner the masked route
reads \(1.99\times10^{-7}\) against the unmasked \(6.27\times10^{-13}\)
and the naive \(7.96\times10^{-8}\) (\textbf{RUN}, ledger row P11), so
the masked identity is a value-channel statement conditioned on
\(V_0=0\), not a matrix identity, and the default keeps the diagonal.
Cards \textbf{J-L3}, \textbf{J-L5}, \textbf{J-L16}. Mechanism:
\textbf{P-3} (\texttt{:316}) --- the nilpotency sentence was true of the
record's operator and would otherwise have been carried forward
unqualified.

\textbf{The refutations proved in-file.} Fourteen declarations ship the
statement they are \emph{not}, each with a witness in the same file ---
the structural answer to \textbf{V-3} (\texttt{:72}) and \textbf{V-10}
(\texttt{:168}). Four are load-bearing.
\texttt{V15.gate\_zero\_not\_stochastic} (\texttt{V15.lean:237},
hypothesis \(1\le i\)) kills the round-11 parity clause in the
multiplicative reading: row \(i\) sums to \(i+1\), smallest witness
\(i=1\) with sum \(2\), so \(g\equiv 0\) lands on \textbf{linear}
attention, and only the additive-logit reading
(\texttt{gate\_zero\_logit\_identity}, \texttt{:251}) gives parity.
\texttt{V16Domain.no\_prefix\_scan\_represents\_a\_zero\_gate}
(\texttt{:165}) forecloses the whole prefix-scan route to exact
segmentation, which is why the F0 certificate is the product itself or a
segmented scan resetting at zeros
(\texttt{READ\ ceq/arm\_smprime.py:157-161}).
\texttt{V16Domain.lean\_log\_junk\_makes\_the\_scan\_form\_silently\_false}
(\texttt{:147}) records that relaxing \(0<a\) to \(0\le a\) while
keeping the \(\log\) form is \emph{silently} wrong ---
\texttt{Real.log\ 0\ =\ 0} is junk, and the file exhibits
\texttt{Wp\ 0\ 0\ 1\ 0\ =\ 1} beside
\texttt{pathProd\ 0\ 0\ 1\ 0\ =\ 0}; the zero-gate machinery is written
on \texttt{pathProd} for that reason alone.
\texttt{V15Source.inverse\_identity\_is\_vacuous} (\texttt{:83}) refuses
reading the contract's Lean \#15 as \(MM^{-1}=1\): true of every unit of
every ring, dependent on no axioms, used nowhere. Beside them
\texttt{zero\_not\_a\_counterexample}
(\texttt{OracleSeparation.lean:193}) records that dropping \(0<Q_{ij}\)
leaves the statement \emph{true} --- the zero matrix is non-negative,
symmetric-support and nilpotent --- a vacuous control caught in proof
form, and the template every Lean card follows (\textbf{J-L0} to
\textbf{J-L18}).

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

BED-M's real support \(\{-1,0,+1\}\) rather than a toy draw, which is
\textbf{V-25}.

The two-branch form \(\mathrm{softmax}(q)V_1+\lambda XV_2\) passes
parity bitwise for the honest gate, for Gaussian noise, \textbf{and for
the label itself} --- an empty rejection region, filed \textbf{V-24}
(\texttt{READ\ workdonenewseal.md:124-126}). The plan inherits the
battery, not the claim: cards \textbf{S-20} to \textbf{S-27}, every
plant journalled as a FOUND cell with its own \texttt{kind}
(\textbf{S-28}, against \textbf{V-7}, \texttt{:117}).

the certified device.} The row that matters is the split, never the
mean: a marginal standing in for a joint claim is \textbf{V-26}
(\texttt{MISTAKES.md:2190}).

\textbf{The mean \(0.829151\) is a value no seed produced.} That is why
the plan reports every capability number as a split with \(n^{+}\), the
seed-agreement count, beside it (\textbf{V-26}; card \textbf{S-61}, the
adjudicator specification written before data). The recorded verdict is
NOT CROSSED with the interval straddling, and two campaign firsts sit
inside the negative: \(\hat h=0.625\) against a nine-cell ceiling of
\(0.389\), and five seeds below a floor no prior arm had crossed at any
cell. Deduplicated across the three journals (\textbf{RUN}, 43 rows to
40 unique on \texttt{(kind,\ seed)}), \textbf{13 of 40} banked cells sit
below \(\mathrm{floor}_1\), 13 read \(\hat h>1.0\), and \(s=64\) on 40
of 40.

\textbf{The three corners, measured distinct.}
\(\lvert c_1-c_2\rvert=4.472918\), \(\lvert c_1-c_3\rvert=1.144938\),
\(\lvert c_2-c_3\rvert=5.335671\) in float64, against
\texttt{corners\_are\_distinct}; the switches move the operator by
\(0.673101\) (\(g\)) and \(3.522037\) (QK), and \(\beta\), \(g\), QK are
all \texttt{nn.Parameter}s. Mechanism \textbf{V-2} (\texttt{:58}) --- a
containment whose corners coincide is decoration, so the distance is
measured, not asserted.

threads, matmul TF32 off, \texttt{CUBLAS\_WORKSPACE\_CONFIG=:4096:8}

Mechanisms: \textbf{M-8} (\texttt{:544}, pricing every arm at one arm's
rate) --- one law per arm with its \(R^{2}\); \textbf{V-22}
(\texttt{:1140}, a constant certified under conditions the reading does
not reproduce) --- the flag regime is journalled on the header;
\textbf{P-8} --- the \(R^{2}\) gate refuses paged points. What the
certificate does \textbf{not} contain is an exponent in \(s\): \(s=64\)
on 40 of 40 cells, and the per-cell timer is un-synchronised host wall
clock whose strongest correlate is run order, \(\rho=+0.7029\),
\(p=0.0024\), above the gate correlation \(+0.5197\) it would have to be
separated from. That is \textbf{D-3} (\texttt{:727}), discharged only by
card \textbf{S-66} --- the \(s\)-sweep with \texttt{synchronize()} and
randomised order, priced at \(206\)--\(537\) GPU-s, band only, with the
run-order confound \emph{surviving} those edits unless a fifth (blocked
order) is made, which no office has priced.

\textbf{The calibration column.} Round 11: \textbf{9 checked, 9 adverse}
on the filed rows (at least 17 checked, 10 adverse, with the
confirmations the table omits). Signs: \textbf{7} optimistic, \textbf{1}
pessimistic (a sizing repair already made, reported as outstanding),
\textbf{1} unsigned. The one-sided sign test on the eight signed rows
gives \(7/8\), \(p=0.0352\) --- direction established at \(\alpha=0.05\)
--- and the Wilson 95 \% interval on the optimism fraction is
\([0.5291,\,0.9776]\): it excludes \(0.5\) and fixes nothing else, so it
\textbf{licenses an ordering only}. The round's own headline ``six
wrong, all six optimistic'' gives \(5/6\), \(p=0.1094\) on the honestly
signed rows: right about the direction, wrong about its evidence. The
single sub-census with a real denominator reads 11 checked, 4 wrong,
\(36.4\%\), Wilson \([0.152,\,0.646]\), so \(9/9\) is a count and not a
rate. The discount rule §5 adopts verbatim: \textbf{D-CALIB-1} the
counter is the point estimate; \textbf{-2} a bare prediction is blocked,
never discounted; \textbf{-3} the licence is a \emph{sign}, never a
size, so \textbf{no numeric shrink factor is authorised}; \textbf{-4}
the cheapest refutation of the optimistic half runs first; \textbf{-5}
the row is appended whether or not it flatters. Mechanisms \textbf{D-7}
(\texttt{:2037}) and \textbf{M-2} (\texttt{:451}); cards \textbf{V-0},
\textbf{V-1}, \textbf{V-19}.

\textbf{Numbers this paper must not use.} The registry holds \textbf{12}
struck constants (\texttt{READ\ STRUCK.md:18-33}, rendered at
\texttt{aa82df7}), named here by quantity.

\textbf{The named stale traps, with their corrected figures.} ``No arm
crosses \(\mathrm{floor}_1\)'' becomes 6 of 24 at it.2 and \textbf{13 of
40} at it.14 (C1, C15); ``6 of 34 cells violate the floor'' becomes
\textbf{13 of 40} (\textbf{RUN}); ``12 of 16 cross'' becomes ``12 of 16
sit below the floor, and the pooled verdict is
\texttt{crosses:\ false}'', the flip turning on seed 9 alone (C13).
\(p=6.730\times10^{-4}\) was \textbf{unpaired} --- the honest paired
figure on the banked record is \(p=0.012821\), a factor of 19, repaired
by measurement at it.10 to \textbf{8 of 9 against 0 of 9}, paired, over
three draws (C14). Every GPU-second and cost ratio derived from
\texttt{scripts/v15\_r1.py}'s \texttt{secs} is void, the K-cert laws
being the replacement (C17). The ``\(5.8\times\) wall-clock gap'' and
softmax at \(71.32\) s per 150 steps do not reproduce: the same arm,
corpus, thread pin, steps and statistic re-read \(12.3\)--\(13.6\) s on
a quiet host, and what survives is E-core placement plus host
contention, \(41\)--\(83\%\) of the gap in log terms with the residual
unexplained. The ``\(13.6\times\) CPU-to-GPU decision'' becomes
\(11.94\times\) at the point estimate and \(29.0\times\) at the
pessimistic end. \(\sum_j\lvert W_{ij}\rvert=1.000000\) as the
softmax-corner certificate equals \(Z_i^{1-\beta}\) and is blind to
\(g\); write \(\sum_j\mathrm{Re}\,W_{ij}\), at
\(2.220446\times10^{-16}\) from \(1\). ``A pole precisely on the unit
circle'' is \texttt{torch.clamp(u,\ 0,\ 1)} at
\texttt{ceq/arm\_smprime.py:113} --- a ceiling, not a converged pole
(C9). ``The arms return a state distribution'' is false: 0 of 40 banked
cells journal one. And ``beats softmax'' on any BED-M cell is banned by
\textbf{R-SKY}; the paired contrast with its resolution statement is
what the paper writes.

Three readings the table cannot carry. First, pivot routing's deeper
one: \emph{\texttt{pivot\_signed} was \texttt{pivot\_unsigned} wearing a
name} --- at the harness geometry the operator is entrywise non-negative
with minimum entry exactly \(0.0\), so the paired test that credited
sign compared two non-negative operators, and every M2 and S2 headline
was measured on \texttt{tgate}, which ships nowhere. The negative on
signed attention is therefore also a negative on the record's ability to
say which operator it had measured;
\texttt{tests/loop/test\_measured\_operator\_is\_shipped.py} exists
because of it, and card \textbf{S-01} extends the identity manifest so a
cell cannot be journalled without its boundary condition. Second, the
hop-2 mechanism transfers: \emph{the label composes values along paths;
the arms composed weights across positions} --- the algebraic content of
the shape's second identity, and the reason §2 does not add a hop to
softmax but makes the resolvent the operator. Third, the native skyline:
DeltaNet's WY matrix
\(T=\big(I+\mathrm{tril}(\mathrm{diag}(\beta)KK^{\top},-1)\big)^{-1}\mathrm{diag}(\beta)\)
is the exact inverse of a strictly lower-triangular, content-dependent,
signed operator, shipping in \texttt{fla/ops/utils/solve\_tril.py}
(\texttt{CITED\ {[}V{]}} \citep{yang-2024-deltanet}; decay gate
\citep{yang-2024-gateddeltanet}; semiseparable framing
\citep{dao-2024-ssd}). ``Beats native'' is banned for the reason ``beats
softmax'' is --- a fellow approximator of the same class at the same
cost --- so what the shape may claim is a \(\Delta_{\rm sky}\) column
with an interval plus the \emph{mechanism} difference: DeltaNet and SSD
carry no absorbing rows, no committor read, no re-solve under
\(\mathrm{do}(a)\). That difference is written
\texttt{NOT\ FOUND\ —\ sweep\ owed}, never ``novel''.

\textbf{The taxonomy.} \texttt{MISTAKES.md} carries \textbf{66}
mechanism headings (\textbf{RUN}
\texttt{grep\ -cE\ \textquotesingle{}\^{}\#\#\#\ {[}VPMD{]}-\textquotesingle{}\ MISTAKES.md};
the record's own 65 omits the sub-entry \texttt{V-14a} at \texttt{:770})
in four classes, ordered by the cost the record assigns them:
\textbf{V}, vacuous controls, a control that cannot fail (26);
\textbf{P}, provenance, a number with no live producer or a claim true
once (11); \textbf{M}, measurement, where the instrument measured but
not the thing the verdict names (21); \textbf{D}, design, where the
answer was fixed before any data arrived (7). The largest single entry
is \textbf{D-1}, \emph{the largest one in the repository, and it
subsumes most of the null results}.
