Three more sit one step behind: \textbf{V-17} (\texttt{:855}, a
threshold out of its units --- a committor threshold is stated in NRMSE
or floor units, never raw feature distance); \textbf{M-15}
(\texttt{:1289}, a statistic without its sharpness-matched null --- any
\(\beta_0\) persistence number needs its random-operator null first,
card \textbf{S-71}); \textbf{M-2} (\texttt{:451}, a threshold refitted
after the data --- every threshold in §5 is frozen with provenance
beforehand, which is why the round declined to pick its own tail
ruling).

\textbf{The eight corrections to the record found while writing this
paper.} Each is a defect in the repository's own documents, found by
reading them against their sources, and filed here rather than by
editing a verbatim-of-record file.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{The contract's M10 lineage identifiers are all misattributed.}
  arXiv:1905.12200 is Bruel-Gabrielsson et al., 1904.09378 is PersLay
  (\citep{carriere-2019-perslay}), 2011.05804 is Corcoran and Deng
  (\citep{corcoran-2020-ph-gradient-regularization}); the intended
  papers are Hofer 1906.09003
  (\citep{hofer-2019-connectivity-optimized}), Moor 1906.00722
  (\citep{moor-2020-topological-autoencoders}), Carrière 2010.08356
  (\citep{carriere-2021-optimizing-ph}). \textbf{P-5} (\texttt{:343})
  compounded by \textbf{P-10} (\texttt{:1382}).
\item
  \textbf{The Cheeger line is stated on a non-reversible chain.}
  \texttt{MATHEMATICS.md:455} cites Levin--Peres--Wilmer Thm 13.10
  (\citep{levin-2017-markov-mixing}, read at p.~183:
  \(\Phi_\star^{2}/2\le\gamma_{\rm gap}\le 2\Phi_\star\)), whose
  hypothesis is \textbf{reversibility}. A causal \(P\) with \(P_{i0}>0\)
  has \(P_{0i}=0\), so the line is a \textbf{V-25} exposure as written.
  It is restated on the transient block's lazy symmetrisation
  \(Q_s:=(Q+Q^{\top})/2\), whose Cheeger bound controls that surrogate's
  spectral gap and \textbf{not} \(\rho(Q)\), and moved to the oracle
  cross-check chains (BED-1, E4′) where reversibility holds. Card
  \textbf{J-D6}.
\item
  \textbf{Three Lean declarations the contract tags \texttt{{[}M{]}} do
  not exist.} \texttt{CEQ\_V20\_R15\_CONTRACT.md:179}, \texttt{:185},
  \texttt{:213}, \texttt{:260} cite Lean \textbf{\#18}, \textbf{\#19},
  \textbf{\#22} as machine-checked; \textbf{RUN},
  \texttt{grep\ -rn\ \textquotesingle{}segment\textquotesingle{}\ lean/CEQ/}
  returns one hit, a prose comment at \texttt{V16Domain.lean:53}, and no
  declaration carries that content. The \texttt{{[}M{]}} is the
  contract's \emph{grade}, not a build, and the F0 instance the same
  line prints (\(8.9\times10^{-16}\), \(595\times\)) has \textbf{no
  located producing file}. \textbf{P-11} (\texttt{:1597}) realised; the
  targets are re-registered as work in cards \textbf{J-L6} and
  \textbf{J-L7}.
\item
  \textbf{\texttt{THEORY.md:22} calls the successor operator new.} The
  row reads \emph{Successor operator \((I-\gamma P)^{-1}\) \textbar{}
  new \textbar{} exists, mislabelled} --- its audit column already
  contradicts its claim column, and the operator is Dayan 1993
  (\citep{dayan-1993-successor}, whose eq. 3.1 is \((I-Q)^{-1}\) with
  \textbf{no} \(\gamma\), per \texttt{Occupancy.lean:6-17}).
  \textbf{P-3} (\texttt{:316}).
\item
  \textbf{arXiv:2604.25655 does not cite Basseville and Nikiforov.}
  ``The learned-model form of §7.2.4'' is the record's own analogy, not
  the source's claim, and is carried \texttt{{[}U{]}}. \textbf{P-10}.
\item
  \textbf{The journal's head phase table is stale.} It still reads
  \emph{it.15 DONE, it.16 next} for Phase C and \emph{not started} for
  Phases D and E while the body carries entries through it.35 ---
  \textbf{P-3 sitting inside the block built to catch P-3}. The plan
  reads its start state from the it.35 entry.
\item
  \textbf{Rulings 11 and 12 have no text in the tree}, although
  \texttt{CEQ\_V20\_R15\_CONTRACT.md:59} binds ``Rulings 1--12'':
  \textbf{RUN},
  \texttt{grep\ -rniE\ "ruling\ 1{[}12{]}\textbackslash{}b"\ *.md}
  returns zero hits, and the same holds for \texttt{L-GRADE}, cited once
  with no rubric anywhere. The plan states the rubric it uses and marks
  it a reconstruction; it cites neither as if it had text.
\item
  \textbf{The scoreboard is 2 of 44 and the leap call is still owed.}
  Both points are the it.4 freeze; the theory table's \(+6\) was refused
  twice and carried unclaimed through it.35; the round's single leap
  call was \textbf{not spent}; it.36 to it.45 are unspent.
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{The bibliographic traps.} The audit merged eight sweep
bibliographies into \textbf{408} canonical entries (508 parsed, 51
same-key duplicates dropped, 49 same-identifier entries folded through
\texttt{bib\_aliases.md}) and re-ran the identifier check directly
against arXiv and Crossref, the audit service having returned ``not
subscribed'': 278 of 278 arXiv identifiers resolved, 91 of 92 DOIs
resolved, and all seven title-agreement misses were subtitle truncations
of the same works. The one unresolved entry is
\citep{singh-2007-mapper}, a Eurographics identifier absent from
Crossref, carried \texttt{{[}U{]}}. Four traps were caught by hand: DOI
\texttt{10.1007/978-1-4684-9455-6} is \emph{Denumerable Markov Chains}
and \textbf{not} \emph{Finite Markov Chains}, so it must not be attached
to the Kemeny--Snell entry; arXiv:2501.00663 is Titans, not test-time
regression (2501.12352); arXiv:2302.11294 is not Sander et
al.~(2302.01425); Contreras Arredondo et al.~carry the journal title
\emph{Learning the committor without collective variables}. One further
trap was found \textbf{RUN} this session by scanning the merged file for
duplicate titles: \textbf{three} groups survive the identifier-level
fold --- \citep{altman-1999-cmdp} with \citep{altman-1999-constrained},
\citep{basseville-1993-abrupt-changes} with
\citep{basseville-1993-detection}, and \citep{kemeny-1960-finitemarkov}
with \citep{kemeny-1976-finitemarkov} (\citep{kemeny-1976-finite} being
a third key for the Springer printing under its appendix subtitle). All
are books carrying neither an arXiv identifier nor a Crossref DOI ---
precisely the 38-entry class the audit says it could not check --- so
the fold is \emph{known} incomplete there and the assembler picks one
key per work before typesetting. None of this establishes that a source
supports the sentence it is cited for: that is the \texttt{{[}V-eq{]}}
mark, carried per citation, and the record's rule stands that theorem
and equation numbers read through a rendered page are re-checked against
the compiled PDF before publication.

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\subsection{4.4 Where the campaign
stands}\label{where-the-campaign-stands}

\textbf{The scoreboard.} After thirty-five iterations of a
forty-five-iteration round the carried score is \textbf{2 of 44} ---
both points the it.4 wing freeze, nothing else banked. The denominator
is 44 and not the contract's 48 because the \emph{winner's cost at most
one-tenth of the incumbent's} bonus (\(+4\)) was shown unreachable by
either surviving wing: on both timing bases W3 costs \textbf{more} than
softmax, \(1.078\times\) and \(1.059\times\). The theory table's \(+6\)
was frozen at it.14 and ruled NOT FIT for the leap, refused at it.10 and
again at it.11, carried unclaimed; the \(+12\) is gated on a single
unmade author ruling; the annex's \(+4\) is forfeit. The single leap
call was \textbf{not spent}, for the reason on the record: \emph{calling
it now would consume the round's single shot on a table whose header
miscounts its own body, whose published grading rule contradicts its
applied one, and three of whose cells the gate cannot read.} Phase E has
not begun.

\textbf{The frozen wings, and the struck one.} \texttt{FROZEN-N\ =\ 2};
\texttt{FREEZE-SHA256\ =\ fbf17e07e6cab495bd1fdbb4392ab88e92f5b0610e40d2b8a283d8bcb9c542ff}
over eight normalised
\texttt{wing\textbar{}clause\textbar{}path:line\textbar{}anchor} rows
--- the digest covers which wings, lines and anchor strings, and
deliberately \textbf{not} the content of the cited files, which is why
the anchor check is a separate node re-run at every HEAD. W1 is
\texttt{arm\_smprime}, the path product
\(G_{ij}=\prod_k m_k e^{i\theta_k}\) with \(\beta\), QK and \(g\)
switches (\texttt{ceq/arm\_smprime.py:144}); W3 is \texttt{arm\_pl}, a
real prefix scan, key-side only, over a value-zero BOS sink
(\texttt{ceq/arm\_pl.py:88}). Wing identifiers are held at their it.1
values so the strike of \textbf{W2} is visible as a gap rather than
hidden by renumbering. The discriminator that struck it, stated so it
can be run: \emph{a wing is FOUND if and only if \texttt{results/} holds
at least one journalled record whose \texttt{kind} field is the wing's
arm name; otherwise it is NAMED --- it exists in source and in prose,
and the ledger has never seen it.} The census by \texttt{kind} over
every \texttt{results/**/*.jsonl}: \texttt{arm\_pl} \textbf{191},
\texttt{softmax} \textbf{182}, \texttt{arm\_smprime} \textbf{182},
\texttt{arm\_phase} \textbf{0}. The counter is calibrated on both sides
--- it must read \(0\) for \texttt{arm\_phase} and above \(0\) for
\texttt{softmax} --- so the zero is a measurement and not a broken
search (\textbf{V-7}, \texttt{:117}). \textbf{W2 is STRUCK and RETIRED
to UNPRODUCED, not refuted}: the route back is one \texttt{make\_arm}
branch, an optimizer path and a field emit, at \(5.114\) s per cell on
the \texttt{arm\_pl} basis, roughly \(41\) to \(130\) GPU-s for eight
cells. \emph{The W2 gate is under half a minute of wall clock on this
box. It was never a cost decision.} Every arm the plan proposes enters
under this discriminator (card \textbf{S-28}).

\textbf{The eight gate cells, both offices, no coordination.} The theory
table grades two wings against Q1--Q6: twelve cells, one F0, five F1,
one F2, two F3, three F4. The gate's population is \textbf{eight}, not
the nine its own heading claims --- the ninth row is Q1/W1 at F0,
\emph{not a failure} --- and one office rules Q4's two F3s a single
harness fact, so the gate graded \textbf{seven distinct failures on
eight cells}.

Tallies: 5 LEAPABLE and 3 TERMINAL against 4 (one conditional) and 4;
agreement on 6 of 8 tokens, both offices against the table on Q5/W1's
field. \textbf{The two divergences are a missing amendment, not a
judgement call.} Q3/W3 and Q5/W3 are exactly the cells whose verdict
column carried a hybrid --- \emph{LEAPABLE, but about 6 GPU-s buys it
outright} and \emph{LEAPABLE, and it is a SCORING RULE, not a theorem}
--- placed in the gate-class and field columns, where the ruling that a
hybrid verdict is not a verdict does not reach. Beneath them: the ledger
publishes one grading rule (LEAPABLE when the gap is a missing statement
\textbf{or a missing measurement}) while a ruling resolved a cell to
TERMINAL against that rule --- \emph{the round has published one grading
rule and applied another, twenty-one iterations, it.14 to it.35.} Three
further cells the gate cannot see at all (Q2/W1, Q6/W1, Q6/W3), one
filing a \textbf{refutation} inside a token meaning \emph{unattempted};
that ruling is in its seventeenth iteration, unruled. Mechanisms
\textbf{V-14a} (\texttt{:770}, the scope test that condemns every
refusal guard --- the role must be declared) and \textbf{M-20}
(\texttt{:1723}, a pre-registration predicting both outcomes in two
sections that never met).

\textbf{The laws in force, one line each.} \textbf{D-1}: work is a DAG,
parallel dispatch only on nodes with no shared repository state.
\textbf{D-2}: planet names label responsibilities inside documents,
never concurrent processes. \textbf{D-3}: no autonomous loop mounts
until the previous loop's cause of death is one sentence, and the
iteration count comes from the DAG's critical path. \textbf{D-4}:
contracts scheduled behind an unreached round are staged, not started.
\textbf{L-DOM}: every theorem that gates a run ships a domain census,
and no overlap means decoration (pays for V-25). \textbf{L-SIGN}: a
counter-prediction of equal specificity beside every prediction, with a
calibration column across rounds (pays for D-7). \textbf{L-DIAG}: a
contract prescribes what a diagnostic must distinguish, never which
statistic does it (M-18). \textbf{L-FLOOR}: every capability number
ships beside its information floor, so ``how good'' reads as
distance-to-floor --- and \(\mathrm{floor}_1\) is not one.
\textbf{L-CERT}: every mask ships its certificate, F0 exact or F1 with
\(\delta\) printed; a mask without one is refused. \textbf{L-EQ}:
\texttt{{[}V{]}} is inadmissible for a load-bearing statement, and
\texttt{{[}V-eq{]}} requires the theorem with hypotheses plus one
numeric instance run. \textbf{L-LEAN}: the arm is not trained before its
identity theorems are green. \textbf{L-G2}: journals never move and are
never deleted, superseded cells staying with a supersede marker.
\textbf{FOUND-not-NAMED}: a wing named rather than found is struck.
\textbf{R-SKY}: the native skyline is read beside every bed with a
\(\Delta_{\rm sky}\) column, and ``beats softmax'' is not licensed where
softmax is a fellow approximator. \textbf{Ruling 1}: CUDA determinism
with \texttt{warn\_only=True}, bitwise for replay and every deciding
forward cell, training compared against a measured noise floor.
\textbf{Ruling 3}: matched parameters, a \(0.032\%\) residual counting
as matched, exact counts in every table header, no re-architecting to
close it. \textbf{Ruling 10′}: ``pinned'' is decided by
\(\Lambda=2\big[\mathrm{LL}(\beta_{\rm final})-\mathrm{LL}(\beta\equiv
1)\big]\) on held-out data, with the minimum detectable departure
printed. Two of these have thinner text than their citation and are
handled as in §4.3, correction 7.

\textbf{The open debts.} Five author rulings stand open at it.35: the
clause-1 tail, one- or two-sided; the missing F0--F4 rubric; the Kaggle
attach behind the chess witness; the gate's blindness to F4 cells; the
collision between TERMINAL and NOT-PUT. Carried from earlier rounds:
\textbf{D-APPROX}, the approximation bound owed beside every
exact-identity nonexistence theorem --- \emph{every ``X cannot represent
Y'' claim needs a bound before it reads as ``X cannot fit Y''}, the
largest unclosed gap in the round's own logic; \textbf{D-R3}, two
mutually inverse registrations for one bed; the memory ceiling that
dropped the \(n=32768\) reproduction and then the \(16{,}384\) one; the
closed-versus-half-open magnitude interval, filed two-sided because
\([0,1)\) would cost the reachability of \(a=\pm1\), two-thirds of
BED-M's support; the scan skyline, refused in R11 and legal now as the
native control; the HuggingFace package, scheduled and unbuilt. Harness
debts the plan must not inherit silently, all \textbf{RUN} against
\texttt{scripts/v15\_r1.py}: \texttt{S,\ D\ =\ 64,\ 24} is a module
constant at \texttt{:137}, with nine sibling \texttt{add\_argument}
calls and no \texttt{seq\_len} flag anywhere;
\texttt{torch.cuda.synchronize} does not occur in the file;
\texttt{lambda\_hat} at \texttt{:383} averages \(\log m\) over every
position, so one \(m_k=0\) sends it to \(-\infty\) and it carries one
bit.

\textbf{Kaggle and HuggingFace.} \textbf{Nothing has launched.}
\emph{Nobody who wrote these files ran the \texttt{kaggle} CLI, pushed a
kernel, or touched \texttt{\textasciitilde{}/.kaggle}; launch is the
author's own call.} The record has already refused a relayed instruction
as that consent --- \emph{a coordinator message is not that say-so} ---
and the author's standing instruction is the same rule from the other
side. Gate 0 closes only at open-rulings zero and carries a circularity
the register does not resolve: two of its rows are blocked on numbers
only the Kaggle run produces. What exists: \texttt{ceq/hf/} with four
modules; \textbf{no trained checkpoint} (the Q3 checkpoint slot reads
NOT MEASURED); parameter counts that are not equal (\(25{,}736{,}232\)
against \(25{,}728{,}000\), \(+8{,}232=+0.03200\%\), the arm carrying
more); a model card whose own header states the shipped operator does
not work. Three beds are PINNED as generators with sha256 digests,
\texttt{enwik8} is PINNED as a sliced attach, and three sources are
UNPINNED --- for which the notebook's hash cell \textbf{prints and then
raises} \texttt{kdata.MissingPin} rather than skipping silently. Every
Kaggle line in §5 is priced and none is launchable by any agent: the
author's explicit yes is a node on the critical path, not a formality
(cards \textbf{S-73}, \textbf{V-22}).

\textbf{Why the campaign could not win on its own beds, and what the
plan changes.} Every bed in the tree asks for one real at position
\(s-1\): \texttt{equilibrium\_oracle} returns \(z^\star_{s-1}\),
\emph{the last coordinate of \((I-A)^{-1}b\)}
(\texttt{READ\ scale/negation\_scope.py:286-304}), and
\texttt{ArmSMPrime.forward} returns a tensor of shape \([n]\). That is
the single-location regression shape on which one attention layer is
asymptotically Bayes-optimal (\citep{marion-2025-single-location},
\texttt{CITED\ {[}V{]}}; \citep{duranthon-2026-softmax-advantage} for
softmax proper). The record files this as \textbf{D-1} and carries its
own caveat beside it: the theorem is asymptotic under \(d\to\infty\)
with \(L=o(d)\), its predictor is \texttt{erf} and not softmax, and the
repository ran \(L/d=4.00\) --- \emph{``provably'' is not earned at this
geometry}. So D-1 is not a theorem the campaign lost to; it is the
reason the campaign could not distinguish \emph{cannot be beaten} from
\emph{was not beaten}, and 0 of 39 scoreboard items were earned under
it. The arena confirms it from the metric's side: Q6 is F4 on both wings
because the arms return one real per draw and no state axis exists for a
state metric to read. What the programme changes is the \textbf{label
class}, not the effort. No milestone in §5 scores a scalar at one
position: the labels are the jointly determined configuration
\(z^\star\), the displacement field \(\Delta z\) under
\(\mathrm{do}(a)\), the \(K+1\) reach-avoid committors, and the argmin
over candidate moves --- each with its own information floor (L-FLOOR),
its own zero-hop guard (\textbf{S-14}), its own leak guard firing both
ways (\textbf{S-15}), and its own VOID-contrast list registered before a
cell runs (\textbf{S-13}). Three of those cost \(0\) GPU-s. Whether the
shape is worth anything is not settled by this section and is not
settled by this paper; it is settled by cards \textbf{S-12},
\textbf{S-52} and \textbf{S-62}, in that order, and each of them can
kill it.

\subsection{5.0 The shape, and the claim the programme can at best
earn}\label{the-shape-and-the-claim-the-programme-can-at-best-earn}

\textbf{The shape, in one sentence.} Consequence-Equilibrium Attention
is one causal, row-stochastic attention operator read through a single
triangular solve,
\[z=(I-\gamma W)^{-1}V,\qquad \Pi_\gamma=(1-\gamma)\,W(I-\gamma W)^{-1},\qquad O=\Pi_\gamma V,\]
over the record's Lean-checked three-corner base
\(W_{\beta,qk,m,\theta}\) (\texttt{READ\ lean/CEQ/V16Domain.lean:92-97},
\texttt{READ\ ceq/arm\_smprime.py:144}), with a declared value-zero sink
set \(\mathcal{A}_{\rm sink}=\{0\}\), a goal set \(\mathcal{A}_0\) and
\(K\ge 2\) constraint sets \(\mathcal{A}_1,\dots,\mathcal{A}_K\) written
as identity rows, so that one solve returns the jointly determined state
\(z\), its displacement \(\Delta z\) under an intervention, and the
reach-avoid committor \(q^{(\bullet)}\) of every transient position; at
\(\gamma=0\) the read is softmax bitwise (\texttt{RUN{[}coord{]}}
\texttt{torch.equal} \texttt{True} at \(s=64\), \(d=16\); rejection
region \(\max|O(0.5)-O(0)|=2.3002850040264393\)).

\textbf{The claim the programme can at best earn, in one sentence.} On
BED-S --- a registered bed whose environment chain is a random DAG in
token order, hence inside the arm's own operator class --- CEQ reads the
reach-avoid vector below the exact restricted-view floor and the
displacement field above depth-1 softmax's field cosine at matched depth
and parameters, with an exact certificate (\(\delta=0\) on the solve
route), a trained \(\hat\gamma\) that is MOVED under the boundary null
\(\tfrac12\chi^2_0+\tfrac12\chi^2_1\) (95 per cent point \(2.7055\),
\texttt{RUN{[}J{]}}), and a separation from ChaCAL-with-a-sink-token
that a column device does not reproduce --- and it can never earn
``beats softmax'' on any bed where a deeper softmax stack is a fellow
approximator that computes the same resolvent by iteration
(\citep{wang-2024-incontext-td}, \citep{xie-2026-softmax-rl}
\texttt{{[}V{]}}; R-SKY, \texttt{READ\ CEQ\_V16\_CONTRACT.md:209}).

Everything the programme can lose is written down beside what it can
win: §5.10's outcome trees name the sentence each ending licenses and
the replacement route it owes.

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\subsection{5.1 How to use this plan}\label{how-to-use-this-plan}

\textbf{The phases and their gates.} Seven phases. Each ends in a
decision the author takes alone, from printed numbers, without an agent.

\textbf{DAG conventions.} Every card names its \textbf{prerequisites}
and what it is \textbf{independent-of} (\texttt{CONTRACT.md} D-1: work
is a DAG; parallel dispatch only on nodes with no shared repository
state). A card marked \textbf{parallel-safe} touches no file another
live card touches and may be picked up in any order the prerequisites
allow; \texttt{results/*.jsonl} are append-only per lane (L-G2), so two
cards writing different lanes are parallel-safe, two cards writing one
lane are not. A card's \textbf{price} is tagged \texttt{{[}FITTED{]}}
with its R-squared, \texttt{{[}RUN{]}}, \texttt{{[}ASSUMED{]}} with its
reason, \texttt{DERIVED} with its arithmetic, or \texttt{NOT\ MEASURED}
with the instrument it needs. \textbf{Evenings} are the author's time,
never GPU time. \texttt{★} marks a cheapest-decisive card. Aliases on a
header line name the planets' own card ids for the same content, so a
reader of one roadmap can find its card here.

\textbf{The five cheapest decisive cards.} Each settles something alone,
at \(0\) GPU-s or seconds, and none blocks another (the coordinator's
order, \texttt{judge/bind\_ledger.md} §7):

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{J-L18} --- the ten \texttt{{[}M{]}} Lean targets build with
  their refusals and \texttt{\#print\ axioms} (\(0\) GPU-s). Decides
  whether the paper may write ``machine-checked'' for component (k).
\item
  \textbf{S-01} --- the extended identity manifest with its one-unit
  drift plant (\(0\) GPU-s). Decides whether any cell in the lane can be
  attributed at all (L-2: \texttt{0\ of\ 24} attributable,
  \texttt{READ\ V20\_R15\_LEAP\_LEDGER.md:23}).
\item
  \textbf{S-12} --- the BED-S domain census on 512 draws (\(0\) GPU-s).
  Decides admission, which is the record's dominant failure shape made
  free (D-4, M-3, V-8).
\item
  \textbf{S-20} --- the ChaCAL-diag smoke test at \(\gamma=0.9\) on
  BED-M with the B-J plant battery (\(\approx 1.7\) s). Decides whether
  the solve path runs, is deterministic, and prices as predicted.
\item
  \textbf{S-61} --- the \(\gamma\)-pinning likelihood-ratio instrument
  specified with the boundary null, the held-out set and the seed rule
  (\(0\) GPU-s). Without it Bet C cannot be scored.
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{FIRST EVENING --- five cards that each settle something at \(0\)
GPU-s or seconds.} \texttt{J-L18} (with \texttt{J-L0}, \texttt{J-L1},
\texttt{J-L3} written the same sitting), \texttt{S-01}, \texttt{S-12}
(with \texttt{S-11} written first), \texttt{S-20}, \texttt{S-61}. A
sixth, independent of all five and long owed, is \textbf{S-52}: the
\(\approx 6\) GPU-s capped run at seeds 2, 3, 7 on BED-M that the record
priced at it.7, it.8, it.9 and it.35 and never took
(\texttt{READ\ V20\_R15\_IT35\_JUPITER.md:157-159}). The evening's total
GPU cost is under \(20\) seconds.

\textbf{How to read a KILL.} A KILL line is the point estimate, not the
tail. The record's only scored prediction census reads 9 checked, 9
adverse, 7 of 8 signed rows optimistic, one-sided sign test \(p=0.0352\)
(\texttt{READ\ V16\_CALIBRATION.md:96-100}), so D-CALIB-1 makes the
counter the planning estimate for every unscored card. That rule is why
the plan is ordered cheapest-refutation-first and why every phase can
end honestly.

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

One line each, with its source line (\texttt{sections/sec\_state.md}
§S.4), and the mechanism each pays for. These bind every card below.

and regenerated in-notebook; the hash assertion is the provenance &

\texttt{7.996\ GiB} & \texttt{READ\ V17K\_RULINGS.md:328-339} & P-8 \\

no equality statement without its floor printed beside it &
\texttt{READ\ V17K\_RULINGS.md:354-379} & L-FLOOR \\
\textbf{Ruling 10-prime} & ``pinned'' by likelihood ratio on held-out,
with the minimum detectable departure printed; supersedes the ulp
criterion & \texttt{READ\ V17K\_RULINGS.md:389-436} & M-20 \\
\textbf{the R15 kills} & named-not-found is struck; a mask without a
certificate is refused; a capability number without its floor is not a
number; a leap output acted on before its instance runs is struck &
\texttt{READ\ CEQ\_V20\_R15\_CONTRACT.md:265-273} & §5.9 \\

}

\textbf{Two laws thinner than their citation, recorded so the plan does
not lean on them.} \texttt{L-GRADE} (the F0-F4 rubric) is cited once and
no rubric text exists in the tree
(\texttt{READ\ V20\_R15\_THEORY\_TABLE.md:22-24}); Rulings 11 and 12 are
cited as binding and have no text (\texttt{RUN\ grep}, zero hits outside
the two citing lines). This plan states the grading it uses and marks it
a reconstruction (P-11).

\textbf{The author's supersession of 2026-09-03.} On that date the
author licensed a new primitive --- ``rethink the shape'' --- and
instructed ``do not write any code only docs''
(\texttt{READ\ BRIEF.md:11-30,\ :40-42}). The round-15 contract's clause
\emph{no new primitive that is not a repair} is therefore
\textbf{superseded by the author for this document}, and this plan says
so here rather than by editing the contract, which is verbatim-of-record
and is never edited (\texttt{READ\ CEQ\_V20\_R15\_CONTRACT.md:3-6}).
What the supersession does \textbf{not} lift, because the author did not
lift it: the leap's remaining clauses (named theorems with runnable
instances, predictions \textbf{and} counters, every untested claim with
its cheapest killer, instances RUN before anything is acted on) and
every standing law in the table above. What it changes in kind: the
iteration count of this programme is derived from its own DAG's critical
path (§5.4), never inherited from the 45 iterations the R15 schedule
named; and the Phase-E template --- candidate built with identity binds,
manifests, certificates, parameter-matched, full arena, verdict by the
registered clauses, purge, prognosis
(\texttt{READ\ CEQ\_V20\_R15\_CONTRACT.md:153-169}) --- is inherited
with the beds and labels replaced. Mechanism: P-3 (a stale claim never
retracted) --- the supersession is dated, and §5.11's corrections index
is where it is amended if the author changes it.

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\subsection{5.3 The phases and their milestone
cards}\label{the-phases-and-their-milestone-cards}

Card format, every field on every card: \textbf{id · title} --- phase ·
prerequisites · independent-of · parallel-safe · aliases; then
\emph{build} (a specification, never code), \emph{prove},
\emph{measure}, \emph{PASS}, \emph{KILL} with what dies, \emph{price},
\emph{mechanism}, \emph{deliverable}, \emph{evenings}.

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\subsubsection{PHASE 0 --- free work: theorems, manifests,
specifications,
censuses}\label{phase-0-free-work-theorems-manifests-specifications-censuses}

\textbf{Goal.} Put every identity the paper leans on into Lean or into a
written derivation, give the lane an identity manifest that refuses an
undeclared field, and freeze the specifications and thresholds later
phases may not move (M-2). Price of the whole phase: \(0\) GPU-s.

\textbf{The gate.} L-LEAN. \texttt{lake\ build} from \texttt{lean/}
exits \texttt{0}, zero \texttt{sorry}, zero \texttt{sorryAx}, axioms
\texttt{{[}propext,\ Classical.choice,\ Quot.sound{]}} only, every
\texttt{{[}M{]}} card green; a target that does not build is
\texttt{{[}S{]}} and is never cited as proved (K-K). The manifest's
\texttt{5/5} single-field flips move \texttt{manifest\_hash} and an
omitted declared field raises. Every obstruction sentence carries its
vacuity inequality: \(h\,m\,p=512\ge64\), \(n\log_2 n=384<544\),
\(64^{1/16}=1.2968\), all vacuous at \(s=64\), \(d=16\), \(p=32\). The
author decides from three printed things: the build log, the flip count,
the census table. \textbf{No arm trains until this gate is green.}

\textbf{J-L0 ★ · Lay the \texttt{Shape} module and run the Mathlib name
census} --- phase 0 · prereq none · independent-of every card ·
parallel-safe · aliases none \emph{build} one import root
\texttt{lean/CEQ/Shape.lean} over \texttt{CEQ.Contraction},
\texttt{Occupancy}, \texttt{Nilpotent}, \texttt{V16Domain},
\texttt{OracleSeparation}, with files
\texttt{Shape/\{Parity,Triangular,Boundary,Segment,Displacement,Neumann,Committor\}.lean},
none above the \texttt{V15Source.lean} scale (201 lines, 7
declarations); shared definitions stated once --- \texttt{LowerTri},
\texttt{Absorbing\ P\ A}, \texttt{IsSink\ 0}; journal fields per card:
declaration, \texttt{file:line}, grade, \texttt{\#print\ axioms},
\texttt{lake\ build} exit, Mathlib rev, census line. \emph{prove}
nothing; the census is a \texttt{grep} over the vendored tree at rev
\texttt{a45ae637} (2024-04-04, toolchain
\texttt{leanprover/lean4:v4.7.0}, \texttt{READ\ lean/lean-toolchain})
for the eight Mathlib names the \texttt{{[}M{]}} cards rest on, each
recorded \texttt{{[}V-name{]}} with \texttt{file:line} or
\texttt{{[}U{]}}. \emph{measure} \texttt{lake\ build} exit \texttt{0} on
the empty module (the warm cache reads \texttt{{[}1530/1531{]}},
\texttt{READ\ workdonenewseal.md:41-45}). \emph{PASS} all eight names
resolve at the pinned rev (they do, \texttt{plan\_jupiter.md} §0).
\emph{KILL} a missing name demotes its dependent card to
\texttt{{[}S{]}}, which must then supply the lemma itself; what dies is
the \texttt{{[}M{]}} grade, never the identity. \emph{price} \(0\) GPU-s
· \emph{mechanism} P-11, P-4 · \emph{deliverable}
\texttt{docs/LEAN\_SHAPE\_MANIFEST.md} · \emph{evenings} 1

\textbf{J-L1 ★ · Parity at \(\gamma=0\), with its refusal} --- phase 0 ·
prereq J-L0 · independent-of J-L2 to J-L17 · parallel-safe \emph{build}
\texttt{gamma\_zero\_is\_softmax} (\(P\,(1-0\cdot P)^{-1}V=PV\)) and the
witness \texttt{gamma\_half\_is\_not\_softmax} on \texttt{Fin\ 2}.
\emph{prove} \texttt{{[}M{]}}; \texttt{Matrix.inv\_one},
\texttt{zero\_smul}, \texttt{sub\_zero}; the witness by \texttt{decide}
or \texttt{norm\_num}. \emph{measure} the float instance stays RUN:
\texttt{torch.equal(O(0),\ PV)\ =\ True} at \(s=64\), \(d=16\)
(\texttt{RUN{[}coord{]}}); rejection region \(2.3002850040264393\) at
\(\gamma=0.5\); the record's own softmax corner is
\(1.110223\times10^{-16}\) off \texttt{ceq/lm.py} on \texttt{19/64}
entries (\texttt{READ\ V16\_ARM\_SMPRIME.md:266-293}), so ``bitwise'' is
against the lane's \texttt{softmaxAttn}, never a fused kernel.
\emph{PASS} both declarations build with the three-axiom set.
\emph{KILL} the witness does not close: the parity proposition ships
RUN-only and Proposition 1 loses its Lean rejection region. \emph{price}
\(0\) GPU-s · \emph{mechanism} V-3 (the pass is by construction, so the
refusal is mandatory), V-24 · \emph{deliverable}
\texttt{lean/CEQ/Shape/Parity.lean} · \emph{evenings} 1

\textbf{J-L2 · Row 0 of a causal softmax is absorbing at \(\beta=1\)}
--- phase 0 · prereq J-L0 · independent-of all but J-L10, J-L18 ·
parallel-safe \emph{build} on the tree's \texttt{Hop}
(\texttt{READ\ V16Domain.lean:366-378}):
\texttt{bos\_row\_is\_absorbing}, refusal
\texttt{bos\_row\_not\_absorbing\_at\_beta\_zero} (at \(\beta=0\) row 0
reads \(e^{qk_{00}}=2.0138\), \texttt{RUN{[}M{]}}). \emph{prove}
\texttt{{[}M{]}}; \texttt{Finset.sum\_range\_one},
\texttt{Real.exp\_ne\_zero}, \texttt{Real.rpow\_one},
\texttt{div\_self}. This ships fact F1 of
\texttt{THESIS\_CORRECTIONS\_2.md} §1 as a theorem. \emph{measure} BOS
undeclared gives \(\rho(Q)=1.000000\), \(\det(I-Q)=0.0\); declared as
\(\mathcal{A}_{\rm sink}=\{0\}\) gives \(\rho(Q)=0.692660\)
(\texttt{RUN{[}P{]}}, \texttt{RUN{[}J{]}}). \emph{PASS} builds, and the
domain census reads \(100\) per cent of BED-S draws with
\(0\in\mathcal{A}_{\rm sink}\). \emph{KILL} the theorem needs
\(0<Z_{\rm norm}\) as an extra hypothesis the tree does not supply:
grade \texttt{{[}S{]}} and census line X-2 stays a per-batch RUN.
\emph{price} \(0\) GPU-s · \emph{mechanism} V-25, V-12 ·
\emph{deliverable} \texttt{lean/CEQ/Shape/Boundary.lean} ·
\emph{evenings} 1

\textbf{J-L3 ★ · \(I-\gamma P\) is a unit on the causal class, with the
diagonal interval} --- phase 0 · prereq J-L0 · independent-of J-L1,
J-L2, J-L5, J-L8, J-L9, J-L11, J-L12 · parallel-safe \emph{build}
\texttt{lower\_triangular\_isUnit}, \texttt{diag\_one\_sub\_smul\_pos}
(diagonal of \(1-\gamma P\) in \([1-\gamma,1]\); the open upper bound
needs \(0<P_{ii}\), which J-L5 supplies), refusal
\texttt{zero\_diag\_not\_unit}. \emph{prove} \texttt{{[}M{]}} ---
\texttt{Matrix.det\_of\_lowerTriangular} (\texttt{Block.lean:265}) and
\texttt{Matrix.isUnit\_iff\_isUnit\_det}
(\texttt{NonsingularInverse.lean:151}), both confirmed at the pinned
rev. \emph{measure} minimum diagonal \(0.4\) at \(\gamma=0.6\), attained
by row 0 and every absorbing row (\texttt{RUN{[}M{]}});
\texttt{solve\_triangular} against the dense inverse
\(1.7763568394002505\times10^{-15}\) (\texttt{RUN{[}coord{]}}).
\emph{PASS} builds. \emph{KILL} the \texttt{LinearOrder\ (Fin\ n)}
instance clashes: prove the determinant by induction, grade
\texttt{{[}S{]}}, plus one evening; the triangular branch stalls one
evening and the substitution stands as RUN. \emph{price} \(0\) GPU-s ·
\emph{mechanism} M-8 (the shape is priced as a substitution; this
theorem is what makes the substitution well-defined) ·
\emph{deliverable} \texttt{lean/CEQ/Shape/Triangular.lean} ·
\emph{evenings} 1

\textbf{J-L4 · Consequences propagate forward only} --- phase 0 · prereq
J-L3 · independent-of J-L5 to J-L9, J-L11, J-L12 · parallel-safe
\emph{build} \texttt{inv\_lowerTri},
\texttt{later\_boundary\_unreachable} (F2: a set after the query has

(\texttt{RUN{[}M{]}}). \emph{PASS} builds. \emph{KILL} the

\textbf{J-L8 ★ · The displacement identity with its planted zero} ---

\textbf{J-D3 · Derive the \(E[\gamma^\tau]\) identity without a
probability space} --- phase 0 · prereq J-L10's statement ·

\(\mathcal{A}_k\) absorbing,
\((P^t\mathbf 1_{\mathcal{A}_k})_i=\sum_{u\le t}f_i(u)\) with
\(f_i(u)\ge0\) the first-passage mass at step \(u\); exchanging sums
gives \((1-\gamma)z_i=\sum_u\gamma^u f_i(u)\), the read is
\(O_i=\sum_u\gamma^{u-1}f_i(u)\) by first-step analysis, and
\(\lim_{\gamma\uparrow1}O_i=q^{(k)}_i\) by monotone convergence of a
non-negative series. The gap line:
\(q_i-E_i[\gamma^\tau\mathbf 1_k]=E_i[(1-\gamma^\tau)\mathbf 1_k]\ge(1-\gamma)q_i\),
small where \(q_i\) is. At \(i\in\mathcal{A}_k\) the read is \(1\), not
\(\gamma^{-1}\). \emph{prove} DERIVED; this overrules
\texttt{refute\_theory\_math} item 8's universal \(\ge1-\gamma\) gap.

\textbf{J-D4 · The DET-class statement, and what ``depth-1'' means} ---
phase 0 · prereq none · independent-of all · parallel-safe \emph{build}
the displayed statement: the exact committor solves
\((I-Q)q=R\mathbf 1\), the exact \(z\) is \((I-\gamma P)^{-1}V\);
inversion and iterated product are DET-complete with
\(\mathrm{NL}\subseteq\mathrm{DET}\subseteq\mathrm{NC}^2\)
(\citep{cook-1985-taxonomy} \texttt{{[}V{]}}); a triangular inverse is
DET-hard (J-L11's \texttt{subdiag\_pow\_entry} is that embedding with
scalar blocks); rational linear \textbf{equalities} are in DET and the
P-complete problem is linear \textbf{inequalities}. So the shape
relocates the log-depth from the parameter stack into the solve:
\(O(s)\) sequential rounds by substitution, or recursive block inversion
at \(O(\log^2 s)\) circuit depth and \(O(\log s)\) matmul rounds with
\(O(s^3)\)-class work ---
\texttt{NOT\ MEASURED,\ needs\ a\ parallel-prefix\ kernel\ timing}.
``Depth-1'' means one attention parameter set, \((P,\gamma)\); the
circuit-depth reading is disclaimed in the same paragraph. Beside it the
skyline facts and their vacuity inequalities. \emph{prove} DERIVED.
\emph{measure} \(64^{1/16}=1.2968\) against \(Hdp=512\); \(512\ge64\);
\(384<544\). \emph{PASS} every obstruction sentence carries its theorem
number, model class and inequality (census X-15). \emph{KILL} (K-10) any
``cannot'' sentence outside the skyline table is struck at assembly.

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\subsubsection{PHASE 1 --- BED-S: specification, census,
admission}\label{phase-1-bed-s-specification-census-admission}

\textbf{Goal.} Build the one bed on which the shape's three capabilities
have a rejection region, and decide from a printed census whether it is
admissible --- before a GPU-second is spent. The record's dominant
failure is a bed that dies at construction (D-4, M-3, V-8); this phase
makes that death free.

\textbf{The gate (D-4 admission).} The author reads one census block and
admits or reroutes. Admit requires, on 512 draws at the design point
\(t^\star=8\), \(m=8\), \(K=2\), \(s=64\): row sums within \(10^{-12}\)
of one including absorbing rows; \(0\in\mathcal{A}_{\rm sink}\) on
\(100\) per cent; \(\rho(\hat Q)=\max_{i\in T}\hat P_{ii}<1\) on \(100\)
per cent; every boundary set before the query and the query in \(T\) on
\(100\) per cent; every per-coordinate label sd over the
\textbf{admitted query region} above \(0.05\); every argmin class
frequency in \((0.05,0.95)\); rule-disagreement fraction above zero; the
sink share printed apart from the goal share; the discard count printed.
Any \(\mathrm{sd}=0\), any class outside the band, disagreement zero, or
\(t^\star\) unplaceable inside the causal window kills admission (K-E2,
K-P): no BED-S reading exists, the reroute is the jittered
\texttt{bed\_1} landscape at \(K=2\) with \(B\) as goal used as an
\textbf{oracle cross-check only}, and \(N\) is repriced from whatever
realised sd the first admitted batch shows. Price of the whole phase:
\(0\) GPU-s, except S-14's single zero-step forward.

\textbf{S-10 · Register the placement convention: BOS is a value-zero
sink set, boundary sets precede the query} --- phase 1 · prereq none ·
independent-of S-01 to S-03, J-\emph{, M-0.3 · parallel-safe }build* the
two construction facts as a written convention. \textbf{F1}: row 0 of
any causal softmax at \(\beta=1\) is \(e_0\), so position 0 is absorbing
whether or not it is declared; undeclared, the transient block is
singular. \textbf{F2}: walks descend, so a boundary set after the query
is unreachable. BED-S therefore declares
\(\mathcal{A}_{\rm sink}=\{0\}\) with value \(0\) on every indicator
channel --- \textbf{not} a goal member, since with \(0\in\mathcal{A}_0\)
``reach the goal'' collapses to ``descend to 0 without hitting a
constraint'' --- and places the sink, \(\mathcal{A}_0\) and
\(\mathcal{A}_1..\mathcal{A}_K\) all before the query, the query in
\(T\). \emph{prove} both facts are theorem instances once J-L2 and J-L4
build; until then they are RUN. \emph{measure} with BOS undeclared,
\(\rho(Q)=1.000000\) and \(\det(I-Q)=0.0\) --- the solve must raise;
with \(\mathcal{A}_{\rm sink}=\{0\}\) declared, \(\rho(Q)=0.692660\),
the conservation row holds to \(4.4\times10^{-16}\) and the sink share
reads \([0.362, 1.000]\) on that draw; a set at position 20 read from
query 12 gives \(q=0.0\) exactly (discard), from query 25 gives
\(q=0.1033\) (\texttt{RUN{[}SATURN{]}}). \emph{PASS} the four readings
reproduce on the bed's own generator at \(s=64\). \emph{KILL} a
declared-sink draw with \(\rho(Q)=1\): the generator has a second
undeclared absorbing row (a zero gate at \(c\), J-L7) and census line 14
must be re-read before admission. \emph{price} \(0\) GPU-s ·
\emph{mechanism} V-25, V-12, V-8, D-3 · \emph{deliverable}
\texttt{docs/beds/BED\_S\_PLACEMENT.md} · \emph{evenings} 0.5

\textbf{S-11 · Write the BED-S generator specification} --- phase 1 ·
prereq S-10 · independent-of S-01 to S-03, S-50 to S-53, every J- card ·
parallel-safe · aliases M-1.1, R-04 (spec half), V-3 (spec half), ledger
S-1 \emph{build} the environment is a random directed graph on the \(s\)
token positions whose edges point to \textbf{earlier} positions --- a
DAG in token order, so a walk from the query descends as the arm's
causal \(\hat P\) does --- with self-loops only on declared absorbing
positions and \(P_{\rm env}\) row-stochastic including them. Node tokens
carry the out-adjacency as a multi-hot feature, the membership flags of
the absorbing sets, and \textbf{nothing derived from any solve}; the
transition matrix is a deterministic function of the edge tokens, so
leak clause (a) is dropped for BED-S and clause (c) is kept as the kill.
Candidate moves are \(m\) \textbf{query-side row clamps}
\(P_{\rm env}[v_a,:]\leftarrow e_{u_a}\), each rank one with
\(u^\top\mathbf 1=0\) (the graph-surgery precedent,
\texttt{READ\ scale/negation\_scope.py:718-732}); a token rewrite is
rank \(s-i\), a different object priced as a suffix re-solve. Label per
move: the reach-avoid tensor \((q^{({\rm sink})},q^{(0)},q^{(1..K)})\)
at \(\gamma=1\) on the transient block, the full vector on \(T\)
retained for the residual; \(a^\star=\arg\max_a q^{(0)}\), the Chebyshev
\(\arg\min_a\max_{k\ge1}q^{(k)}\) and the lexicographic form as
\textbf{columns}; \(z^\star\) and \(\Delta z(a)\) by the displacement
identity on \(P_{\rm env}\) at a bed constant \(\gamma_{\rm env}\).
Dials with registered supports: \(t^\star\in\{2,8,32\}\) (a
\textbf{tolerance} dial read off the hop ladder, never predicted from a
spectrum), \(K\in\{2,3,4\}\) (\(K=1\) is illegal: the committor is
constant to \(1.11\times10^{-14}\)), \(m\in\{4,8,16\}\) default \(8\),
\(|\mathcal{A}_\bullet|\in\{1,2\}\), \(s\in\{64,256,1024,4096\}\).
Oracle: the Dirichlet solve of \texttt{bed\_1.committor}'s form
(\texttt{READ\ ceq/beds/bed\_1.py:188-198}) on \(P_{\rm env}\), never on
\(\hat P\); the Kirchhoff second route covers \(K=2\) single-node sets
only, the multi-node extension is
\texttt{NOT\ MEASURED,\ needs\ the\ grounded\ Laplacian\ extended}.
\emph{prove} nothing here; the identities are Phase 2's. \emph{measure}
nothing yet; the spec names every field S-01's manifest consumes and
every census line S-12 prints. \emph{PASS} the spec states the oracle
runs on \(P_{\rm env}\) and names the D-4 registration triple on the
same commit. \emph{KILL} any sentence handing the arm \(P_{\rm env}\) as
an input channel other than the edge tokens (D-2), or a spec in which
\(K=1\) is legal (V-12). \emph{price} \(0\) GPU-s · \emph{mechanism}
D-2, V-25, V-12, V-8, D-3, M-8 · \emph{deliverable}
\texttt{docs/beds/BED\_S\_SPEC.md} · \emph{evenings} 2

\textbf{S-12 ★ · Run the BED-S domain census on 512 draws and decide
admission} --- phase 1 · prereq S-11 · independent-of S-01 to S-03, S-50
to S-53, every J- card · parallel-safe · aliases M-1.2, R-04, V-3,
ledger S-2, P1.2 \emph{build} the fifteen census lines of
\texttt{judge/sec\_apparatus.md} §A.7 printed per draw batch as one JSON
block, before any arm is trained: (1) row stochasticity including
absorbing rows; (2) \(0\in\mathcal{A}_{\rm sink}\) on \(100\) per cent;
(3) \(\rho(\hat Q)<1\) as a diagonal read --- a V-10 gate, printed,
never counted as evidence; (4) every set before the query, query in
\(T\); (5) per-coordinate label sd over the \textbf{admitted query
region} (the prefix before the first constraint reads
\(q^{(0)}\approx1\) and would inflate it), sink share printed apart from
\(q^{(0)}\); (6) every argmin class frequency in \((0.05,0.95)\), ties
within \(10^{-9}\) discarded and counted; (7) rule-disagreement fraction
above zero; (8) the corpus-alone probe of S-15; (9) \(I(s_0;a^\star)=0\)
by plug-in; (10) \(\mathrm{Var}(\Delta z)>0\) over coordinates
\(\ge i_{\min}\) with the zero-coordinate fraction printed; (11) the
move census (which moves change \(P\) against \(V\) only;
\(u^\top\mathbf 1=0\) and a non-negative edited row on \(100\) per cent;
the Sherman-Morrison denominator printed as a check); (12) and (13)
filled on trained cells; (14) the exact-zero-gate count (expected
\texttt{0\ of\ N} on the softmax corner, so the F0 theorems are silent);
(15) S-03's lines. \texttt{nrmse} returns \texttt{nan} on a constant
label and \texttt{nan\ \textgreater{}=\ 1.0} is \texttt{False}
(\texttt{READ\ MISTAKES.md:149-153}), so the sd print is not optional.
\emph{prove} nothing; F1 and F2 appear as the two structural lines.
\emph{measure} 512 draws at \(t^\star=8\), \(m=8\), \(K=2\), \(s=64\).
\emph{PASS} every admitted-region sd above \(0.05\); every class
frequency in \((0.05,0.95)\); disagreement above zero; lines 1 to 4 at
\(100\) per cent; the discard fraction printed. \emph{KILL} (K-E2, K-P)
any \(\mathrm{sd}=0\); any class outside the band; disagreement zero
(then only one rule may be named); \(\rho(Q)=1\) on any draw (BOS
undeclared: the solve must raise, V-16); \(t^\star\) unplaceable. The
bed is not admitted, \textbf{no arm runs}, every card from S-14 to S-65
is VOID, and §5.10 tree C is the paper. \emph{price} \(0\) GPU-s; the
oracle is \(m\) factorisations with \(K+2\) right-hand sides,
\(|T|^3/3=5.76\times10^{8}\) MACs at \(|T|=1200\), under a second per
draw in float64 (DERIVED) · \emph{mechanism} V-8, V-12, V-25, V-10, D-3,
D-4, M-3 · \emph{deliverable} \texttt{results/bed\_s\_census.jsonl} and
\texttt{docs/beds/BED\_S\_CENSUS.md} (the admission decision, signed by
the author) · \emph{evenings} 1

\textbf{S-13 · Register the VOID-contrast list and the identification
metric before any cell} --- phase 1 · prereq S-11 · independent-of S-12
· parallel-safe · aliases M-1.3, R-16, ledger S-3 \emph{build} because
the arm's operator class contains the oracle's chain,
\texttt{shape\ -\ softmax} and \texttt{shape\ -\ skyline} on the
committor head are reproduction-versus-non-reproduction contrasts and
are \textbf{VOID as capability numbers}. Creditable:
\texttt{shape\ -\ ChaCAL-diag}, \texttt{shape\ -\ ChaCAL-published},
\texttt{shape\ -\ ChaCAL-with-sink-token},
\texttt{shape\ -\ InfSA-Neumann-16}, \texttt{shape\ -\ 0-hop\ MLP} and
\texttt{shape\ -\ 1-hop\ softmax} on the argmin, and
\(\|\hat P-P_{\rm env}\|_\infty\) per seed as a \textbf{learnability}
reading. The model is E1's registration comment
(\texttt{READ\ scale/negation\_scope.py:1033-1041}). \emph{prove} J-L9
is the D-2 separation that fails on an undirected substrate and holds on
the DAG. \emph{measure} (at the arena) \(\|\hat P-P_{\rm env}\|_\infty\)
per seed, sorted. \emph{PASS} the list is in the manifest field
\texttt{void\_contrasts} on every cell, in a commit dated before the
first arena cell. \emph{KILL} (K-D2)
\(\|\hat P-P_{\rm env}\|_\infty<10^{-3}\) on at least 6 of 8 seeds: the
arm copied the environment, every contrast against a non-reproducing arm
is a copy-versus-no-copy statement, and the reading is filed as

\emph{mechanism} D-2, M-7, V-10 · \emph{deliverable}
\texttt{docs/beds/BED\_S\_VOID\_LIST.md} · \emph{evenings} 0.5

\textbf{S-14 · The zero-hop guard: the query carries \(s_0\) only, and
the untrained arm reads chance} --- phase 1 · prereq S-11 ·
independent-of S-12, S-13 · parallel-safe · aliases M-1.4, ledger P1.3,
X-9 \emph{build} \(s_0\) drawn uniformly from \(T\) independently of
\(a^\star\); the plug-in \(I(s_0;a^\star)\) printed with its sampling
error; the zero-step RED gate applied unchanged --- the untrained arm
within \(1/m\) plus or minus its Clopper-Pearson half-width on the
argmin, committor NRMSE \(\ge1-\mathrm{GATE\_TOL}\) with
\(\mathrm{GATE\_TOL}=10^{-3}\) (\texttt{READ\ COSTS.md:137}). Precedent:
BED-M's first builder leaked \(1/(t^\star+1)\) at zero hops and aborted
three of five rungs; \(b[s-1]=0\) restored it
(\texttt{READ\ scale/negation\_scope.py:399-415}). \emph{prove} nothing.
\emph{measure} must-fire: a query token carrying \(q^{(0)}(a^\star)\)
pushes the untrained read below \(1-\mathrm{GATE\_TOL}\). Must-not-fire:
the honest query reads at chance within the interval. \emph{PASS} both
halves. \emph{KILL} the honest query reads below the gate: the label is
legible at zero hops, the bed is a copy task, and it is struck before
any number. \emph{price} \(\le1.524\) s, one zero-step forward
\texttt{{[}FITTED{]}} · \emph{mechanism} V-10, D-5, V-24 ·
\emph{deliverable} \texttt{results/bed\_s\_zerohop.jsonl} ·
\emph{evenings} 0.5

\textbf{S-15 · The leak guard, firing both ways} --- phase 1 · prereq
S-11 · independent-of S-12 to S-14 · parallel-safe · aliases M-1.5,
R-05, ledger P1.4, X-8 \emph{build} a corpus-alone linear probe from the
token features to \(q\) at order 0 on the admitted draws; the planted
positive is a move token carrying its own \(q\); the planted negative is
the strictly-local feature set E4-prime uses (degree, ball sizes to
radius 5, absorbing-endpoint-in-ball flags,
\texttt{READ\ scale/e4\_harmonic.py:220-244}), whose bars are E4-prime's
and are \textbf{re-derived for BED-S} before use (V-22). \emph{prove}
nothing. \emph{measure} the coefficient of determination on the honest
probe and on the planted leak. \emph{PASS} honest below \(0.5\) and
planted at or above \(0.99\) --- the detector fires when the leak is
planted, calibrated both ways. \emph{KILL} honest at or above \(0.5\):
the one-read claim is void on this bed (leak kill (c)); planted below
\(0.99\): the detector is blind and no leak verdict is admissible.
\emph{price} \(0\) GPU-s (CPU probes) · \emph{mechanism} M-21, V-24,
V-7, V-22, D-2 · \emph{deliverable} \texttt{results/bed\_s\_leak.jsonl}
· \emph{evenings} 1

\textbf{S-16 · Fix the metrics and the floors before any arm exists} ---
phase 1 · prereq S-11 · independent-of S-12 to S-15 · parallel-safe
\emph{build} the committor head scored in the Fisher-Rao coordinate
\(\varphi(p)=2\arcsin\sqrt p\) per entry, position-matched NRMSE on
\(\varphi\) (mean and max), plus the harmonic residual
\(r(\hat q)=\|(I-Q_{\rm env})\hat q-R_{\rm env}\mathbf 1\|_\infty\) ---
a \textbf{score, never a loss} (D-2) --- printed beside
\(\sigma_{\min}(I-\gamma P_{\rm env})\) and
\(\|I-\gamma P_{\rm env}\|_\infty\le1+\gamma\), its units. Argmin
accuracy with Clopper-Pearson (\citep{clopper-1934-binomial}) and
McNemar paired on identical draws (\citep{mcnemar-1947-correlated}). The
\(z\) and \(\Delta z\) channels by the position-matched per-coordinate
NRMSE vector, the field cosine and the magnitude ratio over coordinates
\(\ge i_{\min}\) with the masked fraction printed (an arm emitting
\(\Delta\hat z\equiv0\) makes the cosine undefined and is
\textbf{refused}, not scored, V-16), the sign column kept because
softmax owns it (\(0.807843\), \texttt{READ\ MATHEMATICS.md:396-406}),
McNemar on the sign column only and a paired \(t\) or Wilcoxon on the
cosine with its \textbf{own} realised sd. Floors (L-FLOOR): the exact
oracle at \(0.0\) with the hop-ladder ceilings printed per batch; the
argmin zero-information floor \(1-\max_a\hat\pi(a^\star)\), which at
uniform \(m=8\) is \(0.875\); the tight Fano
\((\ln m-I-\ln2)/\ln(m-1)\), which at \(m=8\) with \(I=0\) reads
\(0.7124\), used \textbf{only} where \(I(X_{\le k};a^\star)>0\) is
computed; the weak Fano \(0.6667\) retired as a floor (it sits \(0.208\)
below chance); \texttt{floor\_1} nowhere. Refused metrics: pooled
first-order Wasserstein (the permuted oracle scores \(0.0\) against
NRMSE \(1.421901\)), Procrustes, position-free optimal transport.
\emph{prove} nothing. \emph{measure} nothing; the file is hashed into
the prediction file of S-60 before the first arena cell. \emph{PASS} the
metric file exists and is hashed before S-62. \emph{KILL} any metric
added or removed after the first arena cell (M-2); that cell's numbers
are then not quotable. \emph{price} \(0\) GPU-s · \emph{mechanism} M-2,
V-26, V-17, L-FLOOR, V-16 · \emph{deliverable}
\texttt{docs/apparatus/METRICS\_AND\_FLOORS.md} · \emph{evenings} 1

\textbf{S-17 · The \(\Delta z\) channel's own census and the
cached-mixture plant classes} --- phase 1 · prereq S-11 · independent-of
S-12 to S-16 · parallel-safe \emph{build} for the \(z\) and \(\Delta z\)
lane --- its \textbf{own} journal, because a vector label voids the

--- the intervened position drawn uniformly;
\(\mathrm{Var}(\Delta z)>0\) per coordinate \(\ge i_{\min}\); per-draw
flags \texttt{move\_changes\_P} against \texttt{move\_changes\_V\_only}

separately; the no-change predictor's error printed beside every
\(\Delta z\) number (\citep{vakalis-2026-interventiongap}
\texttt{{[}V{]}} owns that floor). \emph{prove} nothing. \emph{measure}
the two plant classes' counts. \emph{PASS} both classes non-empty, at
least 64 draws each. \emph{KILL} a corpus with no \(P\)-changing move:
the interventional channel has no rejection region against the cached
mixture and card S-33 cannot be scored. \emph{price} \(0\) GPU-s ·
\emph{mechanism} V-8, D-5, V-24, M-1 · \emph{deliverable}
\texttt{results/bed\_s\_dz\_census.jsonl} · \emph{evenings} 0.5

construction} --- phase 1 · prereq S-11 · independent-of S-13 to S-17 ·
parallel-safe \emph{build} the argmin controls (0-hop per-position MLP
on the candidate's context row, 1-hop softmax, majority, random at
\(1/m\), predict-the-mean) plus R-24's closed-form rule, all registered
before any arm trains; the exact zero-information floor from the
realised class distribution; \(I(X_{\le0};a^\star)\) by plug-in over the
zero-hop view \textbf{including} the move tokens and membership flags.
\emph{prove} nothing. \emph{measure} the untrained arm at \(1/m\) within
its interval; committor NRMSE \(\ge1-\mathrm{GATE\_TOL}\) at step 0.
\emph{PASS} the untrained arm reads chance and \(I(X_{\le0};a^\star)\)
is printed, with the tight Fano used only where it exceeds zero.
\emph{KILL} (K-H2, scored at S-26) the 0-hop MLP within the
Clopper-Pearson half-width of the shape at \(N=8\), or McNemar
\(p>0.05\) against the 1-hop softmax: BED-S is a third static task and
is struck before any number is quoted (C8; the record's static beds gave
an iterating arm nothing to compute toward,
\texttt{READ\ D1.md:286-291}). \emph{price} \(0\) GPU-s at registration;

\textbf{R-07 ★ · Census the sink-escape degeneracy of the Chebyshev rule
and the exact-zero tie it creates} --- phase 1 · prereq S-11 ·
independent-of S-13 to S-17, R-06 · parallel-safe \emph{build} the
attack, filed by no office before: with \(\mathcal{A}_{\rm sink}=\{0\}\)
declared and moves realised as clamps, a clamp into \textbf{any}
absorbing position gives \(\max_{k\ge1}q^{(k)}=0\) exactly, so the
Chebyshev column is minimised equally by every move that sends the walk
to the sink or to the goal --- the safest move under Chebyshev is ``fall
off the prompt'' --- and the \(10^{-9}\) tie rule then discards the
draw, which empties the bed. Two census lines are added to S-12: (i) the
fraction of admitted draws on which \(a^\dagger\) has
\(q^{({\rm sink})}(a^\dagger)>q^{(0)}(a^\dagger)\); (ii) the discard
fraction attributable to exact-zero Chebyshev ties. The construction fix
is registered \textbf{now}, before the number: clamp targets restricted
to transient positions (\(u_a\in T\)) so no move is a direct absorption,
and a third column, the conditioned Chebyshev
\(\arg\min_a\max_{k\ge1}q^{(k)}(a)/(1-q^{({\rm sink})}(a))\), printed
beside the other two. \emph{prove} DERIVED: for \(u\in\mathcal{A}_j\)
the clamp gives \(q^{(j)}(v)=1\) and \(q^{(k)}(v)=0\) for \(k\ne j\)
exactly, so the Chebyshev value of every absorbing-target move is zero
unless \(j\ge1\). \emph{measure} lines (i) and (ii) on 512 draws, with
and without the restriction. On the judge's draw at query 20, over the
20 clamp targets below it, the Chebyshev minimum is \(0.000\), attained
by \texttt{9\ of\ 20} targets, of which 7 carry sink share above \(0.5\)
and 2 goal share above \(0.5\) (\texttt{RUN{[}M{]}}). \emph{PASS} with
the restriction, line (i) below \(0.05\) and line (ii) below \(0.05\).
\emph{KILL} line (i) at or above \(0.5\) on the unrestricted bed: the
Chebyshev column as written is struck and only the goal rule and the
conditioned form are named; line (ii) at or above \(0.5\): the discard
rule slices the bed to nothing and admission is refused until the
restriction is in the spec. \emph{price} \(0\) GPU-s · \emph{mechanism}
V-12 in a new guise, V-8, V-10, V-5 · \emph{deliverable}
\texttt{docs/plan/SINK\_ESCAPE.md} and two columns in
\texttt{results/bed\_s\_census.jsonl} · \emph{evenings} 1

\textbf{R-08 · Count no-op moves and correct the chance floor to the
effective \(m\)} --- phase 1 · prereq S-11 · independent-of R-05 to R-07
· parallel-safe \emph{build} the attack: on a causal \(P_{\rm env}\) a
clamp at a row above the query cannot change any committor at the query,
and a clamp at a row unreachable from \(s_0\) is a no-op for the same
reason --- F2 applied to \textbf{moves}, which no office filed (F2
covers boundary sets). A bed with \(m=8\) of which \(k\) are no-ops has
effective \(m-k\) candidates, so the printed chance floor
\(1-1/m=0.875\) and the tight Fano \(0.7124\) are too \textbf{low}, and
a shape reading above the printed floor may be at chance on the
effective set. The census line: per move,
\(\max_k|q^{(k)}(\mathrm{do}\,a)-q^{(k)}|\) at the query, a move below
\(10^{-12}\) counted a no-op; \(m_{\rm eff}\) per draw; both floors
recomputed at \(m_{\rm eff}\); the spec restricted to clamp rows at or
below \(s_0\) and reachable from it, with the fraction of draws touched
by the restriction printed. \emph{prove} DERIVED from triangularity, and
by J-L4's \texttt{later\_boundary\_unreachable} extended to a clamp row.
\emph{measure} \(m_{\rm eff}\) on 512 draws; clamps at rows 21, 25, 31
move the query's four committors by \(5.551\times10^{-17}\), zero to
rounding (\texttt{RUN{[}M{]}}). \emph{PASS} \(m_{\rm eff}=m\) on at
least 95 per cent of admitted draws after the restriction. \emph{KILL}
\(m_{\rm eff}<m\) on more than 5 per cent of draws in any cell quoting a
floor: every argmin accuracy in that cell is re-floored at
\(m_{\rm eff}\) and this census line becomes an admission line.
\emph{price} \(0\) GPU-s · \emph{mechanism} L-FLOOR, V-10, V-17, D-3 ·
\emph{deliverable} \texttt{docs/plan/EFFECTIVE\_M.md} and one column in
\texttt{results/bed\_s\_census.jsonl} · \emph{evenings} 1

\textbf{R-24 · Register the clamp-target membership rule as a
closed-form zero-hop control} --- phase 1 · prereq S-11 · independent-of
R-05 to R-08; consumed by R-06 and S-62 · parallel-safe \emph{build} the
attack: the goal rule is decided at zero hops whenever some clamp target
lies in the goal set, because a clamp into \(\mathcal{A}_0\) gives
\(q^{(0)}=1\) exactly and the goal flag of \(u_a\) is a token feature.
The registered 0-hop control is a \textbf{trained} MLP; a closed-form
rule R0 --- ``pick the move whose clamp target carries the goal flag;
else the move whose target carries no constraint flag and sits
earliest'' --- costs nothing, needs no seed, and reads its accuracy at
construction. R0 and its two ablations (goal-flag only;
earliest-transient only) are computed as oracle-free columns on every
draw batch, with the fraction of draws on which R0 is defined.
\emph{prove} DERIVED: a clamp into \(\mathcal{A}_0\) yields
\(q^{(0)}=1\) and is the unique argmax unless another move also clamps
into \(\mathcal{A}_0\); on the judge's draw \(q^{(0)}(v)=1.0000\)
exactly for \(u=5\in\mathcal{A}_0\) (\texttt{RUN{[}M{]}}).
\emph{measure} R0 accuracy against the oracle argmin with
Clopper-Pearson on 512 draws, before and after R-07's restriction.
\emph{PASS} R0 accuracy within the interval of \(1/m\) after the
restriction. \emph{KILL} (frozen) R0 accuracy at or above
\(\mathrm{acc}_{\rm shape}-\mathrm{MDE}_8\) on at least 6 of 8 arena
seeds, or R0 above the exact zero-information floor by more than its
interval on the unrestricted bed: the argmin label is a flag lookup,
BED-S's argmin head is struck, and only the committor-vector head
remains. \emph{price} \(0\) GPU-s · \emph{mechanism} V-10, D-5, M-21,
M-18 · \emph{deliverable} \texttt{docs/plan/FLAG\_RULE.md} and three
columns in \texttt{results/bed\_s\_census.jsonl} · \emph{evenings} 1

\textbf{R-20 · File the one-step rule as a safety filter, not a policy,
with the multichain hazard tested on the oracle} --- phase 1 · prereq
S-11 · independent-of R-05 to R-08, R-24 · parallel-safe \emph{build} on
512 admitted draws, the oracle-only comparison of the one-step rule
against the two-step optimum (apply \(a\), re-solve, take the best
second clamp) on the same \(P_{\rm env}\); the vocabulary fixed as a
one-step, most-restrictive \textbf{safety filter} whose value is the
goal committor (\citep{hsu-2023-safetyfilter}, \citep{borquez-2023-lrf}
\texttt{{[}V{]}}), the aggregations as columns
(\citep{vanmoffaert-2013-chebyshev}, \citep{yang-2026-lexisafe}
\texttt{{[}V{]}}), and \citep{misra-2023-safety-constrained-mdp}
\texttt{{[}V{]}} (Bellman's principle can fail for safety-constrained
multichain MDPs) carried in Limits beside the rule. \emph{prove}
DERIVED: on a DAG with declared absorbing sets every transient state is
absorbed with probability one (\(\rho(Q)=\max_T P_{ii}<1\)), so the
chain is absorbing; the hazard lives in the policy-level object, which
the paper does not claim. \emph{measure} the fraction of draws on which
the one-step and two-step optima disagree. \emph{PASS} the paper never
writes ``optimal move'' or ``policy'', and the disagreement fraction is
printed. \emph{KILL} disagreement above \(0.5\) on 512 draws: the phrase
``safest move'' is replaced by ``one-step safety filter'' in every
sentence and the sequential problem is filed as future work with the
citation. \emph{price} \(0\) GPU-s (oracle solves) · \emph{mechanism}
P-7, V-17, P-10 · \emph{deliverable}
\texttt{docs/plan/SAFETY\_FILTER.md},
\texttt{results/bed\_s\_onestep\_vs\_twostep.jsonl} · \emph{evenings} 1

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\subsubsection{PHASE 2 --- the binds through the front door, the smoke
test, the device
certificate}\label{phase-2-the-binds-through-the-front-door-the-smoke-test-the-device-certificate}

\textbf{Goal.} Prove on the bed's \textbf{real draw} that every identity
the paper leans on holds and every planted negative fails at \(O(1)\),
that the solve path runs deterministically at the predicted price, and
that the device certificate has a live producer. Nothing here reads a
capability number.

\textbf{The gate (V-14, V-24).} Every plant of S-20 to S-27 exists as a
FOUND cell under \texttt{results/} with its own \texttt{kind} and
\texttt{manifest\_hash} --- a plant that lives only in a script's print
is NAMED, and the bind is inadmissible. Every honest half passes; every
plant fails at \(O(1)\) with counts printed, in the pattern
\(0.9749 / 0.9165 / 1.000 / 0.4845\)
(\texttt{READ\ workdonenewseal.md:114-122}). The smoke cell reads inside
the law-versus-measured band \([-1.8, +14.4]\) per cent of \(1.680\) s,
or is re-labelled the per-op floor it was declared to be. The author
decides one thing: whether the arena may start.

\textbf{M-2.0 · Build the shape arm as a registered \texttt{kind}} ---
phase 2 · prereq none (building is not training; L-LEAN gates M-2.2, not
this) · independent-of every Phase-1 card · not parallel-safe with S-20
\emph{build} one module exposing the corners \texttt{beta/qk/g}, a
learnable \(\gamma\in[0,1)\) initialised at \(0\),
\texttt{boundary\_sets/goal\_set/sink\_set} as identity rows,
\texttt{diag\_convention}, \texttt{route}, \texttt{committor\_route},
the read \(O=(1-\gamma)P(I-\gamma P)^{-1}V\) with the factor carried
(\citep{fagnou-2024-chacal} Eq. 5 \texttt{{[}V{]}}), and two heads:
(H-q) the exact triangular solve at \(\gamma=1\) on the transient block
and (H-z) the state and displacement channel at \(\hat\gamma\); plus the
\texttt{make\_arm} branch, the optimiser path and the journal emit ---
the W2 lesson was that a three-line branch was the whole gap
(\texttt{READ\ V20\_R15\_WING\_MANIFEST.md:115-123}). \emph{prove}
nothing. \emph{measure} the arm produces a journalled record with its
\texttt{kind} under \texttt{results/} (FOUND, not NAMED). \emph{PASS}
one FOUND record. \emph{KILL} a \texttt{kind} with zero records at the
end of Phase 2 is STRUCK, which is the R15 kill applied to this lane.
\emph{price} \(0\) GPU-s to build; the first record is S-20's ·
\emph{mechanism} FOUND-not-NAMED, P-4, L-2 · \emph{deliverable} the
author's module plus the FOUND line in \texttt{docs/PLAN\_STATUS.md} ·
\emph{evenings} 3

\textbf{S-20 ★ · B-J parity at \(\gamma=0\) and the ChaCAL-diag smoke
test at \(\gamma=0.9\) on BED-M} --- phase 2 · prereq S-01, S-02, M-2.0,
J-L18 rows 1 and 5 (L-LEAN) · independent-of every Phase-1 card · not
parallel-safe with M-2.0 · aliases M-2.2, R-12, V-7, N-05, ledger P2.1,
B-J, B-P5 \emph{build} \texttt{torch.equal(O(0),\ PV)} against the
lane's own \texttt{softmaxAttn}; then one 150-step ChaCAL-diag cell at
fixed \(\gamma=0.9\) (ChaCAL's own setting, \citep{fagnou-2024-chacal}
App. C) on BED-M \texttt{e3\_t2}, \(n=2048\), \(s=64\),
\(d_{\rm model}=16\), under
\texttt{use\_deterministic\_algorithms(True)} with the cuBLAS workspace
exported \textbf{before} process start (set in-process it does not take,
\texttt{READ\ V17\_R4\_RETAKE\_PRICE.md:178-181}), timed with
\texttt{torch.cuda.synchronize()} bracketing. Plants: \(\gamma=0.5\)
(\texttt{RUN{[}coord{]}} \(2.3002850040264393\) at \(s=64\);

regardless of support (\texttt{RUN{[}M{]}}) and is recorded as struck.
\emph{prove} nothing here; Proposition 1 is J-L1's. \emph{measure}

\texttt{4/4} plants at \(O(1)\); the cell inside \([-1.8, +14.4]\) per
cent of \(1.680\) s; repeats bitwise both ways. \emph{KILL}
\texttt{torch.equal} \texttt{False} at \(\gamma=0\) (the containment

backward raising under strict mode (the ``first gated wing whose

\textbf{S-21 · B-E1 boundary rows against ChaCAL-diag} --- phase 2 ·
prereq S-12 admitted, S-20 · independent-of S-22 to S-28 · parallel-safe
· alias ledger P2.2 \emph{build} identity half: with
\(\mathcal{A}=\emptyset\) the shape is \texttt{torch.equal} to
\textbf{ChaCAL-diag}, the lane's own re-implementation with
\texttt{diag\_convention\ =\ kept} declared in the manifest (a declared
V-3). Rejection half: with \(\mathcal{A}\ne\emptyset\),
\(\|\Pi_{\rm shape}-\Pi_{\rm ChaCAL\text{-}diag}\|_\infty\) on rows
downstream of the boundary positions exceeds a printed \(O(1)\) gap ---
a \textbf{weight} statement, never a support statement, since on a dense
causal softmax the support of \(\Pi_\gamma\) equals the support of \(P\)
with or without boundary rows (\texttt{RUN{[}J{]}}, rows changed
\(=[3]\), \texttt{RUN{[}M{]}}). The struck plant --- ``a non-identity
absorbing row breaks the conservation row'' --- is empty
(\texttt{RUN{[}M{]}} \(0.0\); the sum uses transient rows only) and is
replaced by the full-\(P\) read at \(\gamma<1\), where it reads
\(0.1491\). The InfSA base must break the identity half:
\texttt{NOT\ MEASURED,\ needs\ the\ base\ wired}. \emph{prove} nothing.
\emph{measure} the identity half and the downstream gap on every
admitted draw. \emph{PASS} \texttt{torch.equal\ True} at
\(\mathcal{A}=\emptyset\) and a downstream gap of at least \(0.1\) at
\(\mathcal{A}\ne\emptyset\) on \(100\) per cent of admitted draws.
\emph{KILL} the identity half fails (the lane's ChaCAL-diag is not the
shape at \(\mathcal{A}=\emptyset\) --- a P-7 object, and the ``same
operator'' sentence dies); the gap reads below \(10^{-3}\) on any draw
(boundary rows change nothing there; census line 4 is re-read).
\emph{price} seconds · \emph{mechanism} V-24, V-3, V-14, P-7 ·
\emph{deliverable} \texttt{results/binds/b\_e1\_boundary.jsonl} ·
\emph{evenings} 1

S-12 · independent-of S-21, S-23 to S-28 · parallel-safe · alias ledger

(\texttt{RUN{[}SATURN{]}}), \([0.9999999999999993, 1.0]\)

V-16, V-3 · \emph{deliverable}
\texttt{results/binds/b\_e2\_conservation.jsonl} · \emph{evenings} 0.5

(the record's own factor was undefined at all eight R1 seeds,
