\subsection{A.1 Beds}\label{a.1-beds}

Column conventions. \emph{Label shape} is what the arm emits.
\emph{Oracle} is what computes the label and \textbf{where it runs} ---
always on the latent environment chain the bed builds, never on the
arm's own \texttt{P̂} (\texttt{READ\ MISTAKES.md:710-725}, D-2).
\emph{Placement} is the F1/F2 convention of
\texttt{THESIS\_CORRECTIONS\_2.md} §1. \emph{Role} says what a reading
on the bed may say about the shape.

\textbf{BED-S specification (the one sentence the refuters asked for,
\texttt{refute\_instrument\_occvac} §2.1 and
\texttt{refute\_theory\_occvac} §2.4).} The environment is a random
directed graph on the \texttt{s} token positions whose edges point to
\emph{earlier} positions (a DAG in token order, so that a walk from the
query descends, exactly as the arm's causal \texttt{P̂} does ---
\texttt{RUN}: with every declared set absorbing the transient block
\texttt{Q} is lower-triangular,
\texttt{ρ(Q)\ =\ max\_\{i∈T\}\ P\_ii\ =\ 0.692660}), with self-loops
only on the declared absorbing positions. Node tokens carry the node's
out-adjacency as a multi-hot feature (so that a depth-1 bilinear logit
can realise \texttt{P̂\ =\ P\_env} at \texttt{d\_model\ ≥\ n\_nodes} ---
a representability claim that is \texttt{NOT\ MEASURED}), the membership
flags of the absorbing sets, and nothing derived from any solve.
Candidate moves are \texttt{m} \textbf{query-side row clamps}
\texttt{P\_env{[}v\_a,\ :{]}\ ←\ e\_\{u\_a\}} (the graph-surgery
precedent of \texttt{READ\ scale/negation\_scope.py:718-732}), each a
rank-one change of \texttt{P\_env} (\texttt{refute\_theory\_math} §1.6:
a \emph{token} rewrite would change every row \texttt{≥\ i} and is not
rank-one; the bed therefore defines \texttt{do(a)} as the clamp). The
transition matrix \texttt{P\_env} is a deterministic function of the
edge tokens by construction; \texttt{sec\_refuted.md} C2's leak clause
(a) is therefore \textbf{dropped} for BED-S (the graph is the input),
and clause (c) --- \texttt{R²\ ≥\ 0.5} on \texttt{q} at order 0 from a
corpus-alone probe --- is kept as the leak kill. Design against V-25
(the mechanism's domain is non-empty), D-2 (the oracle's chain and the
arm's class now overlap, so the D-2 kill
\texttt{‖P̂\ −\ P\_env‖\_∞\ \textless{}\ 1e-3} on \texttt{≥\ 6/8} seeds
has a rejection region and is registered as a \emph{learnability}
reading, not a capability), V-12 (\texttt{K\ ≥\ 2} and a goal set), V-8
(sink share printed).

\textbf{The VOID list for BED-S, registered before any cell (D-2, M-7).}
Because the arm's class contains the oracle's operator,
\texttt{shape\ −\ softmax} and \texttt{shape\ −\ skyline} on the
committor head are reproduction-versus-non-reproduction contrasts and
are VOID as capability numbers. Creditable contrasts:
\texttt{shape\ −\ ChaCAL-diag} and \texttt{shape\ −\ ChaCAL-published}
(the boundary-row mechanism), \texttt{shape\ −\ ChaCAL-with-sink-token}
(the C2 kill), \texttt{shape\ −\ InfSA-style\ Neumann-K} (exactness),
\texttt{shape\ −\ 0-hop\ MLP} and \texttt{shape\ −\ 1-hop\ softmax} on
the argmin (the C8 static-task kill), and the identification reading
\texttt{‖P̂\ −\ P\_env‖\_∞} per seed.

\textbf{Why BOS is a sink set and not a goal member.} With
\texttt{0\ ∈\ 𝒜\_0} every un-absorbed walk ends at BOS and ``reach the
goal'' collapses to ``descend to 0 without hitting a constraint''
(\texttt{refute\_falsify\_math} §3.11); the masked-diagonal route is
exact only when the BOS value is \texttt{0}
(\texttt{refute\_theory\_math} item 22). A separate value-zero sink set
\texttt{𝒜\_sink\ =\ \{0\}} (the leak state of \citep{roffo-2026-infsa}
\texttt{{[}V{]}}, the anchor of \citep{ranmilo-2026-attentionsinks}
\texttt{{[}V{]}}) keeps \texttt{ρ(Q)\ \textless{}\ 1} (F1), keeps the
goal a genuine target, and closes the conservation row: \texttt{RUN}
with \texttt{𝒜\_sink\ =\ \{0\}}, \texttt{𝒜\_0\ =\ \{5,\ 6\}},
\texttt{𝒜\_1\ =\ \{9,\ 10\}}, \texttt{𝒜\_2\ =\ \{15\}} gives
\texttt{q\^{}\{(0)\}\ +\ q\^{}\{(1)\}\ +\ q\^{}\{(2)\}\ +\ q\^{}\{(sink)\}\ =\ 1}
on \texttt{T} to \texttt{1e-15} with sink share in
\texttt{{[}0.362,\ 1.000{]}} on this draw; BOS undeclared gives
\texttt{ρ(Q)\ =\ 1.000000}, \texttt{det(I\ −\ Q)\ =\ 0} (F1 reproduced).
The sink share is a census line (§A.7) and a printed column, never
hidden. The record's value-zero BOS column sink (\texttt{V15Fork.Asink},
\texttt{READ\ lean/CEQ/V15Fork.lean:67-70}) and this row condition are
two distinct facts that coexist on every causal softmax
(\texttt{sweep\_resolvent.md} §2.14; P-7).

The read the paper prints is
\texttt{O\ =\ (1\ −\ γ)\ P\ (I\ −\ γP)\^{}\{-1\}\ V} with the factor
(ChaCAL's Eq. 5, \citep{fagnou-2024-chacal} \texttt{{[}V{]}}); the bare

rows (\texttt{RUN{[}MARS{]}} \texttt{2.5} both; V-23), so any table
printing the bare form declares its row sums. Under the factor the
Neumann certificate is \texttt{δ\_Π\ =\ γ\^{}\{K+1\}}
(\texttt{refute\_falsify\_math} §3.1).

undeclared absorbing state (\texttt{P\_cc\ =\ 1}, \texttt{ρ(Q)\ =\ 1},
\texttt{RUN{[}MARS{]}}) and severs every boundary set before \texttt{c}
from every \texttt{i\ ≥\ c} (\texttt{q\ =\ 0.0}); BED-S therefore either
never uses a zero gate on the committor channel or declares per-segment
sets with the segment head in \texttt{𝒜\_sink}
(\texttt{refute\_theory\_math} item 11) & a cut that is not censused per
segment & D-3, V-25, V-8 \\
\texttt{γ} (arm-side) & \texttt{{[}0,\ 1)}, learnable, init \texttt{0} &
the horizon dial on the \texttt{z}/\texttt{Δz} channel; PINNED/MOVED by
the LR test (§A.11) & a signed \texttt{γ} (forfeits the Neumann
equality) & V-17, M-20 \\
\texttt{s} & \texttt{\{64,\ 256,\ 1024,\ 4096\}} & the sequence length
of the cost law; \texttt{s\ =\ 64} on \texttt{40\ of\ 40} banked cells
left the exponent unidentified
(\texttt{READ\ V20\_R15\_THEORY\_TABLE.md:95-99}) & quoting any price
before the sweep runs (C9) & D-3, P-8 \\
\texttt{β}, \texttt{qk}, \texttt{g} & corners \texttt{\{0,\ 1\}} &
Ruling 2a: \texttt{β} learnable per instance and logged & a sentence
transferred across corners without a bind at the corner it describes &
Ruling 2 \\
\end{longtable}
}

\subsection{A.3 Information floors
(L-FLOOR)}\label{a.3-information-floors-l-floor}

Every capability number ships beside its information floor and never
beside \texttt{floor₁} (\texttt{READ\ V20\_R15\_JOURNAL.md:51}, C15;
\texttt{13\ of\ 40} banked cells violate \texttt{floor₁},
\texttt{RUN{[}WATSON{]}}).

\subsection{A.4 The native skyline and the fellow approximators (R-SKY,
D-1)}\label{a.4-the-native-skyline-and-the-fellow-approximators-r-sky-d-1}

``Beats softmax'' and ``beats native'' are banned sentences on every bed
where a fellow approximator can solve it
(\texttt{READ\ CEQ\_V16\_CONTRACT.md:209}); the shape's separate claims
are exactness (a printed \texttt{δ}), one-read joint consistency
\textbf{as a cost statement} (one solve, depth \texttt{s};
\texttt{refute\_falsify\_occvac} §1.4), and the boundary-row mechanism,
stated as properties with intervals. \citep{wang-2024-incontext-td} and
\citep{xie-2026-softmax-rl} (\texttt{{[}V{]}}) show a softmax stack
computes the same resolvent by iteration.

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{A.7 Domain census (L-DOM) --- printed per
draw batch, before any arm is trained; \texttt{0\ \%} on any line blocks
the bed
(D-4)}{A.7 Domain census (L-DOM) --- printed per draw batch, before any arm is trained; 0 \% on any line blocks the bed (D-4)}}\label{a.7-domain-census-l-dom-printed-per-draw-batch-before-any-arm-is-trained-0-on-any-line-blocks-the-bed-d-4}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Every drawn \texttt{P\_env} row is stochastic including the absorbing
  rows, \texttt{\textbar{}rowsum\ −\ 1\textbar{}\ ≤\ 1e-12} (the
  hypothesis of every row-stochastic theorem; V-25).
\item
  \texttt{0\ ∈\ 𝒜\_sink} on \texttt{100\ \%} of draws (F1; \texttt{RUN}:
  BOS undeclared gives \texttt{ρ(Q)\ =\ 1.000000},
  \texttt{det(I\ −\ Q)\ =\ 0}).
\item
  \texttt{ρ(Q̂)\ =\ max\_\{i∈T\}\ P̂\_ii\ \textless{}\ 1} on
  \texttt{100\ \%} --- a diagonal read on the arm's triangular
  \texttt{Q̂} (\texttt{RUN} \texttt{0.692660} both ways), implied by line
  2 for finite logits and therefore a V-10 gate; printed, not counted as
  evidence.
\item
  Every constraint set, the goal set and the sink lie before the query
  position; the query is in \texttt{T} (F2; a set after the query reads
  \texttt{q\ =\ 0.0} exactly, \texttt{RUN{[}SATURN{]}} R10,
  \texttt{RUN{[}VENUS{]}} RUN-2; a query inside a boundary set reads its
  own indicator --- V-8).
\item
  Per-coordinate label sd of \texttt{q} \textbf{over the admitted query
  region} (the prefix before the first constraint reads
  \texttt{q\^{}\{(0)\}\ ≈\ 1} and would inflate the sd,
  \texttt{refute\_instrument\_occvac} §2.7), each
  \texttt{\textgreater{}\ 0}; the sink share \texttt{q\^{}\{(sink)\}}
  printed separately from \texttt{q\^{}\{(0)\}} (\texttt{RUN}: sink
  share \texttt{{[}0.362,\ 1.000{]}} on one draw --- the balance guard
  is at risk from the fix itself and must be watched).
\item
  Argmin class balance: \textbf{every class frequency of \texttt{a*}
  over \texttt{m} lies in \texttt{(0.05,\ 0.95)}} (the instrument's
  ``argmin-unique fraction outside \texttt{(0.05,\ 0.95)}'' was an
  inverted gate --- \texttt{refute\_instrument\_occvac} §2.4); ties
  within \texttt{1e-9} discarded and the discard count printed.
\item
  Reduction-disagreement fraction between \texttt{argmax\ q\^{}\{(0)\}},
  Chebyshev and lexicographic \texttt{\textgreater{}\ 0}, else only one
  rule is named (C4).
\item
  Corpus-alone linear probe from \texttt{x} to \texttt{q} at order 0
  reads \texttt{R²\ \textless{}\ 0.5} (leak kill (c)); the planted leak
  (a move token carrying its own \texttt{q}) must read
  \texttt{R²\ ≥\ 0.99} --- the detector fires both ways (V-24, V-7).
\item
  \texttt{I(s₀;\ a*)\ =\ 0} up to plug-in error; the untrained arm reads
  argmin accuracy within \texttt{1/m\ ±\ CP} and committor NRMSE
  \texttt{≥\ 1\ −\ GATE\_TOL} (\texttt{GATE\_TOL\ =\ 1e-3},
  \texttt{READ\ COSTS.md:137}).
\item
  \texttt{Var(Δz)\ \textgreater{}\ 0} per coordinate \textbf{over
  coordinates \texttt{≥\ i\_min}}; the zero-coordinate fraction printed
  (V-8).
\item
  Fraction of moves that change \texttt{P\_env} (needs a re-solve)
  versus only \texttt{V}; fraction with \texttt{uᵀ𝟙\ =\ 0} and a
  non-negative edited row (\texttt{100\ \%} required); the
  Sherman--Morrison denominator
  \texttt{1\ −\ γuᵀMe\_i\ =\ (1\ −\ γp\textquotesingle{}\_ii)/(1\ −\ γP\_ii)\ \textgreater{}\ 0}
  is a theorem for \texttt{γ\ \textless{}\ 1}
  (\texttt{refute\_instrument\_math} §2.7) and is printed as a check,
  not censused.
\item
  Measured support of \texttt{β̂} and of \texttt{rowsum(P̂)} on every
  \textbf{trained} cell: Ruling 2 makes \texttt{β} learnable, and at
  \texttt{β̂\ ≠\ 1} rows sum to \texttt{1.31\ …\ 10.29}
  (\texttt{READ\ V16\_ARM\_SMPRIME.md:28-32}), where no row-stochastic
  theorem and no Neumann certificate applies ---
  \texttt{‖P̂‖\_∞\ \textgreater{}\ 1\ ⇒\ no\ certificate\ is\ printed}
  (\texttt{refute\_theory\_occvac} §1.2).
\item
  Measured support of \texttt{γ̂} across seeds with the LR verdict
  (PINNED / MOVED / interval).
\item
  For the segmentation rows only: at least one draw with an exact zero
  gate (BED-M: \texttt{3\ of\ 3} values,
  \texttt{READ\ lean/CEQ/V16Domain.lean:304}); on BED-S at the softmax
  corner the count is \texttt{0\ of\ N} and the F0 theorems are
  \textbf{silent} (\texttt{refute\_instrument\_occvac} §2.6).
\item
  For the obstruction theorems: the vacuity inequalities printed beside
  every citation --- \texttt{h·m·p\ =\ 512\ ≥\ 64}
  (\citep{sanford-2024-inductionheads} Thm 1),
  \texttt{n\ log₂\ n\ =\ 384\ \textless{}\ 544}
  (\citep{peng-2024-transformer-limitations} Thm 1),
  \texttt{64\^{}\{1/16\}\ =\ 1.2968} (\citep{chen-2024-multilayer} Thm
  1.1) --- all vacuous at \texttt{s\ =\ 64,\ d\ =\ 16,\ p\ =\ 32};
  conditional bounds named conditional in the same sentence (V-25,
  P-10).
\end{enumerate}

passes \textbf{and} every plant fails at O(1) with the counts printed

\texttt{kind} and \texttt{manifest\_hash} (V-14;
\texttt{READ\ V20\_R15\_WING\_MANIFEST.md:59-63}), not as an identity
check on random logits.

\textbf{Predictions beside kills (D-7, L-SIGN).} A counter without its
prediction leaves no residual to sign; the arena carries one line per
row, filed before step P3 of the plan: \emph{prediction}
\texttt{\textless{}statistic\textgreater{}\ \textless{}direction\textgreater{}\ \textless{}threshold\textgreater{}},
\emph{counter} the kill as written, and the band between them named
SPLIT. The five bets: A (\texttt{Δz} field cosine above depth-1 softmax
by more than the cosine's own realised \texttt{MDE₈}; counter: within),
B (\texttt{CP\_upper(err\_shape)\ \textless{}\ floor\_exact(k\ =\ 1)};
counter \texttt{CP\_lower\ ≥}; SPLIT the straddle), C (\texttt{γ̂} MOVED
on \texttt{≥\ 6/8} seeds under the boundary null; counter PINNED on
\texttt{≥\ 6/8}; SPLIT \texttt{3/8–5/8}), \textbf{D} (the depth-5
skyline within \texttt{MDE₈} of the shape's argmin accuracy; counter:
short by more than \texttt{MDE₈}, sign logged --- the P2 of
\texttt{sec\_beds.md} §6.E that \texttt{design\_falsify.md} asserted in
its headline sentence and filed nowhere), E (harmonic-residual ratio
\texttt{≥\ 10} at marginal NRMSE within \texttt{MDE₈}; counter
\texttt{≤\ 2}; \texttt{(2,\ 10)} SPLIT). The (j) LM \texttt{γ} row
carries no calibration row until an LM cell runs (V-1, M-20).

(true for a softmax \texttt{P} because \texttt{k\_i,\ v\_i} enter rows
\texttt{≥\ i} only); (iii) \textbf{withdrawn} as an EMC statement &
REPAIR & \citep{dash-2005-emc} as a remark only & V-3, D-7 \\
C8 & regime N (\texttt{StrictlyLower}, nilpotent, any \texttt{γ}, no
absorbing rows possible) and regime S (diagonal kept,
\texttt{γ\ \textless{}\ 1}, Neumann) are a theorem boundary
(\texttt{Nilpotent.one\_not\_nilpotent}); the masked-diagonal route is
exact \textbf{only} on value channels with \texttt{V\_0\ =\ 0}
(\texttt{RUN{[}MARS{]}} \texttt{4.4e-16} vs \texttt{1.99e-07} on
\texttt{𝟙\_\{𝒜\_0\}} with \texttt{0\ ∈\ 𝒜\_0}) --- which is why BOS is a
value-zero sink set; the C8 evidence \texttt{7.96e-08\ =\ γ\^{}\{32\}}
was attained at \texttt{(0,0)} and reads the same with the diagonal
masked (V-4), so the discriminating number is
\texttt{max\_\{i≥1\}(γP)\^{}\{32\}\_\{ii\}\ =\ 6.27e-13} & REPAIR &
\texttt{V15Fork.Asink\_row\_sum} for the sink row & V-25, V-4, P-3 \\
\end{longtable}
}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{A.10 The identity manifest --- field list
(against L-2,
\texttt{READ\ V20\_R15\_LEAP\_LEDGER.md:23})}{A.10 The identity manifest --- field list (against L-2, READ V20\_R15\_LEAP\_LEDGER.md:23)}}\label{a.10-the-identity-manifest-field-list-against-l-2-read-v20_r15_leap_ledger.md23}

The record's \texttt{CONFIG\_FIELDS}
(\texttt{READ\ scale/identity\_manifest.py:67-71}) carry \texttt{beta}
but not \texttt{qk}, \texttt{g}, \texttt{gamma}, and the manifest
reports a missing declared field as \texttt{absent} outside the hash
(\texttt{:141-151}). In the shape lane a missing declared field is a
\textbf{refusal}. The manifest ships its own planted negative: flipping
any one of \texttt{beta\ /\ qk\ /\ g\ /\ gamma\ /\ boundary\_sets} by
one unit must move \texttt{manifest\_hash} (the one-line-drift test of
\texttt{READ\ V20\_R15\_WING\_MANIFEST.md:148-152} is the pattern). A
cell is FOUND iff \texttt{results/} holds a record with the arm's
\texttt{kind}.

  BED-S's paired sd is \texttt{NOT\ MEASURED}; the first eight seeds fix

  \textbf{Parity.} At \texttt{N\ =\ 8} parity is claimed by identity
  bind only, never by TOST (M-13). The equivalence half (shape at

  the only contrast TOST decides (the E1 kill fires or a difference is
  detected; no branch consumes TOST there --- M-7). The
  \texttt{N\ =\ 70} figure is the \textbf{two-sample} requirement
  (\texttt{0.7975} at \texttt{69}, \texttt{0.8073} at \texttt{70},

  \texttt{RUN{[}MARS{]}}); the arena is \textbf{paired}, where the

The critical path is P1 → P2 → P3 → P4; P0 runs beside it; the whole
falsification programme at the design point is under
\texttt{4.0\ GPU-min} (DERIVED, §A.11 item 5). The record's dominant
failure shape --- a bed that dies at construction (D-4, M-3, V-8) --- is
now a \texttt{0\ GPU-s} decision the author takes from the P1 census;
the expensive death (ChaCAL-with-sink matching the committor read) is a
\texttt{≈\ 34\ s} reading escalated to \texttt{N\ =\ 16}, and what
survives it is a property paper about a published operator with five
machine-checked identities, a certificate discipline and a registered
bed, which the paper is written to remain honest in.

\subsection{A.13 Limits (collected
once)}\label{a.13-limits-collected-once}

Every \texttt{RUN} in this file is one numpy float64 draw at
\texttt{s\ =\ 32}, seed 0, Gaussian logits, on this CPU --- an identity
or counterexample check, never a statistic; the two one-liners were: (i)
\texttt{L\ =\ rng.standard\_normal((32,32));\ L{[}triu(1){]}\ =\ −inf;\ P\ =\ softmax(L)},
rows \texttt{\{0\}} sink, \texttt{\{5,6\}} goal, \texttt{\{9,10\}},
\texttt{\{15\}} constraints set to \texttt{e\_i};
\texttt{Q\ =\ P{[}T,T{]}}; \texttt{N\ =\ (I\ −\ Q)\^{}\{-1\}};
\texttt{q\ =\ N\ P{[}T,\ S{]}\ 𝟙} per set;
\texttt{z\ =\ (I\ −\ 0.6P)\^{}\{-1\}\ 𝟙\_S}; z-form \texttt{(1\ −\ γ)z},
read-form \texttt{(1\ −\ γ)Pz}; (ii) the \texttt{s\ =\ 3} mask instance
\texttt{P\ =\ {[}{[}1,0,0{]},{[}ε,1−ε,0{]},{[}0,1,0{]}{]}},
\texttt{γ\ =\ 0.9}, \texttt{ε\ =\ 0.1}, \texttt{V\ =\ e\_0}, with
\texttt{P\_10} dropped and row 1 renormalised; plus the Fano arithmetic.
BED-S has no cell, no realised sd, no measured \texttt{t*}, no
\texttt{I(X\_\{≤k\};\ a*)}; every BED-S number is a floor formula or a
design constant. The DAG-in-token-order substrate is a specification;
its adjacency-feature representability at \texttt{d\_model\ ≥\ n\_nodes}
is unmeasured. The Kirchhoff second oracle covers \texttt{K\ =\ 2}
single-node boundaries only. The committor head carries no truncation
certificate until a Perron weight exists; the F1 mask certificate is
dormant until a mask ships. The paired-TOST \texttt{N\ =\ 36} is a
Monte-Carlo figure at \texttt{sd\_d\ =\ σ}; BED-S's \texttt{sd\_d}
chooses. The boundary-null critical value \texttt{2.706} is stated
without a \texttt{references.bib} citation and is marked owed. Theorem
numbers inside cited sources are the sweeps' HTML readings and inherit
their \texttt{{[}U{]}} marks; ChaCAL's diagonal-removed inverse is
\texttt{{[}V-fetched{]}} by one refuter from the HTML render and should
be re-read against the PDF before it is typeset. Three Kemeny--Snell
keys coexist in \texttt{references.bib}
(\citep{kemeny-1960-finitemarkov}, \citep{kemeny-1976-finite},
\citep{kemeny-1976-finitemarkov}) and the assembler collapses them to
one. No code file, no git write, no Kaggle contact, no external fetch
was made by this judge.

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\section{7. Prior art and occupancy}\label{prior-art-and-occupancy}

JUPITER (MYCROFT), 2026-09-03, HEAD \texttt{207e7b9}. Inputs read in
full: the brief; both corrections; the outline;
\texttt{sec\_intro\_draft.md}; the eight sweeps
\texttt{sweep\_\{resolvent,linrec,expressivity,causality,\ safety,topology,occupied,methods\}.md};
\texttt{judge/sec\_shape.md} §4.0, whose occupancy paragraph this
section must agree with and go wider than; \texttt{bib\_audit.md};
\texttt{bib\_aliases.md}; \texttt{references.bib} (canonical keys only
--- an alias key is never printed). Evidence classes: \texttt{RUN}
(executed on this box this session), \texttt{READ\ path:line},
\texttt{CITED\ {[}V{]}} (abs page, DOI/registry record, catalogue record
or proceedings page fetched by the named sweep this session and the
title matched), \texttt{CITED\ {[}U{]}} (reached through a search index,
a secondary paper, or memory), \texttt{DERIVED} (steps written out). No
number without provenance. Struck constants (\texttt{STRUCK.md}, 12
entries) do not appear.

The rule this section exists to serve is the brief's rule 7:
\emph{occupied components are cited before they are named, and absence
is recorded as NOT FOUND with the query that bounds it, never as
``novel''}. It is the same rule the record breaks in §7.3.

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\subsection{7.0 How to read the table}\label{how-to-read-the-table}

\textbf{Verdicts.} \texttt{OCCUPIED} --- one named source states, in
print, the equation or mechanism with the same object on both sides.
\texttt{NEAR-MISS} --- a named source states a neighbouring object and
the table says which clause is missing. \texttt{NOT\ FOUND} --- no
fetched source states it, and the row carries the queries that failed;
the totals in §7.2 bound how much that absence is worth
(\texttt{MISTAKES.md} V-7, a search structurally incapable of finding
anything read as absence, \texttt{MISTAKES.md:117}).

\textbf{Marks.} \texttt{{[}V{]}} and \texttt{{[}U{]}} are per source, as
the sweep that found it recorded them. \texttt{{[}V{]}} here is a title
match against a fetched identifier page; it is \textbf{not} an
equation-level check. Where a sweep read the body and transcribed the
equation the row says so; those are the only rows on which an equation
number is load-bearing.

\textbf{Boxes.} Six of the shape's structural properties were fixed
\emph{before} the first fetch (\texttt{sweep\_resolvent.md} §0, against
M-2, a threshold refitted to the data it judges): (a) \texttt{P} is
content-dependent, (b) causally masked, (c) row-stochastic softmax, (d)
the read uses the exact resolvent, (e) absorbing rows on named sets, (f)
softmax parity at \(\gamma=0\); and four more --- (g) the interventional
re-solve, (h) the safest-move decision, (i) a printed truncation
certificate, (j) learnable \(\gamma\). The delta letters in §7.2 are
those boxes plus (k) machine-checked containment and the zero-gate iff,
(l) the influence barcode restricted to the F0 endpoint, and (m) the
cover-to-schedule path.

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\subsubsection{7.1.1 The cite-first
sentences}\label{the-cite-first-sentences}

Rule 7 requires that an occupied component be citable in one sentence
\emph{before} it is named. These are those sentences; the assembler
places each at the first occurrence of its component.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Read operator.} ``The read is that of ChaCAL
  (\citep{fagnou-2024-chacal}, EMNLP 2024, Eq. 5), with the diagonal
  kept inside the inverse so that the mixing matrix stays
  row-stochastic.''
\item
  \textbf{Triangular solve.} ``ChaCAL solves it as a lower-triangular
  system (Eq. 7); the per-chunk form of the same primitive is DeltaNet's
  (\citep{yang-2024-deltanet}, Eq. 10).''
\item
  \textbf{Discount.} ``The discount is Bellman's
  (\citep{bellman-1957-markovian}), the operator Dayan's successor
  representation (\citep{dayan-1993-successor}); learning one per head
  is InfSA's (\citep{roffo-2026-infsa}).''
\item
  \textbf{Base family.} ``The three corners are Vaswani et al.'s
  softmax, Katharopoulos et al.'s linear attention, and the
  1-semiseparable mask of \citep{dao-2024-ssd} Def. 3.1.''
\item
  \textbf{Absorbing rows.} ``Clamping labelled positions and reading the
  harmonic extension is \citep{zhu-2003-harmonic}; one absorbing row
  inside attention is \citep{karbalayghareh-2026-doformer}.''
\item
  \textbf{Fundamental matrix.} ``\(N=(I-Q)^{-1}\) and \(B=NR\) are
  Kemeny--Snell (\citep{kemeny-1960-finitemarkov});
  \citep{grinstead-1997-probability} Thm 11.6 states the same, and is
  \texttt{{[}U{]}}.''
\item
  \textbf{Committor.} ``The committor is the discrete Dirichlet problem
  of \citep{metzner-2009-tpt-markov-jump}; its control-theoretic name is
  the reach-avoid probability (\citep{summers-2010-reach-avoid}).''
\item
  \textbf{Decision rule.} ``Selecting by the worst of several scores is
  Chebyshev scalarisation (\citep{vanmoffaert-2013-chebyshev}); as a
  safety filter it is \citep{hsu-2023-safetyfilter}.''
\item
  \textbf{Re-solve.} ``The rank-one re-solve is Sherman--Morrison
  (\citep{sherman-1950-inverse-adjustment}); on a fixed chain with a
  barrier it is \citep{piray-2021-linearrl} Eq. 5.''
\item
  \textbf{Equilibrium layer.} ``A layer defined as a fixed point is
  \citep{bai-2019-deq}; on a causally masked transformer with \(do()\)
  by clamp-and-re-solve it is \citep{scetbon-2024-fip}.''
\item
  \textbf{Barcode.} ``Persistence of a thresholded attention graph is
  \citep{kushnareva-2021-tda-attention}; an intervention read through
  persistence summaries is \citep{kim-2026-topological-causal}.''
\item
  \textbf{Cover and schedule.} ``Mapper is \citep{singh-2007-mapper},
  its estimator theory \citep{carriere-2018-mapper-statistics}; a
  persistence-derived CSR schedule inside an attention kernel is the
  author's own merged \citep{sharma-2026-kernels-22}.''
\item
  \textbf{Depth law.} ``\(k\) hops need depth
  \(\lfloor\log_2 k\rfloor+2\) (\citep{sanford-2024-logdepth} Thm 4.2;
  the matching lower bound is conditional, Cor. 4.3); the same law is
  reached independently for entity tracking by
  \citep{fagnou-2024-chacal} Thm 1.''
\item
  \textbf{Optimality.} ``One attention layer attains Bayes risk on
  single-location regression (\citep{marion-2025-single-location}; for
  softmax proper, \citep{duranthon-2026-softmax-advantage}).''
\item
  \textbf{Certificate.} ``The Neumann tail bound is textbook
  (\citep{meyer-2000-matrix}); no attention paper fetched prints one.''
\end{enumerate}

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\subsection{7.2 The delta, narrowly}\label{the-delta-narrowly}

No fetched source combines the following in one attention operator, and
every part of every clause is owned above. \textbf{(e)} \(K\ge2\)
absorbing constraint sets, a goal set, and a declared sink as identity
rows of a content-dependent, causal, row-stochastic read --- bounded by
\texttt{sweep\_resolvent} searches 17 and 22, \texttt{sweep\_linrec}
Q4/Q11/Q17, \texttt{sweep\_occupied} S4/S5 (S5 returned \textbf{0} hits
containing ``committor'' in a transformer context),
\texttt{sweep\_methods} S11 and \texttt{sweep\_topology} 5/13; the
nearest things found are InfSA's single uniform absorption leak,
DoFormer's one clamped row, and the attention \emph{sink}, which is a
column device and not a row condition. \textbf{(g)} the interventional
re-solve with the displacement as a jointly scored vector label at two
stated prices --- bounded by \texttt{sweep\_resolvent} 19,
\texttt{sweep\_linrec} Q15, \texttt{sweep\_causality} Q1/Q3/Q6/Q9/Q16
and \texttt{sweep\_methods} S10; Q9 returned nothing at all on
interventions in deep-equilibrium layers. \textbf{(h)} the committor
vector and the two safest-move rules over candidate moves placed in the
context --- bounded by \texttt{sweep\_safety} 2/5/10/12/13/17/18/19
(query 14 established that the word ``committor'' does not occur in the
control literature), \texttt{sweep\_occupied} S8,
\texttt{sweep\_expressivity} S15 and \texttt{sweep\_causality} Q11/Q15.
\textbf{(i)} a printed certificate with a planted negative and the
\(1/(1-\gamma)\) mask amplification --- bounded by
\texttt{sweep\_resolvent} 3, \texttt{sweep\_linrec} Q6/Q14 and
\texttt{sweep\_methods} S4; the inequality is textbook and is claimed
only as an instrument, never as mathematics. \textbf{(j)} a learnable
discount in a \emph{causal language-model} read pinned by a
boundary-corrected likelihood-ratio test --- bounded by
\texttt{sweep\_resolvent} 2 and 3 and \texttt{sweep\_linrec} Q1/Q16 (Q16
and \texttt{sweep\_occupied} S12 each returned \textbf{0} hits for the
successor representation used as an attention operator); ChaCAL fixes
\(\gamma=0.9\), InfSA learns it and is non-causal vision. \textbf{(k)}
machine-checked containment of the three corners and the zero-gate iff
--- bounded by \texttt{sweep\_topology} 7 and 14 and
\texttt{sweep\_expressivity} S16; the nearest statements are FoX's
cumulative-log gate, which the record's own theorem excludes at an exact
zero, and the block-mask converse of \citep{yang-2026-boundary-repair}
Thm 1. \textbf{(l)} the influence-Jacobian barcode restricted to the F0
endpoint, with a directed stability \(\delta\) --- bounded by
\texttt{sweep\_topology} 4/8/16. \textbf{(m)} the cover-to-schedule
path, Mapper nerve to CSR blocks to a certified resolvent read ---
bounded by \texttt{sweep\_topology} 6/15/2. \textbf{The totals.} Across
the eight sweeps, \textbf{143} numbered queries were logged (resolvent
22, linrec 18, expressivity 18, causality 18, safety 25, topology 17,
occupied 12, methods 13), plus five bibliographic-repair searches in the
methods sweep; \texttt{references.bib} carries \textbf{408 canonical
entries} (RUN, \texttt{grep\ -c\ "\^{}@"\ references.bib} = 408, this
session), of which the audit resolved \textbf{278/278} arXiv identifiers
and \textbf{91/92} DOIs against the registries, with 38 entries carrying
neither identifier; the five sweeps that print an entry-level
\texttt{{[}U{]}} count report \textbf{13} in total (methods 9, occupied
2, topology 1, causality 1, expressivity 0), and the other three mark
\texttt{{[}U{]}} per field rather than per entry, so no merged
\texttt{{[}U{]}} count exists. Absence here is exactly as strong as
those 143 queries and no stronger, and two of the sweeps paid for their
absences with planted positives --- \texttt{sweep\_topology} §1.1 shows
the same instrument returning the seed papers it was required to find,
and \texttt{sweep\_occupied} §4 plants the record's own dead signed
programme as a lineage the table must return red.

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\subsection{7.3 The record's own prior-art
defect}\label{the-records-own-prior-art-defect}

The paper that owns CEQ's read operator has been in print since October
2024 and the campaign never cited it. The mechanism is a filter, and the
filter is written down. At round 1 the record's prior-art trawl
concluded: \emph{``Every multi-hop propagation found (APPNP 1810.05997,
GDC 1911.05485, MAGNA 2009.14332) requires a non-negative matrix; every
signed attention found \ldots{} is single-hop''}
(\texttt{READ\ DONE\_ARCHIVE\_ROUND1.md:676-680}). The clause ``requires
a non-negative matrix'' was a \emph{disqualifier}: the programme then
alive was the signed strictly-causal path sum, so a multi-hop operator
built on a non-negative base was recorded as inapplicable and dropped.
Every candidate in the class ChaCAL belongs to was therefore discarded
by construction, and the same page later concedes that the survey
sentence built on that filter was false
(\texttt{READ\ DONE\_ARCHIVE\_ROUND1.md:1046-1050}). When the signed
programme died (\texttt{RESEARCH.md}, \texttt{PROGNOSIS.md}, per
\texttt{BRIEF.md} §3), the filter was never re-run without its sign
criterion. The shape is now itself a non-negative multi-hop operator,
which is to say the record's search was, for the whole life of the
campaign, structurally incapable of finding the paper that occupies its
read.

The measurement of the gap is a grep.
\texttt{grep\ -rlEi\ "ChaCAL\textbar{}Fagnou\textbar{}2410\textbackslash{}.05565"\ -\/-include=\textquotesingle{}*.md\textquotesingle{}}
over the repository returns \textbf{0 files} (RUN, 2026-09-03, this box;
confirmed twice); a full-tree
\texttt{grep\ -rlEi\ "2410\textbackslash{}.05565"} returns exactly
\textbf{one} file, \texttt{docs/references.bib} --- the bibliography
this paper assembles. \texttt{2603.00175} (InfSA, which put
``fundamental matrix of an absorbing Markov chain'' into an attention
abstract in February 2026) appears in \textbf{2} \texttt{.md} files,
both inside the dead signed programme's archive, where it was read as a
signed near-miss and its absorbing-chain paragraph was not read
(\texttt{READ\ DONE\_ARCHIVE\_ROUND1.md:1317-1318}).

This is \texttt{MISTAKES.md} \textbf{V-7} --- \emph{a search
structurally incapable of finding anything, read as absence}
(\texttt{MISTAKES.md:117}) --- applied to a \textbf{literature} search
rather than to a code search, which is the form the taxonomy had never
recorded. V-7's existing instances condemn \texttt{grep} patterns and
test selectors that could not match; this instance condemns an inclusion
criterion in a prior-art census. It compounds \textbf{P-3} (a stale
claim never retracted, \texttt{MISTAKES.md:316}): \texttt{THEORY.md:22}
still calls the successor operator new, and \texttt{THEORY.md:225-235}
still marks Dayan 1993, Ramsauer 2020 and Bai et al.~2019 unverified,
all three of which the sweeps have now fetched. \textbf{The rule the
plan installs against it:} every prior-art census keeps \emph{one
mechanism-keyed query per component beside every name-keyed query}, and
no census criterion may name the programme's current hypothesis. ChaCAL
was found by \texttt{sweep\_expressivity} S6 and
\texttt{sweep\_resolvent} search 2 --- queries built from
\emph{triangular solve} and \emph{resolvent}, the shape's mechanism ---
and not by any query naming an operator family; the name-keyed query
\texttt{sweep\_resolvent} 18 (``chain and causal attention \ldots{}
citing papers'') returned only ChaCAL's own pages after it had already
been found. The census's planted positive is its own refuted lineage: a
table that cannot return a red on the signed programme is not
calibrated.

One interval in the sweeps is not supported by anything in the tree.
\texttt{sweep\_occupied.md} §0.1 says ChaCAL was in print ``eleven
months before the brief''; the identifier is arXiv 2410.05565, submitted
October 2024, twenty-three months before 2026-09-03, and the tree's own
history runs only 2026-08-25 to 2026-09-01 (RUN,
\texttt{git\ log\ -\/-format=\%ad\ -\/-date=short}), so no in-tree date
supports either figure. The paper prints the identifier's date and no
interval.

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\section{8. Limits}\label{limits}

Collected once, here, and nowhere else.

\textbf{There is no result.} No CEQ arm has been trained and no
capability number exists; the paper reports a plan and the identities
that justify it. Proposition 8 --- the safest move --- has no cell, no
realised label standard deviation and no measured hop depth, and the
barcode and Mapper rows have no instrument at all
(\texttt{beta0\_interleaving} consumes point clouds,
\texttt{READ\ ceq/certs/topological.py:476-505}). The record's last
deciding measurement crossed a one-hop threshold on five seeds of eight,
straddled it on the interval, and that threshold was afterwards shown
not to be an information floor (13 of 40 cells violate it,
\texttt{V20\_R15\_THEORY\_TABLE.md} §0.2). Every number here is an
identity check, a price, or a citation.

\textbf{Theorems cited beyond their hypotheses license nothing, so the
hypotheses are printed.} The one-layer composition bound of
\citep{peng-2024-transformer-limitations} Thm 1 requires
\(n\log n > H(d+1)p\); at \(s=64\), \(d=16\), one head, float32 this
reads \(384 < 544\) (DERIVED, \(\log_2\)), so the inequality is
\textbf{vacuous} at the record's geometry and the theorem is cited for
its asymptotic shape with those numbers beside it. The same discipline
applies to \citep{sanford-2024-logdepth} Cor. 4.3 and
\citep{sanford-2024-graph-algorithms} Thm 3/19, both conditional on the
one-vs-two-cycle conjecture, and to \citep{chen-2024-multilayer} Thm
1.1, whose parameter budget \(n^{2^{-4L}}\) reads \(1.30\) at \(L=1\),
\(n=64\). The Cheeger restatement is owed: the record writes
\(g\le 2\varphi\) at \texttt{MATHEMATICS.md:455}, while Levin--Peres Thm
13.10 (\citep{levin-2017-markov-mixing}, read at printed p.~183 by the
methods sweep) states \(\Phi_\star^2/2\le\gamma\le 2\Phi_\star\)
\textbf{for a reversible chain}; the shape's \(P\) is causal and not
reversible, so the sentence must be restated on a named symmetrised or
lazy surrogate, or replaced by a non-reversible bottleneck bound, and
until it is, no conductance sentence is written about \(P\). A second
hazard is carried, not resolved:
\citep{misra-2023-safety-constrained-mdp} {[}V{]} warns that Bellman
optimality can fail in multichain constrained MDPs with several unsafe
sets --- the class the safest-move rule inhabits --- so the rule is
stated as a one-step filter over an evaluated committor vector and no
card claims policy-level optimality.

\textbf{Determinism and cost carry their own caveats.}
\texttt{solve\_triangular} was observed bitwise identical forward and
backward over eight repeats under
\texttt{use\_deterministic\_algorithms(True)}, but torch documents no
such guarantee for it, so that is an observation on one box and one
version, not a contract; \texttt{cumsum} raises under the same setting,
which is why the segmentation route is a mask and not a scan. The
timings inherit an unsynchronised timer and a run-order confound --- the
arms were not interleaved --- so every price is a \textbf{per-op floor}
under a measured dispatch gap of \(2.0\times\) to \(6.6\times\)
(\texttt{READ\ scale/m3\_flops.py:101-121}). The measured increment of
the solve over the softmax head it contains is \(2.514-1.473 = 1.041\)
ms/step at \(n=2048\) (\(2.312\) at \(4096\), \(4.542\) at \(8192\)),
float32, one box, producer owed to \texttt{scripts/k\_cert.py}; the
chunked and CSR paths are
\texttt{NOT\ MEASURED\ —\ needs\ a\ chunked\ kernel}, and the dense
control is not runnable at \(n=2048\), \(s=4096\) (\(\approx137\) GB
against \(7.996\) GiB), so \(n\) is stated per \(s\) everywhere.

\textbf{The bibliography is verified at the identifier, not at the
equation.} The audit resolved 278 of 278 arXiv identifiers and 91 of 92
DOIs with no identifier paired to a wrong title; the unresolved one is
\citep{singh-2007-mapper}, \texttt{10.2312/SPBG/SPBG07/091-100}, absent
from Crossref (Eurographics digital library), carried \texttt{{[}U{]}}
with two independent records agreeing on title, authors and year.
Thirty-eight entries carry neither identifier --- books, nineteenth- and
twentieth-century papers, unregistered proceedings --- and rest on
catalogue, ISBN, zbMATH or DBLP marks; nine of those were never fetched
at all (Cantelli 1928, Bonferroni 1936, Frobenius 1912, Neumann 1877,
Kantorovich--Rubinstein 1958, Grünwald 1867, Letnikov 1868, Kramers
1927, Littlewood--Offord 1943) and carry no load-bearing sentence, a
textbook statement standing in where one is needed. \textbf{The sweeps'
\texttt{{[}V{]}} marks are abs-page title matches, not equation-level
\texttt{{[}V-eq{]}} marks.} Only a handful of sources were read at the
body --- ChaCAL, InfSA, DeltaNet, SSD, Hu et al., FoX, Dash 2005, Bottou
§7.3, Grinstead--Snell §11.2, Levin--Peres Thm 13.10 --- and every other
equation number, theorem number and hypothesis here was read through a
rendered page or a summarising fetch and \textbf{must be re-checked
against the compiled PDF before typesetting}, ChaCAL's diagonal
convention included, since it rests on one HTML fetch and decides two of
the paper's controls. That is not a formality; it is the record's own
hazard. Extracting the LRU paper's §3.3 with \texttt{pypdf} returned
text whose Type-1 encoding mapped \(-\) to \texttt{\textbackslash{}x00}
and \(\infty\) to the glyph \texttt{1}, turning an open interval into a
closed one --- \emph{``the single sentence that decides that node is the
one the extractor corrupts, and it corrupts it into the claim under
test''} (\texttt{READ\ workdonenewseal.md:468-473};
\texttt{V15\_X36\_PRIOR\_ART.md:45-56}). The remedy there was to read
every ML source from arXiv LaTeX source, and it is the remedy owed here.
One internal inconsistency, recorded: \texttt{sweep\_expressivity}'s
header says sixteen searches where its own table and count section say
eighteen; eighteen is used.

\textbf{The provenance of this document.} It was produced by two
multi-agent passes, both cut by a session cap, and the reader is owed
what was lost. In the \textbf{first} pass two of three design documents
reached the first synthesis and no refutations reached it at all:
\texttt{design\_instrument.md} (525 lines) and
\texttt{design\_falsify.md} (fourteen bets with counters and killers)
survive as inputs, \texttt{design\_theory.md} was never written
(\texttt{THESIS\_CORRECTIONS\_2.md} §2, \texttt{READ};
\texttt{PAPER\_OUTLINE.md} §8), and the theory design was produced fresh
in the second pass. In the \textbf{second} pass the plan synthesis was
the casualty and was re-run separately. So this section was judged
against six refutations, all present, but the plan it points at was
assembled on a second attempt rather than in one pass, and no single
agent saw every input at once. The judge's \texttt{RUN{[}J{]}} numbers
are each one numpy float64 draw at \(s=32\), \(\gamma=0.6\), seed 0, CPU
--- identity and counterexample checks, not statistics --- and other
planets' runs are quoted at their own differing geometries. No planet
wrote a code file or made a git write, so nothing here is reproduced by
running this document; the reproduction path is the plan's first-evening
list, and it starts at zero GPU-seconds.

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\section{Appendix A. The two ledgers}\label{appendix-a.-the-two-ledgers}

The proposition ledger and the bind ledger are the paper's audit trail.
Every proposition of §2 appears in the first with its status, its
evidence class and number, the failure mechanism it is designed against,
the verdict each of the six refuters returned on it, and the ruling that
settled it; anything left \texttt{OPEN} names the §5 card that
discharges it. Every bind, control, census, certificate and kill of §6
appears in the second with the same fields and the card that builds it.
Neither is a summary of the sections: they are the record of what
survived adversarial review and what was deleted, and they are the
fastest way to check that a sentence in the body is carried by
something.

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\textbf{Tally.} Propositions and definitions judged: 40 rows. Kept
unchanged: 6 (P2, P5(b), P7(i) algebra, O1, O3, C-cost arithmetic).
Repaired in the refuters' wording: 27. Killed as written and answered
with a number (a new proposition or a split definition replaces them): 5
--- D6 support, P4(ii) naive union bound, P5(c) support claim, P6(ii)
composed segmentation, P7(v) value-only moves. Killed and deleted: 2 ---
the EMC proposition (retired to a remark) and the \(\beta_0\) barcode
row on BED-S. Refuter overruled with a RUN: 1 ---
\texttt{refute\_theory\_math} item 8's universal \(\ge1-\gamma\) gap
(RUN{[}J{]} \(1.42\times10^{-7}\); the bound is \((1-\gamma)q_i\)).

\textbf{Open items and the plan nodes that discharge them.} S-1..S-4
(BED-S specification, census, VOID list, first pair), J-1 (LR instrument
with the boundary null), K-1 (skyline bet), C-1 (the one code edit
owed), T-1/T-2 (topological instruments), L-1 (the Lean evening).
Independent nodes: L-1, S-1, J-1, T-1, T-2 may be picked up in any
order; S-2 needs S-1; S-3 needs S-1; S-4 needs S-2 and S-3 and L-1
(L-LEAN); K-1 needs S-4's realised sd; C-1 needs nothing.

\textbf{Citations owed (not in \texttt{references.bib}, therefore not
cited).} The boundary likelihood-ratio null; the reach-avoid LP papers
the safety sweep did not fetch; the 2026 anytime-valid
equivalence-testing preprints. The ChaCAL diagonal convention is
\texttt{{[}V-fetched{]}} by one HTML read and is re-read against the PDF
before it is typeset.

designed), \textbf{REPAIR} (adopted with the refuter's correction
applied; the correction is in the ``what changed'' column),

applied), \textbf{KILL→deleted} (reversal needs an instrument or bed
that does not exist; the row is out of the apparatus and appears only

five the author can settle alone in one sitting

\section{Appendix B. Vocabulary --- the field's name for each
construction}\label{appendix-b.-vocabulary-the-fields-name-for-each-construction}

Every object in this paper was built or reached inside the repository
before it was named. This appendix gives the standard term for each, the
field the term comes from, and what the term commits its user to, so
that a sentence about this work can be read by someone who has never
opened the repository. It is a naming table, not a claim table: a term
appearing here says the construction belongs to a known family, never
that the family's results transfer to it. Where two fields use one word
for different objects, both are given, because that ambiguity has cost
this campaign real numbers.

\subsection{B.1 The operator and its
read}\label{b.1-the-operator-and-its-read}

{\def\LTcaptype{none} % do not increment counter
\begin{longtable}[]{@{}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\linewidth - 6\tabcolsep) * \real{0.2500}}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\linewidth - 6\tabcolsep) * \real{0.2500}}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\linewidth - 6\tabcolsep) * \real{0.2500}}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\linewidth - 6\tabcolsep) * \real{0.2500}}@{}}
\toprule\noalign{}
\begin{minipage}[b]{\linewidth}\raggedright
the construction
\end{minipage} & \begin{minipage}[b]{\linewidth}\raggedright
the field's term
\end{minipage} & \begin{minipage}[b]{\linewidth}\raggedright
field of origin
\end{minipage} & \begin{minipage}[b]{\linewidth}\raggedright
what the term commits you to
\end{minipage} \\
\midrule\noalign{}
\endhead
\bottomrule\noalign{}
\endlastfoot
\(z=(I-\gamma P)^{-1}V\) & \textbf{resolvent} of \(P\) at \(1/\gamma\);
equivalently the \textbf{Neumann series} \(\sum_t (\gamma P)^t\) &
operator theory, numerical linear algebra & convergence needs
\(\|\gamma P\|<1\) or nilpotency; the object is linear in \(V\) \\
the same, for row-stochastic \(P\) and a reward \(V\) & \textbf{policy
evaluation}; \((I-\gamma P)^{-1}\) is the \textbf{successor
representation} & reinforcement learning
(\citep{bellman-1957-markovian}, \citep{dayan-1993-successor}) &
\(\gamma\) is a discount; the entries are expected discounted visit
counts, so they are non-negative and sum to \(1/(1-\gamma)\) \\
the same on a graph adjacency & \textbf{personalised PageRank} /
\textbf{Katz index} & network science (\citep{katz-1953-status},
\citep{brin-1998-anatomy}, \citep{gasteiger-2019-appnp}) & teleport
probability \(1-\gamma\); the read is a centrality unless \(V\) carries
content \\
\(\Pi_\gamma=(1-\gamma)P(I-\gamma P)^{-1}\) & the \textbf{discounted
occupancy kernel}; a \textbf{Markov kernel} in its own right & Markov
chains & it is row-stochastic, so the read is a convex mixture and
cannot leave the value hull \\
solving \((I-\gamma P)z=V\) by substitution rather than inverting &
\textbf{forward substitution} on a \textbf{triangular system} &
numerical linear algebra & cost \(\Theta(s^2 d)\), sequential depth
\(s\); the inverse is never formed \\
computing it in blocks & the \textbf{chunked} or \textbf{UT-transform /
WY} form & linear-attention kernels (\citep{yang-2024-deltanet},
\citep{dao-2024-ssd}) & the intra-chunk term is a small dense triangular
inverse; inter-chunk is a matmul \\
\(\gamma\) swept from \(0\) to \(1\) & the \textbf{discount}, or the
\textbf{horizon}; \(\gamma\uparrow1\) is the \textbf{undiscounted limit}
& RL, Markov chains & at \(\gamma=1\) with an absorbing row the
resolvent of the full matrix does not exist; the limit is taken on the
transient block \\
\end{longtable}
}

read}\label{b.2-the-boundary-rows-and-what-they-read}

a row set to \(e_a\) & an \textbf{absorbing state}; the set is an
\textbf{absorbing class}; imposing it is a \textbf{Dirichlet boundary
condition} & Markov chains; potential theory & once entered, never left;
the chain is then a \textbf{killed} or \textbf{absorbed} walk \\
the block form \(\begin{pmatrix}Q&R\\0&I\end{pmatrix}\) &
\textbf{canonical form} of an absorbing chain; \(Q\) is the
\textbf{transient block} & Markov chains
(\citep{kemeny-1960-finitemarkov}) & \(\rho(Q)<1\) is \emph{absorption
almost surely}, and it is a hypothesis, not a given \\
\(N=(I-Q)^{-1}\) & the \textbf{fundamental matrix} & Markov chains &
\(N_{ij}\) is the expected number of visits to \(j\) from \(i\) before
absorption \\
\(B=NR\), its column \(k\) & the \textbf{absorption probability}; for
two sets, the \textbf{committor} or \textbf{splitting probability}; in
control, the \textbf{reach-avoid probability} & Markov chains;
transition-path theory (\citep{metzner-2009-tpt-markov-jump});
stochastic reachability (\citep{summers-2010-reach-avoid}) & the columns
sum to one across \emph{all} absorbing sets, which is why a goal set
must exist \\
the same quantity solved as \(Lq=0\) with \(q\) clamped on the boundary
& the \textbf{discrete Dirichlet problem}; \(q\) is a \textbf{harmonic
function}; the machine-learning instance is \textbf{label propagation} &
potential theory (\citep{doyle-1984-electric}); semi-supervised learning
(\citep{zhu-2003-harmonic}) & harmonicity is on the \emph{interior}
only; the boundary values are data \\
the set \(\{q=\tfrac12\}\) & the \textbf{isocommittor surface}; in
chemistry, the \textbf{transition state} & transition-path theory
(\citep{e-2010-tptreview}) & it is a level set of a function, not a
partition of the state space \\
a position that absorbs mass without being asked to & an
\textbf{attention sink} (a \emph{column} device) --- distinct from an
absorbing \emph{row} & transformer analysis
(\citep{xiao-2023-attentionsinks}) & a sink receives weight; an
absorbing row emits none. The two coexist and are not each other \\

decisions}\label{b.3-interventions-and-decisions}

rewriting a row of \(P\) and re-solving & an \textbf{intervention},
\(\mathrm{do}(a)\); on a linear system, \textbf{graph surgery} on a
\textbf{structural causal model} & causal inference
(\citep{pearl-2009-causality}, \citep{shimizu-2006-lingam}) & the model
must be the data-generating one for the word ``causal'' to be earned; on
a learned \(P\) it is an intervention on the \emph{model} \\
\(\Delta z\) after that rewrite & the \textbf{total effect}; for an
equilibrium, the \textbf{equilibrium displacement} & causal inference;
comparative statics (\citep{mooij-2013-ode2scm},
\citep{bongers-2021-cyclic}) & it is defined relative to a stated
pre-intervention state \\
updating the solve after a rank-one row change & the
\textbf{Sherman--Morrison} formula; for several rows, \textbf{Woodbury};
on a Markov chain, \textbf{fundamental-matrix perturbation} & numerical
linear algebra (\citep{sherman-1950-inverse-adjustment}); Markov chains
(\citep{schweitzer-1968-perturbation}) & the update is exact, not an
approximation; it costs one solve's worth of back-substitution \\
whether ``intervene then settle'' equals ``settle then intervene'' &
\textbf{equilibration--manipulation commutability} & causal modelling of
equilibria (\citep{dash-2005-emc}) & it can fail; it holds here by
triangularity, which is a property of the operator and not of the
world \\
\(\arg\max_a q^{(0)}(\mathrm{do}\,a)\) & a \textbf{safety filter} or
\textbf{least-restrictive filter}; the value is a \textbf{reach-avoid
value function} & safe control (\citep{hsu-2023-safetyfilter},
\citep{fisac-2019-bridging}) & it is a decision rule over
\emph{evaluated} candidates, not a learned policy \\
\(\arg\min_a\max_k q^{(k)}\) & \textbf{Chebyshev scalarisation} of a
multi-objective problem & multi-objective optimisation
(\citep{vanmoffaert-2013-chebyshev}) & the max-form and the weighted-sum
form give different optima; the choice is a modelling decision and must
be declared \\
ranking constraints instead of maxing them & \textbf{lexicographic}
ordering; bounding each is a \textbf{constrained MDP} & multi-objective
RL (\citep{yang-2026-lexisafe}); CMDPs (\citep{altman-1999-cmdp}) &
Bellman's principle can fail on multichain safety-constrained problems
(\citep{misra-2023-safety-constrained-mdp}) \\
\end{longtable}

\subsection{B.6 Two words this campaign used in two senses, and what
each
cost}\label{b.6-two-words-this-campaign-used-in-two-senses-and-what-each-cost}

\textbf{``Causal.''} In a transformer, \emph{causal} names the
triangular mask: position \(i\) reads only \(j\le i\). In causal
inference, \emph{causal} names invariance under intervention. The
repository's operator is causal in the first sense throughout, and the
second sense is earned only on a bed where the label is generated by an
intervention on a stated model. Every sentence in this paper that uses
the second sense names the bed. This is the term-slide the campaign paid
for most often.

\textbf{``State.''} In linear algebra a state is a vector; in
Markov-chain language a state is a \emph{position with a transition
law}; in state-space models the state is the recurrence's carried
summary, whose \emph{dimension} is a capacity bound. The repository's
``next state toward equilibrium'' is the Markov sense; its
\texttt{d\_model} is the linear-algebra sense; the delay theorems of
\texttt{V15Kernel} are about the third. A sentence that slides between
them proves nothing, and the record has one such sentence per round.

\textbf{``Depth.''} Depth in a transformer is the number of parameter
blocks; depth in circuit complexity is the length of the longest
computation path. The shape has one parameter block and a solve whose
sequential depth is the sequence length. Both are said, in the same
paragraph, every time either is claimed.
